# Data Plan

## Context provenance
- `Could you build a ai trucker gps with all the alerts,speed limits, semi trucks restriction roads, weight station and bridges height` (prior user request; defines the core feature set)
- `Build it — full truck GPS` (current request; user chose the full build over the lean option)
- Assistant proposal accepted by the current request: truck routing avoiding truck bans/weight limits/low bridges, bridge height alerts, speed limit + overspeed alert, weigh station alerts, restriction alerts, trip sheet tied to the mileage log; defaults 13'6" / 80,000 lbs; foreground-only GPS honesty.
- Standing context: employer pays $0.55/mile, miles logged state line to state line with full state names, pay per state and total — carried into trip sheet and route pay math.
- Added in this build: 3 route options, avoid-tolls ON by default, fuel stops + best-stop recommendation with Love's / Pilot-Flying J toggles, safety scoring from real weather with traffic honestly unavailable, loads by photo/file with vision extraction + Route it + per-load $/mi, Ask Meca assistant fed from live app state, satellite/hybrid map layers, and installable-app (PWA-style) prep for a later native wrapper.

## Tested sources
### Nominatim (geocoding + reverse)
**Used by**: destination search, load city geocoding, state detection
**Test**: `curl -A TruckerGPS-Test https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?q=Mobile,AL` + `/reverse` at Mobile — returned city results and address.state = "Alabama" (full name).
**Processing**: throttled reverse-geocode (>=60s or >0.5 mi) during trips.
**Update (2026-10-23, driver report "GPS wouldn't pull up some addresses")**: verified live that Nominatim free-text returns ZERO results for casually typed queries ("Loves Travel Stop Biloxi MS" — no apostrophe/comma) that DO resolve when well-formed. All forward geocoding now runs one chain (`geocodeSearch` in App.tsx): pasted "lat, lon" → Nominatim free text (viewbox-biased to the truck, unbounded) → Nominatim structured (street/city/state/ZIP parsed from the typed text) → Photon fuzzy fallback. Result labels are rebuilt from structured address parts (short, checkable) instead of raw display_name.

### Photon — Komoot (fuzzy fallback geocoding)
**Used by**: fallback in the geocoding chain above, only when the Nominatim and ArcGIS passes return nothing
**Test**: `curl "https://photon.komoot.io/api/?q=Loves%20Travel%20Stop%20Biloxi%20MS&limit=6&lang=en"` — returned Love's Travel Center, Biloxi MS (the exact query Nominatim missed); "IP Casino Resort Biloxi MS" resolved on both engines. Keyless, OSM-based, GeoJSON out; US-filtered client-side via properties.countrycode. Slower than Nominatim (~2-4s), hence fallback-only.

### ArcGIS World Geocoder — Esri (non-OSM address coverage)
**Used by**: second pass in the `geocodeSearch` chain (after Nominatim free text, before structured/Photon), plus the labelled area fallback is Nominatim-based (see chain comment in App.tsx).
**Why (2026-10-24, driver report "GPS still don't pu new addresses", screenshot query "7809 Oakmont dr n Theodore al 36582")**: verified live that this address returns ZERO from Nominatim free text (with and without viewbox), Nominatim structured, Photon, AND the US Census geocoder (oneline + structured, benchmark Public_AR_Current) — the street is absent from OpenStreetMap entirely, so no OSM-based engine can ever find it. ArcGIS matched it exactly: `findAddressCandidates?SingleLine=7809 Oakmont dr n Theodore al 36582&countryCode=US` → candidate "7809 Oakmont Dr N, Theodore, Alabama, 36582", score 100, Addr_type PointAddress, location 30.56173, -88.22418 (inside the Theodore CDP extent). Also verified on "Loves Travel Stop Biloxi MS" (100), "Pilot Travel Center Jackson MS" (100), "Mobile AL" (100), junk query "asdfgh qqqq" (no candidates — no invented matches). CORS open (Access-Control-Allow-Origin echoes origin). Keyless endpoint; requests carry `forStorage=false` and candidates scoring <70 are dropped client-side. Chain is now: pasted lat/lon → Nominatim free text → ArcGIS → Nominatim structured → Photon → labelled city/ZIP-center area fallback (full street addresses only, label states plainly it is the town/ZIP center, not the exact address).

### Inspection checklists (pre-trip / post-trip DVIR) — 2026-10-24
**Owner ask**: "driver need a way to do checklist for pretrip and post trip for hos and to earn points". Built on the existing formal DVIR machinery (`dvir_reports` + `saveDvir`/`listDvirs`, defects flag 🚨 to the owner feed; both kinds already appeared in the DOT inspection packet). Changes: (1) `DvirPanel` (Driver tab) now carries BOTH checklists behind a Pre-trip/Post-trip toggle — previously the form could only file pretrip even though the schema/action already accepted posttrip; Today gained an "Inspection checklists" card (start-of-day / end-of-day buttons) that lands on the panel with the kind pre-picked. (2) New action `beginDvirChecklist`: starting a checklist seals a `dvir_checklist_started` chain event; starting the PRE-trip also seals a `duty_change` to On Duty (not driving) with source `pretrip_checklist` (badged "PRE-TRIP START" in the HOS log & fixes list) — only when the driver is Off Duty/Sleeper/no prior state, never over On Duty/Driving, and only from the driver's own tap (no role gate: employee drivers, owner-operators, and owners alike — a checklist start is not an HOS log edit). Post-trip start changes no duty state. (3) Points: new daily-table line `posttrip_dvir` "Post-trip DVIR signed" **+25**; table lines now sum to 525 while `DAILY_POINTS_CAP` stays **500** (unchanged, enforced in `awardDailyTask`), so a perfect day still pays exactly 500. Pre-trip keeps its existing +50 "dvir" line. No schema migration — `dvir_reports.kind` already held both values and points are ledger rows.

### 30-minute scan ahead (driver intel + Valhalla re-check) — 2026-10-24
**Owner ask**: "add a 30 minute scan ahead just incase a route is needed therefore driver won't be stuck in traffic if an alternative route is available." Client-only, no new source and no schema change. While GPS is live and a route is active, a heartbeat (first scan immediately, then ~every 2 min, sooner when a fresh intel report lands) projects the already-loaded driver intel reports (`listIntelReports`, the same rows the map draws) onto the selected route line: blocking kinds only (road_closed, crash, hazard, debris, stopped_truck, scale_line), ≤0.75 mi off the line, ≤6h old, inside a window of the miles the route's own pace covers in 30 minutes (bounded 5–50 mi). A hit spends ONE silent Valhalla call from the truck's current position to the same destination (identical truck costing + toll setting as `planRoute`; no route state touched) and holds the result: an escape qualifies when it leaves every threat >0.6 mi off its line at ≥ −3 min cost, or saves ≥6 min outright. The alert (Drive screen both layouts + Routes screen, banner + voice once per threat signature, AI activity log entry) offers the pre-built route as a one-tap switch — never auto-applied; taking it applies the scanned options like a fresh plan (weather enrichment + fuel stops reload, load ETA plan updated, auto-pick told the batch is already picked). Dismissal is per threat signature; the held escape is reused for 3 min per signature so the heartbeat never hammers Valhalla. Honest limits, stated in the UI copy: at launch the scan saw driver-reported trouble only; since the 2026-10-24 TomTom wiring (see the Live traffic section), Gold+ scans ALSO see live TomTom incidents in the corridor plus a flow-based delay estimate — Basic still sees driver reports only (and makes zero TomTom calls), a TomTom outage falls back to driver reports with the chip saying so, and when fresh routing finds nothing better the card says the current route is still the best known way through.

### Valhalla demo (truck routing)
**Used by**: up to 3 truck-legal routes, maneuvers, toll flag
**Test**: POST https://valhalla1.openstreetmap.de/route with costing truck + alternates:2 — trip 168.98 mi with summary.has_toll, plus 1 alternate; second costing variant (use_highways 0) as fallback for an extra option.
**Processing**: profile dims converted (ft->m, lbs->kg); use_tolls 0 when Avoid tolls ON. Valhalla gives NO toll dollar cost — UI shows flag + "cost unavailable", never an invented price.

### Overpass API (limits, restrictions, weigh stations, fuel)
**Used by**: maxspeed lookup, maxheight/maxweight/hgv=no hazards, weigh stations, amenity=fuel corridor scan
**Test**: POST https://overpass-api.de/api/interpreter — first probe without User-Agent got HTTP 406; with UA it returned real ways (maxspeed "25 mph"/"35 mph") and a weighbridge node. Slow (30-90s) — client uses long timeouts, caching, and honest unavailable states.
**Processing**: maxspeed/maxheight/maxweight parsers handle OSM unit variants; fuel brand from brand/name/operator tags (Love's, Pilot/Flying J); diesel price only from rare OSM price tags, else "price unavailable" — no keyless live price feed exists.

### Open-Meteo (weather along route)
**Used by**: per-route safety score
**Test**: `curl https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=30.69&longitude=-88.04&current=weather_code,wind_speed_10m,visibility` — returned live WMO code/wind/visibility, keyless.
**Processing**: sample 4 points per route; hazards (storm/snow/ice/heavy rain/fog/high wind) deduct from a 5-star score; failure marks weather unavailable.

### Live traffic — TomTom Traffic API (edit, 2026-10-24)
**Used by**: Gold+ live-traffic layer — 30-minute scan incidents + flow delay, route-card delay/traffic-aware ETA annotations.
**Source**: TomTom Traffic API (Freemium/Evaluation key "STONEMILES-APP", products: Traffic API, Routing API, Orbis Traffic Flow API Extended Tiles, Orbis Traffic Incidents API Extended Tiles). Monthly free quotas on the account: Routing 20,000/mo, Flow Segment Data 20,000/mo, Incident Details 2,500/mo (incidents are the scarce call).
**Endpoints** (called SERVER-SIDE ONLY via `server/src/tomtom.ts`; the key is a server-side secret — never in client code, never rendered, never returned by any action):
- Incidents: `https://api.tomtom.com/traffic/services/5/incidentDetails` (bbox + fields for geometry/properties incl. delay, magnitudeOfDelay, roadNumbers).
- Flow: `https://api.tomtom.com/traffic/services/4/flowSegmentData/absolute/10/json` (point + unit=MPH → currentSpeed/freeFlowSpeed/travel times).
**Test**: key verified live before wiring — real Flow and Incidents responses returned for the Theodore/Mobile, AL area; re-verified after wiring through the app's own actions (`getScanTraffic`, `getRouteTraffic`, `getTomTomStatus`) in the sandbox.
**Processing**: Valhalla remains the truck-legal router — TomTom never supplies geometry. Route delay = max(on-route incident delay sum capped 90 min, flow slowdown estimate = free-flow minutes × (mean freeFlow/current speed − 1) over ≤4 sampled points). Scan: incidents in the corridor bbox are projected onto the route line client-side and merged with driver-report threats; flow over ≤3 window points estimates delay through the window. Caching/discipline: server caches incidents per ~0.02°-rounded bbox and flow per rounded point for 5 min; the scan client asks at most once per ~5 min per active trip and only while GPS + an active route are live (Gold+ only — the client never calls on Basic and the server gate refuses Basic before any fetch, so Basic sessions make zero TomTom calls). Failure/quota: actions answer `available: false`; the scan chip says it fell back to driver reports only and route cards show the honest traffic-unavailable state — a delay is never invented.

### Address/place search — TomTom Search API (edit, 2026-10-27)
**Used by**: PRIMARY rung of the one shared geocode chain (`geocodeSearch` in App.tsx — Explore search bar, Drive destination box, A→B planner, saved places, load cities) plus as-you-type autocomplete suggestions on the Explore bar and the Plan-a-trip destination box (the A→B planner already had as-you-type live search through the same chain).
**Source**: TomTom Search API fuzzy search — `GET https://api.tomtom.com/search/2/search/{query}.json` (countrySet=US, language=en-US, lat/lon bias toward the truck). Called SERVER-SIDE ONLY via `fetchTomTomSearch` / `fetchTomTomSuggestions` in `server/src/tomtom.ts` behind the Gold+-gated actions `searchPlaces` / `suggestPlaces`; the key never appears in client code, UI, or logs. Suggestions reuse the same fuzzy endpoint fed partial text (each suggestion carries a real position). Full search and suggestions share a server cache (10 min per normalized query + ~7-mi position grid); queries <3 chars never call out; the client debounces autocomplete ~450 ms. This respects the account's shared free allowance (2,500 non-tile requests/day across TomTom APIs).
**Entitlement test (2026-10-27, BEFORE wiring)**: the existing STONEMILES-APP key is **NOT entitled to the Search API** — fuzzy search, autocomplete, and POI search all returned HTTP 403 `{"detailedError":{"code":"Forbidden","message":"You are not allowed to access this endpoint"}}` for known-good queries ("7809 Oakmont dr n Theodore al 36582", "IP Casino Biloxi MS", partial "loves travel st"). **Shipped state: wiring complete, TomTom rung cleanly DISABLED.** The first 403 trips a 6-hour circuit breaker in `tomtom.ts`; while disabled, `searchPlaces`/`suggestPlaces` answer `available: false, searchDisabled: true`, the chain falls through to the free rungs (Nominatim → ArcGIS → structured → Photon → labelled town/ZIP-center area fallback), and autocomplete suggestions are served by the free Nominatim pass — no dead end, no invented pin, no fake TomTom result. `getTomTomStatus` now also reports `searchCalls`, `suggestionCalls`, and `searchDisabled`. **To turn TomTom on: enable the Search API product on the STONEMILES-APP key in the TomTom account** — the breaker then lapses and TomTom becomes primary with no code change.
**Tier discipline**: identical to the traffic layer — Gold+ only; the server gate refuses Basic before any fetch, so Basic sessions make zero TomTom calls and keep the free chain. No changes to routing, traffic, money, rewards, or data semantics.

### Map tiles — OSM + Esri
**Used by**: Leaflet base layers (Streets / Satellite / Hybrid toggle)
**Test**: OSM tile HEAD 200 image/png with CORS *; Esri World Imagery tile 200 image/jpeg; Esri Reference World_Transportation and World_Boundaries_and_Places tiles 200 image/png (label overlays for Hybrid).
**Processing**: dark CSS filter on the OSM pane for cab look; satellite/hybrid unfiltered; routes/markers sit in overlay panes above all base layers.

### Leaflet (unpkg CDN)
**Used by**: map library loaded in index.html (window.L), typed via globals.d.ts declarations.

## Installable-app prep
- index.html carries theme-color #0b0d0e, mobile-web-app-capable, apple-mobile-web-app-capable, black-translucent status bar, apple-mobile-web-app-title "Trucker GPS".
- main.tsx injects a runtime web app manifest (name/short_name Trucker GPS, display standalone, portrait, background/theme #0b0d0e) using build-asset PNG icons (192/512, derived from the artifact icon) and sets apple-touch-icon.
- All logic is client-side against typed server actions — a Capacitor wrapper can package the same build later without a rewrite.

## Long-term data behavior
- **Refresh policy**: no cron. All live data fetched client-side in use. Profile, trips, loads persist in app.db via actions; load documents stored as blobs.
- **Growth**: trips and loads grow one row per save; profile is a single row.
- **Ordering**: trips/loads newest first; routes fastest-first; alerts/stops by distance.
- **Time semantics**: instants stored UTC, rendered viewer-local.

## Rejected approaches
- **Overpass without UA** — 406; all queries go via browser fetch.
- **Toll costs / live diesel prices from keyless sources** — don't exist; flagged unavailable, never invented. (Live traffic was in this bucket until 2026-10-24, when the keyed TomTom layer landed — see the Live traffic section above.)
- **Static manifest file in dist** — the SDK bundler only emits index.html + imported assets, so the manifest is injected at runtime from imported icon assets instead.

## Addendum — all major truck stops (edit, 2026-10-22)
- Brand system extended from Love's/Pilot to every major chain in OSM brand/name/operator data: Love's, Pilot/Flying J, TA TravelCenters, Petro, Sapp Bros, Kwik Trip/Kwik Star, Casey's, Maverik, Road Ranger, Ambest, Bosselman/Pump & Pantry, plus independent truck plazas (name/tagged as truck stops, incl. `truck_stop=yes` and hgv-tagged truck stops). Each brand: own toggle (all default ON), own marker color + monogram, own label in lists.
- Persistence: profile gains `brand_offs` (comma-separated OFF brand keys, migration 0010); Love's/Pilot keep their original columns and the server keeps all three in sync, so a pre-existing Love's/Pilot choice is never lost. getProfile returns the effective OFF list.
- Data: same Overpass corridor query (amenity=fuel + truck_stop=yes), now sampling the route corridor AND a center around the driver, `out … 400`, with a 5-minute client-side cache of RAW elements per corridor — brand toggles re-filter locally and never re-fetch. Amenities come only from tags: fuel:diesel → diesel yes/no/unknown; truck_parking / parking:truck / parking:hgv / capacity:truck → truck parking; shower(s) → showers; weighbridge/scales → scales. Unknowns render as "unknown". Prices still only from OSM price tags, else "price unavailable".
- Best-stop pick unchanged in shape: cheapest reachable diesel first across all ON brands; a named ON brand is only the tie-breaker among close prices; OFF brands never enter the list.
- Ask Meca: brand toggles generalized to one `toggle_brand` command with a brand key; the state snapshot lists brands ON/OFF by label and each stop's amenity line.

## Addendum — 2026 visual refresh (edit, 2026-10-22)
- Outfit (geometric sans) for UI text + Barlow Condensed numerals, tabular figures throughout. Frosted-glass panels (blur + translucency + luminous 1px border + layered shadow), 20px panel radius, floating glass bottom dock with neon active state, glass-pill banners with glow edges (alerts stay solid high-contrast red), neon teal→green gradient reserved for primary actions, the selected route line (glow underlay + slow flow dashes), chat "mine" bubbles, rank ring, and points highlights. Amber remains the data accent. All motion honors prefers-reduced-motion; driving surfaces (speed/limit) stay near-black and stark. Satellite/hybrid get pointer-events-none dark scrims behind overlays.

## Addendum — Swap + subscription tokenomics (edit, 2026-10-22) — STAGING, design only
- Swap screen (Driver tab, beside Points): STONERxSWAP design lineage (~/workspace/stoner-swap — Uniswap v4 on Robinhood Chain 4663, 1% fee to the project fee wallet, wallet signs, app never holds keys). Pairs run through $STONER ⇄ ETH / USDG. The 1% fee math is real and local (itemized from the entered amount, slippage selectable); the receive estimate is NOT shown — $STONER has no market price pre-launch and no rate is ever invented. No wallet connection, no transactions. Points are explicitly not swappable.
- Subscription flow (Plans sheet): at go-live, Full/Owner payments auto-buy $STONER and split exactly 25/25/25/25 — burn / earning pool / team / locker. "Coin flow" transparency view shows the four buckets plus pool paid-out and recycled-in totals, read (at go-live) from the sealed records; in staging all totals are honestly 0 with the no-simulation note. Locker: unlock terms "set at go-live", not spendable from the app by anyone.
- Earning pool is a recycling loop: funded by the 25% slice; pays driver rewards in $STONER for the same GPS-verified events that earn Points pre-launch (Points = pre-launch accounting unit); in-app $STONER spending (discounted subscriptions, featured profile, merch) runs through the same 4-way split so 25% returns to the pool. Hard rule surfaced in UI: if the pool is dry, rewards pause — never minted from air.
- Nothing in this addendum moves money: billing, the auto-buy, and any on-chain step still wait for the app-store release and the owner's separate go-live approval.

## Addendum — wallet features (edit, 2026-10-22) — STAGING, design only
- Swap pairs per the user's latest spec run through $STONER ⇄ USDT / ETH (supersedes the USDG wording in the swap addendum above; STONERxSWAP lineage unchanged).
- Wallet area (Driver tab): Balances view ($STONER / USDT / ETH honestly 0 pre-launch; Points shown from the real points ledger, explicitly separate and non-transferable; pending rewards = none — Points settle rewards until token go-live), quick actions Send / Swap / Buy / Withdraw (scroll to the designed sections).
- Sends: user-to-user $STONER, initiated from the Wallet Send form (username search over the existing directory action, incl. display-name results), from a profile ("Send $STONER" button), or from a DM header ("Send" button) — all preset the same confirm design (receiver avatar initial + name + @username + amount, "sends are final", default limits 10,000/send · 50,000/day labelled owner-configurable). Confirm is disabled in staging; at go-live sends are internal balance transfers recorded in the sealed chain (sender/receiver/amount/time). Points can never be sent.
- Pool view: $STONER/USDT and $STONER/ETH pairs, depth shown as "— at go-live" (never invented), price source stated (the Uniswap v4 pool quoter itself). Informational only; user-provided liquidity is a separate future decision, not offered.
- Buy path: "Buy $STONER & pay Full" one-tap card in Swap — buys exactly the Full amount and applies the $STONER discount; disabled in staging like everything else.
- Withdrawals: token pick, address paste, Robinhood Chain shown, summary with network fee "shown exactly at go-live" (never guessed), first-withdrawal-to-new-address double-check step (checkbox) + session address book with labels, empty withdrawal history that will read from the sealed records. Custody is labelled honestly in the UI: in-app balances are platform-held until withdrawn; the app never holds keys and never asks for a seed phrase/private key.

## Addendum — card payments (edit, 2026-10-22) — STAGING, design only
- Debit/credit card is a designed payment method for Full/Owner subscriptions and for a standalone "Buy $STONER with card" in the Wallet balances view. No live processor, no charges, no card fields anywhere in the app.
- Design rules rendered in the Plans "Pay by card" section + wallet buy card: card numbers NEVER touch the app (licensed provider's hosted fields at go-live; app stores only provider token + last 4); confirm screen shows the full chain (card charge total → $STONER bought → 4-way split → subscription active) with ALL fees itemized (subscription price, card processing fee, on-ramp/buy fee) — fee amounts come from the provider at checkout and are never invented in staging; flow is all-or-nothing (declined/failed card → plain reason, no half-activation, no split recorded, failure itself recorded); refunds/chargebacks reverse the activation and mark split entries reversed in the sealed records, never deleted.
- Platform honesty line included verbatim in the UI: "Card checkout available where platform rules allow." (iPhone subscriptions may be required to run through Apple billing; card route = e.g. web checkout.)

## Addendum — per-user wallet, sell & cash-out (edit, 2026-10-22) — STAGING, design only
- Every user auto-gets an in-app wallet at signup (no Web3 knowledge, no seed phrase). Balances view now includes a USD cash balance beside $STONER/USDT/ETH. Custody is labelled verbatim in the UI: "your in-app wallet is held by the platform for you — you can withdraw to your own Web3 wallet anytime," plus the never-seed-phrase line, plus an optional advanced "Connect your own Web3 wallet" path (disabled in staging) for self-custody users to receive sends/withdrawals directly.
- Sell for cash: designed flow — pick $STONER or USDT, amount → quote shows coin price / sell fee / exact USD (all "at go-live", never invented pre-launch) → sells into the USD cash balance, recorded in the sealed records. Disabled in staging.
- Cash-out rails from the USD balance: bank ACH (1–3 business days), debit card instant (fee shown separately, where supported), Cash App $Cashtag (where the off-ramp supports it) — all labelled "COMING WITH PROVIDER LAUNCH" rather than presented as available; Web3 wallet rail routes to the existing Withdraw design. Every rail's confirm shows amount, itemized fees, net, and arrival (modelled in copy).
- KYC stated plainly before first cash-out, in the user's words: "Cash withdrawals need a one-time ID check — that's the law, handled by our payout partner; the app never sees your ID documents." Cash-out limits shown as configurable defaults ($2,500/tx · $5,000/day). Tax: "Tax documents / activity export" entry — the activity export is REAL today (downloads the user's points history + a no-token-activity statement from their own records); tax documents arrive with the provider at go-live, labelled as such.

## Addendum — multi-chain wallet + $STONER-only swap rule (edit, 2026-10-22) — STAGING, design only
- Chains in the wallet design: Robinhood Chain (home — $STONER, ETH, USDT; swaps, subscription auto-buy, earning pool, in-app sends live here), Solana (SOL, USDT), Bitcoin (BTC), Polygon (POL, USDT), Sui (SUI, USD stablecoin). Balances are grouped BY CHAIN with a chain badge per group and the chain named on every row; same-ticker assets on different chains are separate rows, never merged.
- $STONER exists only on Robinhood Chain. Other chains: hold / send (external, via Withdraw) / withdraw, plus buy/sell where a provider supports it; cash-out can source from a sell on any supported chain. Bridging is a separate, clearly-labelled step — never silent (the one designed bridge is inside the cross-chain "Swap to $STONER" action, with its fee and wait itemized).
- Network safety, modelled with REAL local logic that already runs in staging: Withdraw and Sell force an explicit asset+network pick (grouped select); the address is validated against the selected chain's format (EVM 0x/42, Sui 0x/66, Bitcoin bc1/1/3, Solana base58 32–44) with plain-language rejection, including "that looks like a <other chain> address" detection; the confirm summary states "You are withdrawing X SYM on CHAIN" in large type; first withdrawal to a new address shows the double-check step with the line "Sending on the wrong network can lose these funds permanently."
- Receive: one receive address per chain listed in the wallet, labelled by network, with copy + QR controls present but disabled until real addresses exist at go-live (no fake addresses shown).
- Swap rule: outside-chain assets offer exactly one direction — [asset] → $STONER, presented as one action ("Swap to $STONER") with bridge fee + 1% swap fee + expected wait itemized in the quote (values at go-live, never invented). No pairs exist between outside assets. Picking an outside asset as the swap TARGET shows the plain explanation ("Other chains swap into $STONER only") and routes the target to $STONER. Home-chain $STONER ↔ USDT/ETH pairs unchanged.

## Addendum — hash chain (added during build)
- chain_log table: append-only SHA-256 chain (hash = sha256(prev_hash | event | payload)), genesis "GENESIS". Trip events logged live under a session id (trip_start, state_crossing, trip_stop) and linked to the saved trip; per-state miles/pay + trip_complete appended at save; load_saved/load_updated/load_deleted and trip_deleted also chained. verifyChain recomputes the whole chain server-side; export downloads the record + its entries as JSON. Local integrity only — on-chain anchor explicitly "not yet enabled" in the proof UI; no wallet, no chain writes.
- Esri World Imagery + Reference overlays verified live (200 image/jpeg and image/png) for Satellite/Hybrid layers.

## Addendum — alt-token subscription split (2026-10-22)
- Second subscription payment path, modelled in Plans beside the four-way split: payment in other listed tokens (SOL, BTC, MATIC/POL, SUI, USDT, other wallet-listed assets) splits 90% to the team and 10% auto-buys $STONER which is burned entirely (none to pool or locker). Card/fiat and $STONER payments keep the 25/25/25/25 rule. The plain-words rule ("$STONER/card payments split four ways (burn/pool/team/locker). Other-token payments: 90% team, 10% buys $STONER and burns.") is shown in Plans before any payment, as the confirm screen will at go-live.
- Coin Flow gains two totals for the second path (team total — other-token payments; $STONER bought & burned — other-token payments), both honestly 0 in staging, and its copy states each sealed subscription record carries the payment method and the exact split applied. Staging unchanged: no live payments, no simulated splits.

## Addendum — checkout UX: no splits at checkout (2026-10-22)
- Buyer-facing purchase flow (Plans "How paying works" + payment tabs + Pay by card) shows ONLY the clean process — 1. Pay → 2. $STONER buy completes → 3. Subscription active — with amounts paid/received and the neutral line "Paid subscriptions support the $STONER ecosystem." No split percentages or bucket breakdowns anywhere in checkout, for either payment path. Payment tabs inside the purchase area: [Card] [$STONER] [Other tokens]; the Other tokens tab lists every listed wallet asset (CHAIN_ASSETS minus $STONER) with balance (0 in staging) and price ("Price at go-live"), and selecting one shows its total + the same steps.
- The split logic itself is unchanged underneath (25/25/25/25 for card/fiat/$STONER; 90% team / 10% buy-and-burn for other tokens) and its full detail now lives ONLY in the Coin Flow ledger view ("How each payment is split — the ledger rules" block + per-path totals + per-subscription payment method and split applied in the sealed records).

## Addendum — cab features, ELD, owner portal, onboarding (2026-10-22)
- HOS clocks computed client-side from the sealed duty log (11h driving / 14h window / 70h-8-day cycle / 30-min break after 8h driving); warnings at 60/30/0 min via banner+voice, each sealed via logSealedEvent(hos_warning). Labelled "Planning aid — your official record is your ELD."
- Detention: sealed detention_arrival/departure events per load+site (free time default 2h, rate default $50/h, both editable, stored on-device); dwell/excess/estimate computed from the sealed events; exportable per-load report; detention line joins the chat share snapshot. Events appear in the load's sealed timeline automatically (refKind=load).
- Parking planner: fuel stops (existing brand data) + OSM rest areas (highway=rest_area/services via Overpass) + parking intel reports; ranked reachable-on-HOS-clock → off-route <1mi → known availability; one tap routes via setDestination. Threshold default 2h, editable.
- NWS: api.weather.gov active alerts (keyless) for position + route points, warning/watch severe types only, banner+voice once per alert id; route cards show NWS hazards as a separate labelled chip — never blended into the Open-Meteo score. US-only labelled.
- Breakdown: persistent Drive-tab button; Overpass vehicle_repair/car_repair/towing-name search; coords big+copyable; share card via chat kind "location"; breakdown_open/resolved sealed; queued offline via the seal queue (localStorage, flushed in order on reconnect — sync never rewrites).
- Offline routes: corridor tile prefetch (zooms 9–13, ~5mi each side, browser cache) + route data (coords, maneuvers, stops, alerts) in localStorage with size estimate before download; Settings cache management (list/delete/storage); offline banner; auto-download toggle. Free = chosen route; alternates tagged FULL.
- ELD/tablet: Settings "Connect truck" — BLE/Wi-Fi pairing design, read-only, J1939/OBD + per-vendor integrations at go-live; staging state is honestly "No ELD connected" with NO fabricated vehicle data. Merge rules displayed: ELD odometer becomes miles source of record when connected (trip rows label "Miles: GPS" today), ELD clocks = official next to app planning clocks; DTCs would surface as plain-language alerts + seal into the trip record.
- Owner portal + WALLET WALL (absolute): portal shows work data only (duty/HOS, trip sheets, detention, paperwork, IFTA). NO wallet fields exist in any company-facing action or screen — the wallet domain is never connected, and the join consent screen says so verbatim. Portal/document views are sealed as portal_access chain events; drivers read them in "Who viewed my records."
- Owner onboarding: company_setup + company_docs tables (migration 0011). Setup complete = required fields (legal name, address, EIN, MC, USDOT, contact name/email, fleet size) + W-9/authority/insurance uploaded; generateDriverCode refuses until complete; createCompany no longer mints a first code. Docs are blobs (12MB cap), status uploaded/verified, authority/insurance expiry with <30d renewal warnings; owner marks verified; year-end per-driver packet export (organizes paperwork, does not file taxes — stated in UI).
- Company identity is PUBLIC business information per the user: verification card shows legal name, MC, USDOT, address openly; EIN lives in business paperwork/document flows. Only a driver's PERSONAL identifiers stay protected — driver documents are files; lists show name/kind/status only, document view is role-gated and access-logged.
