## Addendum — Company Owner Auto Log (2026-10-22)
- Migration 0012: company_setup.auto_log_enabled + auto_log_notify (immediate|digest), company_members.auto_log_override (null|on|off), daily_logs table. Owner controls company default + notification preference + per-driver override (setAutoLogSettings, owner-only); effective state per driver from getAutoLogSettings.
- Day flow: morning pretrip (9 items, safety-critical flagged: tires/brakes/lights/coupling/load securement) → startDailyLog seals daily_log_open with start miles (ELD-labelled when connected — none in staging, so driver-entered odometer or GPS-labelled), fuel at start, HOS snapshot, pretrip JSON; critical defects return criticalDefect so the client warns the driver (never blocks) and the portal feed shows 🚨 immediately. Stop clock → closeDailyLog seals daily_log_close with finish/total miles, fuel usage (source-labelled: ELD burn at go-live; staging estimates from GPS miles at the driver's mpg setting), end HOS, loads delivered (POD seals that day — podSealedAt added to listLoads), detention pointer.
- Missed close: startDailyLog auto-closes a prior open log labelled "auto-closed (no manual stop) — closed when the next day's pretrip started"; listDailyLogs sweeps open logs past 14h window + 2h grace with the same label; the driver sees an acknowledge prompt. Driver and owner read the SAME daily_logs rows — identical log, no separate versions. Wallet wall: logs carry work data only.

## Addendum — security hardening: blockchain, payments, PII vault (edit, 2026-10-22)
- **PIN (was the weak point):** the old flow stored an unsalted SHA-256 of the PIN — brute-forceable in seconds from any DB copy. Now the client sends the PIN over the HTTPS action channel and the server stores only a PBKDF2-SHA256 record (120,000 iterations, random 16-byte salt, constant-time compare). Legacy hashes verify once and auto-upgrade. Changing/removing a PIN requires the current PIN. `pin_guard` (migration 0015): 5 consecutive failed unlocks lock unlocking for 15 minutes; the lock screen shows tries left and the lockout time. All PIN events (set / removed / failed / locked / change-denied) land in `security_events` AND the sealed chain.
- **PII vault:** the EIN was plain text in `company_setup`. It is now AES-256-GCM encrypted (random IV per write) with a 256-bit vault key generated once and held as a server-only blob — separate from the database, so dumps/exports/logs never contain a readable EIN. Legacy plain values mask identically and encrypt on next save; a masked value submitted by the form means "unchanged". `getCompanySetup` returns only the mask (••-••••1234) + `einOnFile`/`einEncrypted`. Full EIN leaves the server only via `revealEin` (owner-only, PIN-verified when a PIN is set, audited in `security_events` + chain). Company/load document blobs are now also deleted by the full data wipe (previously orphaned), and the wipe clears the new security/payment tables + company setup/docs/daily logs.
- **Payments:** still NO live processor and nothing charges — that stays staged until app-store release + the owner's go-live approval. What was added is the secure rails design made real in code: `payment_ledger` (migration 0015) records payment *intents* only (method, amount, plan, status `requires_provider`) under a UNIQUE idempotency key — a retry/double-tap returns the same intent (`duplicate: true`) and can never double-charge. No card column exists anywhere; a Luhn-checked card-number detector hard-rejects card-shaped data from intent fields and logs the rejection as a security event. The go-live rules (hosted-fields tokenization — only token + last 4 return; all-or-nothing activation; refunds reverse, never delete, split entries; webhooks trusted only with verified processor signature) are rendered verbatim in the Security Center from the `getSecurityCenter` action.
- **Blockchain:** the hash chain already sealed trips/loads/PODs; it now also seals `security_event` and `payment_intent` entries. `getSecurityCenter` recomputes the whole chain server-side and returns intact/broken + head hash, so the Security Center (Settings → Privacy & data) shows live integrity, PIN state, vault state, payment readiness, and the recent audit trail in one place, with a Re-check button (verifyChain + refetch). On-chain anchoring/wallets/escrow remain OFF by design, stated in the UI — "hard to impossible to hack" is presented honestly as defense-in-depth (salted slow KDF + lockout, field-level AES-GCM, masked reads, append-only tamper-evident chain, audit trail, no card/key material anywhere in the app), never as an absolute guarantee.

## Addendum — exports everywhere: spreadsheet, paperwork, trip logs (edit, 2026-10-22)
- **One ExportBar pattern** (client): every exportable record gets ⬇ Download · 📋 Copy · 🖨 Print view · 📤 Send out. Print view is a clean in-page sheet (record only, mono text, full state names) — printing runs from the browser/device print command on that view (in-iframe `window.print` is not used). Send out uses the system share sheet (file attached when the device supports file sharing), falls back to an email draft (mailto, text body), then to copy. Nothing is ever shared automatically — every path starts from the driver's tap.
- **Spreadsheet (server `exportTripSpreadsheet`):** CSV (opens in Excel/Sheets/Numbers) with one row per trip × state — Trip ID, Start, End, Destination, State (full name), Miles, Rate 0.55, Pay, Record hash — plus a grand-total row carrying the chain head hash; a second CSV totals miles/pay by state. Surfaced in the Trips tab ("Spreadsheet & trip-log exports" panel) and the Driver tab.
- **Trip logs:** the IFTA text export (pre-existing) gains the full ExportBar in both Trips and Driver tabs; each saved trip sheet gets its own ExportBar whose text is built from the trip (full state names, miles, pay at $0.55/mi) with the record's seal hash fetched from `getProof`. Daily auto logs: server `exportDailyLogs({logId})` renders one log or all logs as clean text with per-log seal hashes recovered from the chain's daily_log events; ExportBars sit on the past-logs list (all) and on each opened log. Role scoping matches `listDailyLogs` (owner/dispatch see the company, drivers see their own).
- **Paperwork:** server `exportPaperworkIndex` builds a manifest of every load document (lane, SHA-256 fingerprint, POD seal status/time) and every vault document (kind, filename, subject, status, expiry) with the EIN **masked only** — plus ExportBars in Company Setup (manifest + year-end packet, whose text builder was extracted so download/copy/print/send all use the identical text). Originals: load documents get Download original + Send out in the document viewer; company/driver vault documents get Download buttons in every list (through the role-gated, access-logged `getCompanyDoc`) and Download/Send in the viewer; per-document ExportBars carry the document's info text (name/kind/status — never file contents, never identifiers).
- **Rules on every export:** seal/hash included wherever the record has one; the wallet wall holds — no wallet data exists in any export text; personal identifiers stay masked in list/copy views exactly as on screen.

## Addendum — beta codes, referrals, reward wallet (edit, 2026-10-22)
- **Beta codes (`beta_codes`, migration 0013)**: seeded with the exact issued invites the user handed over — owner/master `MILE-X-OWNER1` (reusable) and five single-use driver codes (`MILE-AUPI-KNWY`, `MILE-09V5-R58Q`, `MILE-MAZR-E86U`, `MILE-VSQ5-F1VO`, `MILE-XMHB-R3L9`). No other rows are seeded anywhere in this feature. First run is gated (`beta_access` single row); redemption, revocation, and regeneration append hash-chain events (`beta_code_issued/redeemed/revoked/regenerated`), so the codes live in the sealed records. Owner panel (Driver tab) shows used/available + redeemer + date; owner can revoke/regenerate unused driver codes.
- **Referrals (`referral_codes`, `referral_links`)**: personal code minted lazily per username (`MILE-REF-XXXXXX`), shown on the profile (Settings) and in the Wallet. Link at beta signup (optional field) or later in Settings; self-referral blocked server-side; one link per user, sealed as `referral_linked`.
- **Referral rewards (`referral_rewards`)**: created exactly once per referred user when they first record a paid plan (Full/Owner via `setPlan`, this staging build's subscription event — billing itself stays staged/not-live). Amount from `admin_settings.referral_reward_stoner` (default 100 $STONER, owner-editable in the beta panel). Paid from the reward wallet when it covers the amount, else `queued`; queue drains oldest-first whenever funding lands. Payouts sealed with reason `referral`.
- **Reward wallet (`reward_wallet_ledger`)**: signed $STONER ledger; balance = sum. Funding rule (alt-token path): of the 90% team share, 10% routes here — net per alt-token dollar 81¢ team / 9¢ reward wallet / 10¢ auto-buy $STONER + burn. `fundRewardWallet` (owner) records funding that arrived (auto-posted by the payment path at go-live); `payTeamReward` (owner) pays team-run rewards/giveaways, hard-capped at the held balance. Coin Flow (Plans sheet) reads the bucket live via `getCoinFlow`: balance, total in, referral payouts vs team rewards listed separately, queued rewards shown honestly. Buyer-facing checkout copy is untouched — no percentages there.

## Addendum — Next Level pass: IFTA Complete, Money Truth, Worth-It, Settlements, Co-pilot, Verify-Anywhere (edit, 2026-10-22)
- Competitor research (2026-10-22, parent-supplied): Trucker Path / CoPilot / Hammer / TruckMap / Garmin dēzl and IFTA/bookkeeping tools (TruckLedger, TruckOmeter, Load Cabin, FleetCollect). Gaps closed in-app from the driver's OWN sealed records only; no market rates anywhere.
- Migration 0016: fuel_purchases, expenses, settlements, scale_tickets, personal_docs, verify_codes, dvir_reports, passport_prefs tables; profile.hazmat(+class), loads.hazmat, app_settings.language columns.
- IFTA Complete: fuel purchase logging (state from GPS position via on-device current-state, station, gallons, price/gal, receipt photo with vision extraction + SHA-256 fingerprint, sealed) + quarterly report (miles by state from sealed trips, fuel by state, fleet MPG, per-state tax estimate). Tax rates: IFTA quarterly diesel matrix snapshot labelled October 2026 in code (IFTA_DIESEL_RATES) with source+date shown on screen and in the export; labelled estimate, Oregon 0 (weight-mile tax). One-tap export via the standard ExportBar.
- Money Truth: expense log (9 tax categories, receipt extraction, sealed), real CPM + break-even = own logged costs ÷ own sealed miles, week/month/quarter/all views, per-load profit carried from sealed Worth-It scores, tax-time category export.
- Load Worth-It Advisor (Loads tab + Ask Meca commands): green/yellow/red from own break-even, deadhead, rate per TOTAL mile, and detention history at the named facility from own sealed detention events; math printed line by line; every score sealed (load_score) and its profit feeds Money Truth. Facility Memory lists per-facility dwell/detention averages from the same sealed events.
- Settlement Checker: import by photo (vision extraction of lines) or by hand with optional per-line load matching → line-by-line compare against sealed loads/PODs + sealed detention owed (2h free, $50/h labelled defaults) + internal net sanity → sealed mismatch report (settlement_check) with ExportBar.
- Meca Co-pilot: getCopilotBriefing aggregates active load, today's sealed miles/pay, break-even, open detention clocks, documents due (personal vault ≤45d, company docs ≤30d). Morning Briefing card on Drive (spoken via the app voice), Post-Trip Debrief card at trip close + in the briefing card. Ask Meca context now carries the co-pilot snapshot; local answers handle "log fuel", "find parking", "what's my break-even", "is this load worth it", settlement and verify questions with real navigation actions.
- Verify-Anywhere: verify_codes minted per sealed trip/load/POD/settlement/passport, anchored to the record's chain hash; verifyCode re-walks the chain to that hash and answers genuine/tampered with a state-level summary (no precise location, no wallet data); checkCustody does the receiver-side delivery check. QR codes are generated locally (client/src/qrcode.ts, byte mode, EC level M, versions 1–10) — no external QR service, no hotlinked images.
- Reputation Passport: passport_prefs, OFF by default, per-stat toggles; export locked while sharing is off.
- Merkle root: getMerkleRoot computes the daily (fallback all-time) Merkle root over chain hashes, displayed in Settings → Security Center with anchoring honestly staged OFF until separate go-live approval.
- Road gaps: weigh-station/parking status from driver intel reports only (timestamped, "not reported" otherwise); CAT scale tickets with per-axle weights + sealed photo; HazMat flag on truck profile + per-load, labelled planning aid; multi-stop planner (add/reorder, leg-by-leg routing through the existing truck router, localStorage-persisted); chain-law/winter notes for states on the driver's board, labelled state-DOT summaries dated October 2026; formal DVIR (9-system checklist, defect workflow, signature, sealed, critical defects flagged); personal document vault (CDL/medical/insurance/registration/IFTA licence) with driver-entered expiries + reminders; Spanish option in Settings (core driving screens: dock, Drive buttons, co-pilot cards).
- Standing rules held: wallet wall (no wallet fields in any new table/action/export), points untouched, $0.55/mi default unchanged, full state names, on-chain anchoring/payments still OFF.

## Addendum — ELD connections: Samsara + Motive (edit, 2026-10-22) — Feature 1 of 3, PREMIUM
- Owner-approved build order: (1) ELD connections, (2) Safety Training academy, (3) full onboarding suite. This pass ships FEATURE 1 ONLY. Gating: PREMIUM — Driver Full / Owner; Driver Simple sees the hub locked with an upgrade path (server also refuses connect/reveal/revoke off-plan). Billing stays staged; choosing a plan still just records it.
- Migration 0019: `eld_connections` (provider, company, label, status not_connected|validating|connected|error|revoked, vault-encrypted token + last4, feeds_json availability check, connected_by, revoked_at) and `eld_snapshots` (per-connection synced feed payloads — EMPTY in production; the go-live sync worker is the only writer). `trips.miles_source` added ("GPS" default).
- Token handling = the EIN pattern verbatim: AES-256-GCM vault encryption (`encryptPii`), masked reads (••••last4), `revealEldToken` owner/admin-only + PIN-verified + audited via security_events + sealed chain; card-shaped tokens hard-rejected pre-storage; revoke wipes the token and seals `eld_revoked` (payload records that already-synced records stay sealed; the ELD remains the official copy). Connect seals `eld_connected`. Passwords are never requested — admin mints an API token in their own provider dashboard (in-app step-by-step for both providers).
- Sync mapping (the contract the go-live worker implements; live provider calls are STAGED with the backend/native wrap — no provider data is fabricated in this build): Samsara — HOS clocks GET /fleet/hos/clocks, HOS logs /fleet/hos/logs, DVIRs /fleet/dvirs, vehicle stats (odometer+GPS) /fleet/vehicles/stats, IFTA /fleet/reports/ifta. Motive — HOS clocks, HOS logs /v1/logs, vehicle inspections, vehicle locations + odometer /v1/vehicle_locations, IFTA trips + summaries. Geotab listed "Coming next" with no connect path.
- Data-availability check: every mapped feed always carries an explicit state — received (snapshot exists, timestamped) / blocked_by_plan (sync worker marks it; the Samsara Telematics-license caveat is printed in the connect explainer, the provider card, and the HOS/DVIR feed details — Safety-only plans return a limited feed) / awaiting_first_sync (staged state). Never silently empty.
- What connected data drives: trips get `miles_source` server-side at save — "ELD odometer — Samsara/Motive" ONLY when a connected ELD's vehicle_stats snapshot actually delivered an odometer reading, else "GPS"; the label shows on trip cards, the trip sheet text, the IFTA text export, and a new spreadsheet column. HOS panel mirrors ELD clocks from the hos_clocks snapshot with "Official record: <provider> ELD", never blended with the planning clocks. DOT Inspection packet gains a separate source-labelled ELD DVIR list + an honest note when a connection exists but no DVIRs have arrived; the packet's first-line planning-aid label is unchanged. `getEldIftaReconciliation` puts sealed app miles/state beside the ELD IFTA feed with differences shown, never overwritten. Owner Portal shows fleet connection status and logs ELD-data views via the existing portal access log. Wallet wall untouched: no ELD field exists in any wallet action/export.
## Addendum — Safety Training Academy (edit, 2026-10-22) — Feature 2 of 3, PREMIUM
- Owner-approved build order pass 2 (ELD was pass 1; onboarding suite and profiles/board are later passes, not in this build). Gating: PREMIUM — Driver Full / Owner; Driver Simple sees the Academy hub locked with the catalog + upgrade path, `getSafetyAcademy` returns no progress off-plan, and `submitTrainingQuiz` refuses off-plan server-side. Billing stays staged.
- Migration 0021: `training_attempts` (every graded attempt — username, course, season, score, pass/fail) and `training_completions` (written ONLY on a pass, with the hash-chain seal hash stored on the row). Production starts EMPTY — no seeded progress anywhere; the full data wipe clears both tables.
- Catalog (content in client/src/SafetyAcademy.tsx; grading keys ONLY in server actions): Defensive Driving (CORE), Hours of Service Rules & Logs (CORE), Pre-Trip Inspection & DVIR (CORE), Winter & Ice Driving, Load Securement, Fatigue & Wellness, Distracted Driving & Phone Policy, Backing, Parking & Tight Spaces, Roadside Inspection Readiness (points at DOT Inspection Mode), Hazmat Awareness. Each course: 3 short plain-language lessons + a 5-question quiz, passing bar 80% (4/5) shown before every quiz; failed attempts are recorded, can be retaken after re-reading, and never create a completion. Lessons and quizzes are fully bilingual EN/ES following the app's existing language setting (Settings → Language), like the other core screens.
- Sealing: a pass appends `training_completed` to the hash chain (course, score, season, and the honest record-kind label) and the completion row stores that hash; completions render on the driver's profile surface (Academy panel sealed-completions list + export), on the Reputation Passport card (behind the driver's existing show-Safety toggle), and on the owner fleet training board.
- Seasons: the shared 31-day season cycle (epoch 2026-10-12, the same `seasonInfo` helper now used by getDriverStats) drives refreshers — the 3 CORE courses come due again each new season (status flips current → due); non-core courses stay passed. Season history is the completions table itself, archived by season_number, never deleted.
- Safety score tie-in (rule printed in the Academy AND in the score's own reasons list): Safety = 100 − 3 × overspeed events (last 7 days) − 5 × CORE courses due this season, premium plans only (a locked feature never costs a free driver points); passing a core refresher removes its −5. Path-back line names the due courses.
- Owner board (`getFleetTrainingBoard`, owner/dispatch or solo Owner-plan): per driver — completed count, current vs due, last completion date, per-course status; opening another driver's detail writes the existing `portal_access` seal (visible in that driver's "Who viewed my records"). Other members' rows are honestly marked as syncing at release (their sealed records live on their own devices in this staging build) — never estimated. Wallet wall holds: training is company-domain work data; no wallet field exists in any Academy action, export, or screen.
- Honesty label, verbatim on the Academy landing, the sealed chain payload, and the Passport line: these are STONEMILES training-completion records — real documentation a fleet can file — NOT an accredited third-party certification.

- VAULT FIX (found by this pass's probes, applies to the EIN vault from the 0015 hardening pass too): the vault key was stored as a blob and read back with `ctx.blobs.getUrl` + `fetch` — but blob URLs are document-relative (`./blobs/…`), so the server-side fetch throws, EVERY `vaultKey()` call silently minted a fresh key, and no ciphertext (EIN or ELD token) could ever be decrypted on reveal. Diagnosed with a temporary probe action (blob head: 32 bytes present; getUrl: relative; fetch: threw; in-call round-trip: false). Fix: the key now lives in the server-only `vault_keys` table (migration 0020; never returned by any action, never in any export, deleted by the full wipe), with the blob kept only as a best-effort mirror. Ciphertext written under the old unreadable keys stays undecryptable — the reveal actions' existing copy ("re-save it" / "revoke and reconnect") is the honest path, and re-saving re-encrypts under the working key (verified by probe: save → mask shows real last-4 → reveal returns the value; ELD connect → reveal → revoke end-to-end).


## Owner-uploaded training videos (owner addition to Pass 2, 2026-10-22)

- Storage: `training_media` rows (company_id, uploaded_by, title, description, mime, blob_key, size_bytes, course_id nullable = lesson inside that Academy course / null = standalone company lesson, sort_order, quiz_course_id nullable, custom_check_json nullable, removed_at) + video bytes in `ctx.blobs` under the company document-vault rules; `training_watch` rows carry honest per-driver progress (started, furthest-point percent, check_passed, completed_at, chain_hash). Migration 0022. Production starts EMPTY — no pre-uploaded videos.
- Company-only, enforced server-side on every action (list, playback URL, progress report, check submit, board stats): the media row's company must equal the caller's own company membership. There is no public or cross-company path; no wallet field exists anywhere in the video actions (wallet wall).
- Upload gate: company owner role (fleet admin) + premium (Simple refused with the premium line; dispatch can view the board but cannot upload/replace/remove). Replace swaps the blob and keeps watch records; remove marks removed_at, deletes the blob, seals `training_video_removed`, and states in the response, the confirm prompt, and the removed row that past completion records stay sealed.
- REAL LIMIT stated in the upload UI and enforced server-side (never a silent failure): 50 MB per video; formats MP4 / WebM / MOV (QuickTime) / M4V. Oversize or wrong-type uploads return the specific reason and save nothing.
- Completion rule, printed in the player: watch to >= 90% (furthest-point tracking, reported by the player) AND pass the attached check when one exists — either one Academy quiz (graded server-side at the same 80% bar, key never shipped) or one owner-written custom question (key stored server-side inside custom_check_json, never returned to clients). Check attempts are recorded in `training_attempts` under courseId `video-<id>`.
- Sealing: on completion the chain gains `training_video_completed` with source "owner-uploaded", the media/course context, and the same not-an-accredited-certification label; the hash is stored on the watch row and shown in the Academy player, the owner's board, and counted on the Reputation Passport alongside Academy completions with the source labelled.
- Owner board: per video — completed members (with dates), in-progress members (with %), and members who haven't started; removed videos stay listed as REMOVED with the completions-kept note.

## Addendum — Pickup load photo + BOL, camera-fresh only (edit, 2026-10-22)
- Applies to company employees (drivers) and individual users (owner-operators) alike — the pickup documentation step lives on every load, no plan/company gate.
- Migration 0023: loads gains two camera-fresh slots — pickup load photo and pickup BOL photo — each with blob key, mime, SHA-256, captured-at, GPS lat/lon, and source flag, plus pickup_sealed_at. Production starts EMPTY (all null) — no pickup photos exist until a driver takes them.
- Actions: `submitPickupPhoto` (loadId, slot load|bol, source, image, GPS, capturedAt) and `getPickupPhoto` (blob URL + stamp for viewing the proof). The server REJECTS any submission whose source is not exactly "camera" with the rule in plain words ("New picture only — gallery uploads aren't accepted for pickup load photos or the BOL.") and stores nothing. Accepted photos are blob-stored, fingerprinted, stamped with capture time + GPS (or "no GPS fix at capture" honestly), and sealed as `pickup_load_photo_sealed` / `pickup_bol_photo_sealed`; when both are in, `pickup_sealed` seals and pickup_sealed_at is set (a still-booked load also moves to picked_up).
- UI: PickupDocsSection renders in the Load Tracker (per load) and on every All-loads card. Each slot renders ONLY a capture-only camera picker (`capture="environment"`, accept JPG/PNG) — no gallery/file control exists for these slots. The exact rule line is printed in the section. BOL capture also runs the existing `extractLoad` vision flow: extracted details prefill a confirm card and are saved to the load only when the driver taps Confirm (unchanged confirm-before-saving pattern).
- Tracker: steps are now Booked → Pickup sealed → Picked up → In transit → Arriving → Delivered. The Pickup sealed step fills only when both photos are sealed; the All-loads progress line reads Booked → Pickup sealed ✓ / Pickup photos missing → Picked up → Delivered & sealed.
- Missing-docs honesty: a load with either photo missing shows "Pickup photos missing" on the tracker, the load card, the paperwork text, and the manifest; `sealDelivery` writes pickupSealed/pickupPhotosMissing/pickupNote + both pickup fingerprints into the pod_seal payload, and the delivered views + seal banners state the note. Missing photos NEVER block the load or the delivery seal.
- Scope guard: gallery/file upload for CREATING a load (saveLoad doc + the Add-a-load photo/file control) is untouched — camera-only applies ONLY to the two pickup slots. Delete/wipe also delete the pickup blobs.
- Enforcement depth (honest): app-level camera-only — capture-only picker + source flag + server rejection. A web browser cannot cryptographically prove camera origin; the native wrap can harden it later. The UI states the app-level layers and never claims stronger proof.

## Addendum — Full Onboarding Suite (edit, 2026-10-22) — Pass 3 of 3, guided support PREMIUM
- Owner-approved build order pass 3 (ELD pass 1, Safety Academy pass 2, pickup-photo pass between; profiles/board is a later pass, not in this build). Gating (owner's rule): basic signup, joining a company with an owner-issued code, and a basic driver profile stay FREE — the plain path. The FULL support — guided checklists, white-glove fleet import, priority onboarding help — is PREMIUM (Driver Full / Owner); free users keep the three free-path steps writable and see the rest offered with locks, never forced. Billing stays staged.
- Migration 0024: `onboarding_state` (per-username: mode, dismissed, owner_intro_seen, steps_json), `onboarding_help_requests`, `fleet_trucks`. Production starts EMPTY — no rows, no pre-completed checklists for anyone; the full data wipe clears all three.
- Driver checklist (8 steps): account (verified: beta unlocked or profile row) → company (choice: verified by membership, or the user's recorded solo/individual-mode choice) → profile (verified: a display name actually saved on the social profile) → document vault intro (CDL/medical/insurance/registration/IFTA licence + renewal reminders; acknowledged after opening the real VaultPanel) → ELD connect (verified: a connected ELD row; skippable with the honest "connect later — GPS labels your miles until then" note) → Safety Academy intro (verified: first sealed completion; first core course offered is Defensive Driving; skippable) → first-trip walkthrough (acknowledged; three explainer cards — starting a trip, the 1-mile duty prompt that never changes duty status without the driver's answer, and the camera-only pickup photo + BOL rule) → Ask Meca intro (acknowledged by opening it). Solo owner-operators get the same guide in individual mode. Every step deep-links to the real feature (Plans, Chat company section, Settings profile, Driver vault, Settings ELD hub, Academy, Drive tab, Ask Meca) — no dead tutorial screens.
- Verified-step honesty enforced server-side twice: `getOnboarding` derives done-ness from the real tables at read time, and `setOnboardingStep` re-checks before recording a "done" for a verified step and refuses with the specific reason + the do-it-or-skip alternative. Skips store "skipped", render as STILL TO DO, and never count in the progress percentage.
- Owner onboarding: the setup wizard is unchanged; on FIRST entry to the Owner Portal the owner sees the Owner Portal introduction — the owner-saved copy of 2026-10-22 stored verbatim server-side (OWNER_PORTAL_INTRO in actions.ts) and rendered from the action response. `markOwnerIntroSeen` records it; it never auto-shows again and stays re-readable in the guide. Owner checklist: company setup (verified: setup.complete) → issue first driver codes (verified: ≥1 code) → ELD connect (verified/skippable) → invite drivers (verified: roster > 1; skippable) → training board tour (acknowledged; deep-links to the FleetTrainingBoard in the portal).
- White-glove fleet setup (Owner premium: premium plan + company owner role): two-phase structured paste/CSV import. `previewFleetImport` validates every line and reports per-row ready/rejected/skipped with reasons (bad username, bad role, duplicate in paste, already on roster, duplicate truck unit, load without a lane, non-numeric rate/weight, header row reported as skipped, blank line reported as skipped) and writes nothing. `commitFleetImport` re-validates only the rows the owner ticked, writes drivers (roster + both company chats, same as addCompanyDriver), trucks (`fleet_trucks` — the app's first real truck roster; before this a truck was only a plan count), and opening loads (loads rows + sealed `load_saved` with source white_glove_onboarding_import), then seals `fleet_import_completed` and returns a summary whose counts match the per-row results exactly. Nothing is silently dropped at either phase.
- Priority onboarding help: `requestOnboardingHelp` (premium) stores the request flagged by topic — owner requests carry the `owner_onboarding` flag — seals `onboarding_help_requested`, and the UI continues the same request in Ask Meca with the flag banner visible. Staged honesty printed before and after filing: in-app guided help now; a human concierge follows at store launch; nobody human is on the other end of this staging build.
- First-run behavior: a brand-new account (no onboarding_state row) auto-lands in the guide once after the beta gate; dismiss (`dismissOnboarding`) records a done state and it never auto-opens again; progress persists server-side and resumes from the Driver-tab card or the Menu entry.

## Addendum — Driver Profiles + Milestones Board (edit, 2026-10-22) — Pass 4 (final queued pass)
- Profiles: `getUserProfile` now returns the season rank + all-time-best badge (same formulas as `getDriverStats`, shared `rankSummaryFor` helper) and a MILES PANEL computed from the driver's real trip records in calendar windows (today / week from Monday / month / year), with the distinct per-trip miles sources (GPS / ELD odometer — provider) returned as labels, never blended. Privacy: `social_users.show_miles_panel` (migration 0025, default ON) is the driver's own toggle in Settings → My social profile; when off, another driver's profile response carries NO numbers at all (visible=false + the driver's-choice note) — the board and profiles never override it; the driver always sees their own numbers. Other drivers' miles/ranks live in their own sealed records on their devices in this staging build and are labelled as such — never estimated or invented.
- Milestones Board: migration 0025 adds `board_posts` + `board_likes`; production starts EMPTY (no seeded posts/likes). The board has NO customization by the owner's design law: fixed areas enforced server-side by a zod enum (Milestones • Safety Lessons • Fuel & Money Tips • Road Stories • Shout-Outs), one fixed client layout, and no board-settings action or storage anywhere. Posts are structured (title, body, area, city + state required, date/time auto-stamped at posting) and sealed (`board_post` / `board_post_updated` chain events, hash stored on the row). Location source is explicit: driver-entered, or GPS-derived via the same Nominatim reverse-geocode the app already uses (city + state only — no precise address or raw coordinates are ever stored on a post). Area detail fields: miles_reached (Milestones) + one detail line. Edits are author-only server-side (owners/admins get the refusal too) and set edited + a fresh updated_at, rendered as "updated <time>".
- Reader actions: like = one row per (post, driver), toggle removes it; share uses the app's existing share pattern with a state-level-only text (same privacy rule as Verify-Anywhere). Gift $STONER is a visible, disabled, honestly-labelled "when available" control — there is NO gift action server-side, so no transfer (real or fake) can occur; it activates only at the owner's separate on-chain go-live word, same gate as the swap. Points are never giftable (unchanged standing rule).
- DM privacy (owner's standing rule, now enforced server-side): personal DMs are between the two drivers only. `listChatGroups` no longer lists a DM group to anyone but its two members (not even its existence/preview); `listChatMessages` returns nothing for a DM to a non-member; `sendChatMessage` refuses non-member writes in a DM; `deleteChatMessage` in a DM is sender-only; `pinChatMessage` and `deleteChatGroup` refuse DM groups outright. No owner/admin/portal action reads DM content anywhere (company chats remain a separate, company-visible domain by design). The rule is stated verbatim in the board panel and in Settings → My social profile: "Your DMs are yours — nobody else can read them."
- Untouched by this pass: pricing constants ($149 Full annual / $90 add-on annual / $71.50 Owner monthly example / $760 Owner annual example, $0.55/mi), wallet/swap staging, ELD, Academy, pickup-photo, onboarding.

## Addendum — 3 Driver Tiers + Multiple Trips + Fuel Brand Selection + Fuel Coach + Net-Price Planner + Fuel Tips (edit, 2026-10-22)
- Driver tiers re-locked by the owner: **Basic FREE** (was Driver Simple — core GPS, 1 route, alerts, trip sheet at $0.55/mi, 1 open trip), **Gold Driver $9.99/mo or $99/yr** (NEW mid tier — 3 routes, fuel stops with brand selection, parking, weather, offline maps, multi-stop, loads by photo/file, document vault, multiple trips), **Diamond Driver $15/mo or $149/yr** (was Driver Full — everything in Gold plus ELD, Safety Academy + owner videos, Settlement Checker, Worth-It full, Co-Pilot, Fuel Coach, white-glove import, priority help, AI learning opt-in). Owner plan numbers UNCHANGED ($49/mo ≤3 trucks, $490/yr annual / $588 billed monthly, +$7.50/mo per extra driver/dispatch seat, $90/yr each annual; example 5 trucks + 2 seats = $71.50/mo · $760/yr). NEW: Owner-plan drivers get Diamond included. Constants added client-side: GOLD_MONTHLY 9.99 / GOLD_ANNUAL 99; no existing constant or total changed. Billing stays staged — plan choice records the plan only, no checkout.
- Migration 0027: profile.plan values renamed (simple→basic, full→diamond; server normalizes legacy values on read), trips.label + trips.queue_id, loads.trip_queue_id, new `trip_queue` table. Production starts EMPTY — no seeded trips. Queue actions (`listTripQueue`/`createTripQueue`/`updateTripQueue`/`setTripQueueStatus`/`completeTripQueue`/`deleteTripQueue`) enforce: one GPS/queue-active trip at a time (activation refused while another is active — pause or Stop & save; miles are never silently discarded, the running sheet keeps its on-device resume snapshot), Basic limited to 1 open trip (Gold prompt names the open trip), completed trips are sealed records. The GPS trip binds the active queue trip at start (label + pay rate + queue id seal into the sheet and exports; per-trip rate replaces the $0.55 default for that trip only). Save completes the queue row + seals `trip_queue_completed`.
- Fuel brand selection (replaces the Love's/Pilot toggles): an **All** chip plus one chip per brand in the data (Love's, Pilot/Flying J, TA, Petro, Sapp Bros, Kwik Trip, Casey's, Maverik, Road Ranger, Ambest, Bosselman, Truck plazas, Independent/Other). All = every stop shows (default). Tapping a brand from All narrows to just it; more taps add/remove; emptying the picks returns to All. The selection is stored as the profile's brand-off list (server-persisted, remembered), drives the fuel finder, route view, parking planner, map markers and the best-stop pick (cheapest among what's shown), and applies to every queued trip. The empty state honestly reports when the selection hides stops the road data found (raw count kept pre-filter).
- Fuel Coach (migration 0028: trips.over65_min / over70_min / idle_min, GPS-measured client-side, sealed with each trip; older trips null, never back-filled): `getFuelCoach` costs time over 65/70 with the plain-language rule (~0.1 mpg per mph over ~60; 65–70 band at ~67.5 mph, over-70 at ~72.5, against the truck's MPG setting) and stationary live-trip time at 0.8 gal/hr, ALWAYS labelled probable idle inferred from GPS, never engine data. Dollars use the driver's own average logged price (or the diesel setting, labelled). Diamond: per-trip "fuel left on the table: $X" + ONE fix + weekly rollup. Gold: weekly totals only + Diamond prompt. Basic: locked. Privacy: coach reads only the driver's own trips; NO owner-facing surface reads coach data in this pass.
- Net-Price Fuel Planner v1 (Gold+): `getFuelPriceData` returns the app's labelled IFTA rate snapshot (Oct 2026 matrix; IN/KY/VA flagged as surcharge states — surcharge earns no purchase credit, flagged not figured), driver-reported prices (≤30 days, `fuel_price_reports`, migration 0028, sealed `fuel_price_reported` at report time via `reportFuelPrice`), the driver's own fuel purchases, and the EIA weekly regional snapshot (national $6.276 / Gulf Coast $5.903 / West Coast $7.394, week of Oct 19, 2026, from the 2026-10-22 research — labelled regional context, never a station price). Client matching order: nearest driver report ≤1.2 mi → own-log station-name match → OSM map price tag (age unknown, labelled); stop states resolved from the price source or a cached Nominatim zoom-5 lookup. Every priced stop shows pump / state tax / NET; ranking is by NET, unpriced stops show "no price reported" and are never ranked by price; the trap warning fires when the cheapest pump ≠ cheapest NET. Plain-language line ships with the planner: "IFTA tax settles on where you burn fuel, not where you buy it — this ranks fuel cost only." NO live feed, partnership, referral, or discount claim added; no prices seeded.
- Fuel Tips per load (`getLoadFuelTips`, `sealLoadFuelDebrief`; migration 0029: loads.fuel_debrief): triggered by a load's pickup + drop addresses however the load was created (BOL/rate-con extraction or manual). BEFORE: lane plan lines (tax spread heads-up across the state line, surcharge flags, own-log/reported price evidence for the lane states, EIA context) — or the plain fallback "no prices reported on this lane yet — tax + driving tips only." DURING: glanceable rotating tips on the load card + tracker (next best NET stop ahead, state-line warning, coach nudge; rotates on a 3-minute bucket, no interaction required). AFTER: at delivery seal the client calls `sealLoadFuelDebrief`, which prices fuel logged while the load was open and (Diamond) the coach left-on-table + single top tip for the lane, saves it on the load record and seals `load_fuel_debrief`; Gold stores the basic cost line. Basic gets only the generic pre-trip checklist (tire pressure / idle plan / speed rule / plan stops) + the Gold prompt. Ask Meca's context now carries the active load's tips + the top net-priced stops so it can read them out.
- Untouched: wallet/swap staging, $0.55/mi default (per-trip queue rate aside), hash-chain sealing, exports' wallet wall, Owner pricing, production data (starts empty).

## Addendum — Welcome $STONER Credit + Earning Pool + $STONER Mini Chart (edit, 2026-10-22)
- Welcome $STONER credit: a NEW subscriber's FIRST paid subscription (Gold, Diamond, or Owner — any payment method) lands a $STONER credit in that subscriber's own wallet, paid from the **Earning Pool** (the 25% card/$STONER subscription slice — "$stoner recycling in app"), never minted, never a new slice. Locked splits unchanged (25/25/25/25 burn/pool/team/locker; alt-token path nets 81¢ team / 9¢ Reward Wallet / 10¢ buy+burn).
- PER-PLAN amounts (set by x, admin-editable): **first Gold plan → 1,000 $STONER · first Diamond → 10,000 $STONER · first Owner → 25,000 $STONER**. Migration 0031 adds `welcome_credit_gold_stoner` (1000) / `welcome_credit_diamond_stoner` (10000) / `welcome_credit_owner_stoner` (25000) to admin_settings. Once per account, ever — the amount is set by the first paid plan, later upgrades never top it up; refunded/charged-back first payment reverses it. Pool shortfall → queues honestly oldest-first (`processWelcomeQueue` pays only what the pool holds, never from air); wallet shows "welcome credit queued". Owner/admin actions: `setWelcomeCredit` (per plan), `fundEarningPool`, `reverseWelcomeCreditAction` — owner-gated like the referral amount; mirrored admin UI in the Beta panel next to the referral-reward amount editor. Every movement sealed (`welcome_credit_queued` / `welcome_credit_paid` with source "earning_pool" / `welcome_credit_reversed`; `earning_pool_funded`).
- Referral reward UNCHANGED: 100 $STONER to the referrer from the Reward Wallet. Referral terms show both rules side by side (referrer: amount from Reward Wallet; new subscriber: per-plan amount from Earning Pool) so the two amounts and two sources are never confused.
- Display: Plans page cards and plan-confirmation banners state the exact per-plan amount ("Your first Gold plan includes a 1,000 $STONER welcome credit to your wallet, paid from the driver earning pool"); coin-flow view reads the Earning Pool live from the ledger (balance, refills in, welcome credits paid out) with a queued-welcome note mirroring the referral queue; buyer-facing copy never shows the underlying splits.
- $STONER mini chart: the wallet's $STONER balance card shows token balance, USD equivalent, a mini price chart (24h default sparkline) and % change with honest up/down colouring; tapping opens a detail view with a larger chart + 24h/7d/30d switches. HARD HONESTY RULE: $STONER does not trade until the Nov 20 CHIP launch — `getStonerPrice` returns status "pre_launch" and the client renders a flat placeholder ("Price chart goes live at launch — $STONER doesn't trade until the Nov 20 CHIP launch"), NO drawn line, NO invented price, NO fake % change; when a live price source is ever unavailable the card shows the token balance with "price unavailable" instead of a made-up USD figure. After x's separate on-chain go-live word, the same action reads the real pool/DEX price. Points NEVER get a price chart (not a token, no cash value — label intact). Wallet history below the card lists every $STONER movement, including the welcome credit line ("Welcome credit — first subscription", amount + pool source), or the honest empty state.
- Billing mechanics are built now and activate with the existing app-store billing + on-chain go-live gates; nothing fakes a payment or a credit before then. Untouched: Owner pricing constants, referral default (100), $0.55/mi, wallet/swap staging.

## Addendum — Final credit table: signup credit + per-plan welcome credit + buy→cash-out fast lane (edit, 2026-10-22, x's final word)
- FINAL CREDIT TABLE (replaces all earlier amounts in this pass). TWO distinct one-time credits, both paid from the Earning Pool under the existing rules (not minted, splits unchanged: 25/25/25/25 burn/pool/team/locker; alt-token path nets 81¢ team / 9¢ Reward Wallet / 10¢ buy+burn; queue honestly if the pool is dry; admin-editable amounts):
  1. SIGNUP CREDIT — 100 $STONER to every new account at signup (Basic/free included). Shows in the wallet immediately but stays LOCKED for sending/swapping/selling until the account seals its first GPS trip (anti-farm: real drivers unlock it with their first drive; bot farms can't cash it out). Wallet history line: "Signup credit". Paid-subscription welcome credits are NOT locked this way.
  2. FIRST-SUBSCRIPTION WELCOME CREDIT — set by the first paid plan the account ever buys, stacking on top of the signup credit: Gold → 1,000 $STONER · Diamond → 10,000 $STONER · Fleet (Owner plan) → 100,000 $STONER. (AMOUNTS SUPERSEDED by the 2026-10-26 Option-C addendum at the end of this file — credits are now dollar-denominated: Gold $1.00 · Diamond $5.00 · Fleet $25.00, paid in $STONER at the payout price.) Once per account ever; later upgrades never top it up; reverses on refund/chargeback of that first payment; any payment method.
- QUEUE ORDER: subscription welcome credits pay BEFORE signup credits when the pool can't cover both (`processWelcomeQueue` sorts welcome-first, oldest-first within each kind).
- Data: migration 0032 adds `welcome_credits.kind` (welcome|signup), `locked` (boolean), `plan` (which first paid plan set the welcome amount), and `admin_settings.signup_credit_stoner` (100); it also moves the carried-over Owner default 25000 → 100000 where still at the old default. `maybeAwardSignupCredit` is idempotent (one per account ever) and is called from beta-code redeem, onboarding dismiss, and plan choice. `maybeUnlockSignupCredit` runs inside `saveTrip` — sealing a GPS trip unlocks the signup credit and seals `signup_credit_unlocked`. Ledger kinds: `welcome_payout` / `signup_payout`; chain events: `signup_credit_queued|paid`, `welcome_credit_queued|paid|reversed`. `reverseWelcomeCredit` touches only the welcome kind. Owner/admin: `setSignupCredit`, `setWelcomeCredit` (per plan), `fundEarningPool`, `reverseWelcomeCreditAction` — all owner-gated, with mirrored editors in the Beta panel.
- Display: signup-flow beta gate + Plans cards + plan-confirmation banners state the numbers plainly ("Sign up free: 100 $STONER in your wallet (unlocks for sending/swap when you seal your first trip)" / "First Gold plan: +1,000 $STONER welcome credit" / "First Diamond plan: +10,000" / "First Fleet plan: +100,000", each noting "paid from the driver earning pool"). Wallet $STONER card + movements list show both credits with their lock/queue state. Referral terms keep the referral reward (100 $STONER to the referrer from the Reward Wallet) displayed separately with its source named, plus the signup credit as its own separate line. Coin-flow reads both credit kinds live from the ledger (paid out / queued, welcome before signup) and names the per-plan settings.
- BUY → CASH OUT, FAST LANE (subscribers). BUY: the existing card buy of $STONER (licensed-provider hosted fields; card numbers never touch the app) stays the entry; at buy time the app now offers the one-time KYC right there — "Verify once now so your cash-out is instant later" — because KYC at first cash-out is already required; front-loading it is what makes a later cash-out fast. Bought $STONER lands in the wallet when the provider confirms. CASH OUT has TWO lanes, chosen before confirming, with amount / provider fee / destination / ETA shown pre-confirm and a live status tracker after (requested → provider review → sent → arrived): (1) Instant — debit card or Cash App, target minutes, paid subscribers (Gold/Diamond/Fleet) only; the provider's instant fee is itemized before confirm, never hidden or silently marked up. (2) Standard — bank ACH, 1–3 business days, honestly labelled, lowest/no fee, everyone including Basic (Basic sees Instant locked with a Gold upgrade prompt).
- HONEST HOLDS: licensed providers can put a fraud hold on a FIRST card purchase. Migration 0033 adds `cashout_holds` (username, amount, plain-words reason, available_at) + `profile.kyc_status` (not_started|in_review|verified). When a hold applies, the wallet $STONER card and the cash-out screen show the held amount, the reason in plain words ("your card provider holds first purchases — available [date]"), and a live countdown; the app never promises instant when the provider hasn't released the funds. `getCashoutState` returns KYC status, instant eligibility (plan-based), holds, and locked signup amount. Owner/provider-webhook actions: `recordProviderHold`, `setKycStatus` (owner-gated in staging; at go-live the provider's webhook posts both automatically) with Beta-panel controls.
- Existing locks untouched: the signup 100 stays locked until the first sealed trip (excluded from cash-out, noted in the cash-out screen); welcome credits and earnings cash out under the same lanes once in the wallet. Points never cash out (not a token — label intact). Everything runs through licensed providers only (the app never holds fiat or card data) and stays staged OFF until the provider agreements + x's separate on-chain go-live approval: buy, KYC, and cash-out confirms are present but disabled with honest "activates at go-live" labels — no fake instant payouts in staging. The go-live send/swap/sell path must exclude locked signup $STONER (helper data is in `getCashoutState.signupLockedStoner`).

## Addendum — Weekly Driver Giveaway (edit, 2026-10-22, x chose BOTH giveaways)
- WEEKLY DRIVER GIVEAWAY (in-app; the launch giveaway itself is marketing ops, handled outside the app). Entries are AUTOMATIC from GPS-verified activity that week — sealed trip = 1 entry, on-time delivery seal = +1 bonus, accepted road intel = +1 bonus (hooked into `saveTrip`, the on-time branch of `sealDelivery`, and `confirmIntelReport`). No purchase needed; paid plans never buy entries. One account's entries cap per week (admin setting `giveaway_weekly_cap`, default 10) so nobody farms the draw.
- Weekly prize ladder (target): 10 × 100 + 2 × 1,000 + 1 × 10,000 $STONER (13,000/week), funded from the REWARD WALLET (the established home of team-run $STONER rewards). HONEST FUNDING RULE: at week start the prize list LOCKS from the wallet's actual balance (migration 0034: `giveaway_locks`; sealed `giveaway_prizes_locked`) — the target ladder fills from the top prize down while the balance covers it, so a short week locks a smaller list that shows as-is BEFORE the draw. Never an unfunded prize, no IOUs.
- Draw at week end (`runGiveawayDraw`, owner-gated; go-live path `executeGiveawayDraw`): one prize per account, largest prize first, weighted by entry count, each winner removed from the pool; pays from the Reward Wallet only what it holds. Winners posted on the giveaway page AND the Milestones Board Shout-Outs area with amounts (public receipts, system post with empty city/state — no location), and paid into winners' in-app wallets. ANTI-DUMP PAYOUT DEFAULT: prizes of 1,000 or less land liquid at the draw; the 10,000 prize VESTS (`giveaway_vesting`: vestPct % at the draw, then the same % monthly until paid — default 25, admin-editable via `setGiveawaySettings`; the schedule shows in the rules BEFORE anyone is entered). Due tranches pay via `payGiveawayVesting` (owner-gated, oldest first, only what the wallet holds). Chain events: `giveaway_entry_added`, `giveaway_prizes_locked`, `giveaway_drawn`, `giveaway_prize_paid`, `giveaway_vesting_paid`, `giveaway_winners_posted`. Coin-flow adds "Weekly giveaway paid out" to the Reward Wallet rows.
- Timing gates: the giveaway activates only with the Nov 20 token launch + x's on-chain go-live approval (`GIVEAWAY_LIVE` flag, false in staging). Pre-launch the giveaway page (Wallet tab, "🎁 Weekly driver giveaway") shows "Weekly giveaway starts at launch" + the full rules, the driver's accruing entries, the locked prize list, and past draws (empty) — no fake draws, no fake winners, ever. The draw and vesting-payout actions refuse with the launch message until the gate flips.
- The giveaway page also carries the LAUNCH GIVEAWAY terms summary (40×100 + 8×1,000 + 2×10,000 = 32,000 $STONER, 100–10,000 prizes, winners drawn + posted publicly with amounts on launch day Nov 20, paid into STONEMILES wallets, funded from the team share — never the Earning Pool, never minted; entries from signups + first sealed trips; 10,000 prizes vest 25% at payout + 25%/mo ×3).

## Addendum — Welcome-credit vesting: "Vest the big ones" (edit, 2026-10-22, x's final call)
- VESTING — FINAL RULES (x verbatim: "Vest the big ones + add lock-for-perks later"). Threshold: welcome credits of 10,000 $STONER or more vest; anything smaller is liquid on landing (Gold 1,000 = liquid; signup 100 = liquid once its first-sealed-trip lock clears; giveaway 100/1,000 prizes = liquid).
  - Diamond welcome credit (10,000): 2,500 at first subscription (liquid, paid through the normal welcome queue), then 1,250/month × 6. (Schedule thinned 2026-10-26. TOKEN AMOUNTS SUPERSEDED by the Option-C addendum at the end of this file — the credit is now $5.00 of value with a 25% head + 6 monthly tranches at the payout price; the ×6 schedule stands.)
  - Fleet/Owner welcome credit (100,000): 25,000 at first subscription, then 6,250/month × 12. (Schedule thinned 2026-10-26. TOKEN AMOUNTS SUPERSEDED by the Option-C addendum at the end of this file — the credit is now $25.00 of value with a 25% head + 12 monthly tranches at the payout price; the ×12 schedule stands.)
  - Both vest only while the subscription stays active: the account's current plan must be at least the credit's tier (Diamond credit stays active on Diamond OR Owner, since Owner-plan drivers get Diamond included; Owner credit needs Owner). If the plan lapses, pending tranches flip to "returned" — they were never disbursed, so the pool balance does not move (a zero-amount memorial ledger row + the sealed event keep the paper trail) — and the wallet says plainly "vesting stopped — subscription ended; X $STONER returned to the driver earning pool".
  - Giveaway 10,000 prizes (launch + weekly): 25% at payout, then 25%/month × 3 — TIME-ONLY vesting (a winner keeps the schedule even if they stop using the app). Unchanged; kept as built.
- Admin-editable amounts keep the same vesting SHAPE (percentages, not fixed coins): 25% liquid head + the remainder in 6 (Diamond) or 12 (Owner) equal monthly tranches. If an admin edits an amount below the 10,000 threshold, the credit lands liquid (no vesting).
- Data: migration 0035 adds `welcome_vesting` (username, welcome_credit_id, plan, tranche_no, total_tranches, amount_stoner, due_at, status pending|paid|returned, paid_at, note). The `welcome_credits` row holds the liquid head (25%) and keeps queuing/paying through the existing `processWelcomeQueue` (welcome-before-signup order unchanged). `processWelcomeVesting` settles due tranches: head must be paid first (no leapfrogging), due date passed, plan still active, pool covers the tranche (pool-short tranches stay pending — queued honestly, never paid from air). It runs opportunistically inside `getWelcomeCredit` (so the wallet is always fresh) and explicitly via the owner-gated `payWelcomeVesting` action. Ledger kinds: `welcome_vesting_payout` (negative, tranche paid) / `welcome_vesting_returned` (0-amount memorial, lapse). Chain events: `welcome_vesting_paid`, `welcome_vesting_returned` (lapse); `welcome_credit_queued` now carries headStoner/vested/monthlyTranches; `welcome_credit_reversed` carries reversedVestingTranches. `reverseWelcomeCredit` reverses paid tranches back into the pool and cancels pending ones with note "reversed — first payment refunded/charged back".
- WALLET DISPLAY (three numbers, never blended): `getWelcomeCredit` returns `balance { available, vesting, locked, total }` + a `vesting` object (per-tranche status/due dates, nextUnlockAt + nextAmount, stopped flag + stoppedNote) + per-plan `vestMonthlyTranches` (0 = liquid). The $STONER card shows available as the headline number with an available/vesting/total strip (next unlock date under vesting) and locked signup credit shown separately; the detail view lists every tranche with due date + paid/pending/returned status. Available = paid head + paid tranches + paid-and-unlocked signup credit; vesting = pending tranches; locked = paid-but-still-locked signup credit; total = the sum — every coin visible, always.
- COPY (amounts SUPERSEDED by Option C — see the addendum at the end of this file; schedules as amended stand): Plans cards + plan-confirmation banners state each schedule in plain numbers BEFORE purchase ("Diamond includes 10,000 $STONER welcome credit: 2,500 now, then 1,250 a month for 6 months while your plan stays active" — derived from the admin amounts via `vestLineFor`). Referral terms and the coin-flow carry the same schedule. Giveaway rules already stated their schedule in plain numbers; kept. New FAQ items: "Do the big $STONER welcome credits vest?" and "Can I lock $STONER for perks?".
- LOCK-FOR-PERKS: NOT built. x approved the direction as a post-launch roadmap item — the FAQ lists it as "coming later" with no promised perks, rates, or mechanic.
