## Addendum — SM Points locked system (edit, 2026-10-23, x: "Lock it — build it into STONEMILES"; supersedes the earlier same-night HOLD and every "points never convert / no cash value" line above)
- DAILY TABLE (server-enforced, per-task once-per-day caps + 500/day hard cap computed from the ledger in `awardDailyTask`; days are driver-local America/Chicago): Morning Briefing check-in +25 (awarded server-side when the unlocked Co-Pilot briefing loads) · Pre-trip DVIR signed +50 (`saveDvir`, pretrip only) · Trip driven and sealed +100 (`saveTrip`) · On-time POD seal +150 (`sealDelivery`, geofence-verified + appointment-grace) · Clean driving day +75 (lazy in `getPoints`: completed local day with ≥1 sealed trip and zero overspeed chain events, last 14 days) · Road intel confirmed +50, first confirmed intel of the day only (`confirmIntelReport`) · Fuel stop logged +25 (`logFuelPurchase`) · Post-Trip Debrief reviewed +25 (`recordDebriefReview`, called when the driver opens the debrief). A perfect day = exactly 500 by construction.
- WEEKLY BONUS: clean-week streak +200 (was +30), Mon–Sun driver-local weeks, OUTSIDE the daily cap. Season rank milestones unchanged (Bronze 20 · Silver 40 · Gold 75 · Platinum 120 · Diamond 200).
- HARDENING: `sealDelivery` takes the client's geocoded delivery spot and the SERVER computes the seal-to-delivery distance (points only when ≤3 mi AND on-time; the seal itself never blocks, and the chain payload records geofence verified/outside/unavailable + miles). `confirmIntelReport` takes the driver's GPS and the server enforces age ≥10 min + distance ≤0.25 mi before confirming. `voidPodSeal` claws back the exact points that seal awarded (negative ledger row keyed to the award, stamped at the award's instant; the daily task re-opens and can be re-earned on a re-seal). Daily caps are enforced in the ledger write path, not the UI.
- CONVERSION (SUPERSEDED — Option C addendum 2026-10-26 at the end of this file replaces the token-fixed rate with a dollar anchor paid at the payout price; that anchor itself was raised $0.20 → $0.25 by the REWARDS REWORK addendum 2026-10-26, also at the end of this file): 1,000 SM Points = 100 $STONER · minimum balance 10,000 points · 1,000-point blocks · monthly cap 100,000 points per driver (paid + queued count) · pays from the EARNING POOL only via `points_conversions` (migration 0039) — pool-short requests stay "queued" and show as queued; points leave the ledger only at the moment a conversion actually pays (`processPointsConversionQueue`, same honest-queue discipline as welcome credits). Points remain non-transferable: never sendable or swappable between users (the owner→driver company gift mechanic is unchanged and separate).
- ACTIVATION GATE: execution is OFF via `admin_settings.points_conversion_live` (migration 0039, default 0) until the Nov 20 launch + x's separate on-chain go-live word — while gated, `requestPointsConversion` refuses with the gate stated and moves nothing (no fake conversions). The Points panel shows the locked terms, the gate, monthly usage, pool balance, and conversion history (queued shown as QUEUED). All in-app copy that said points can never convert / have no cash value was rewritten to the locked terms (Points panel, wallet points row, send/cash-out/swap walls, dictionary, FAQ, disclaimer, coin-flow earning-pool line, intel panel).
- Production data stays empty; no rows seeded. All existing tiers, splits, credits, vesting, giveaway, locker, and CHIP panel behavior preserved.

## Addendum — pay basis: CPM or % of load pay (edit, 2026-10-24)
- Owner ask: "Add cpm or %" — a company driver chooses HOW the company pays them: CPM ($/mi, built 0042) OR a percentage of the load's pay. Migration 0043: profile.pay_basis ('cpm'|'percent', default 'cpm') + profile.driver_pay_pct; trip_queue.pay_basis + pay_percent; trips.pay_basis + pay_percent + load_gross (snapshots). Both numbers stay saved side by side — switching basis never erases the other.
- Percent math runs ONLY off a load's real gross (rate + additional charges — the same total the load card shows). The driver's cut = round(gross × pct)/100, exact cents. On a trip sheet the cut is split across states by miles, per-state amounts rounded to cents with the remainder on the biggest state so states sum to the exact cut. Route cards: the cut is identical on every option (options differ on fuel/time/safety); Load Tracker shows cut-so-far pro-rated by route progress.
- No gross on file (or no % set) → NO figure is invented: route pay shows 0 with the reason on the card, the live sheet shows "cut not figured" with the fix, and the saved sheet stamps the basis + % with loadGross null and the plain statement that no percent pay was figured. Additional charges on the trip still add on top in all bases.
- Settlement Checker is basis-aware: for a percent driver every load is checked against their % of the sealed gross (rate + charges), the report header states the basis, and missing/short amounts are the cut amounts. CPM behavior unchanged.
- Exports (IFTA text + spreadsheet) stamp the basis per trip: CPM rows keep $/mi; percent rows show "% of load pay $gross" (or "gross not on file") and the pay-basis line. Sheets saved before 0043 keep their CPM stamps untouched — nothing back-filled.
- Tier rule unchanged: pay basis works on every tier, Basic included (it's the driver's own pay record). Queue trips inherit the Settings basis/% at create; each trip can override basis + value inline (💰 Pay editor) or in the create form.

## Addendum — Rewards & House Rules update (edit, 2026-10-25, x locked)

- SIGNUP POINTS: a new free (Basic) account earns 100 SM Points once ever (source "signup", refKey `signup-points:<user>`), awarded by `maybeAwardSignupPoints` from the beta-gate redeem, onboarding dismiss, and plan-choice backstop. There is NO $STONER signup credit anymore.
- SUBSCRIPTION POINTS: a NEW paid subscription earns 1,000 SM Points (source "subscription") and EVERY settled renewal earns another 1,000 (source "renewal"), through the single settled-payment path `recordSubscriptionRewards` (migration 0044: `subscription_events`, payment_ref unique = idempotency). Points land ONLY when settled=true (House Rule 2). New-subscription points/counting pay once per account ever (anti-farm); renewals always pay. Promo/discounted payments earn points at the value actually paid (paid/list ratio) and a discounted FIRST subscription earns NO welcome credit (House Rule 5). New action `recordSubscriptionEvent` is the billing-path entry (go-live webhook; owner-recorded in staging) — kind=new also runs the referral reward + (undiscounted) welcome credit.
- YEARLY SUBSCRIBER CREDIT (replaces the signup credit; AMOUNT SUPERSEDED by the 2026-10-26 Option-C addendum — now $1.00 of value paid in $STONER at the payout price): the 100 $STONER goes ONLY to annual (yearly) subscribers — yearly gets BOTH the 1,000 points AND the 100 $STONER; monthly gets points only. Same machinery as before: `welcome_credits.kind` "signup" → "yearly" (migration 0044 renames `admin_settings.signup_credit_stoner` → `yearly_credit_stoner`), Earning Pool funded, welcome-before-yearly queue order, LOCKED until the first sealed GPS trip (`maybeUnlockYearlyCredit` in saveTrip). Admin action renamed `setYearlyCredit`; reads renamed (`getWelcomeCredit.yearly/yearlyAmountSetting`, `getCoinFlow.yearlyPaidOut/yearlyQueued*/yearlyCreditStoner`, `getCashoutState.yearlyLockedStoner`). REFERRAL RECONCILE: the referrer's 100 $STONER on the referred user's first paid subscription is UNCHANGED; the referred user's own 100 $STONER now comes only via their own yearly subscription.
- SUBSCRIBER GIVEAWAY (replaces the weekly activity giveaway ENTIRELY — GPS-activity entries, the 13,000/week ladder, week keys, `maybeAddGiveawayEntry`, `lockWeekPrizes`, and `setGiveawaySettings` are all removed): a cycle (migration 0044: `giveaway_cycles` + `giveaway_cycle_entries`) fills at 5 NEW paid Gold + 10 NEW paid Diamond subscriptions counted ONLY from the settled-subscription path; one entry per subscriber per cycle. One draw pays 100,000 $STONER from the Reward Wallet — 25,000 to a random Gold subscriber, 75,000 to a random Diamond subscriber — both vesting 25% at payout + 25%/month × 3 in the existing `giveaway_vesting` machinery. The draw runs ONLY when the wallet holds the full pot; otherwise the cycle is publicly "queued — waiting on pool balance" (`refreshGiveawayCycleStatus`). Winners are sealed, posted to the Board Shout-Outs WITH the payout chart, and `getGiveaway` returns cycle progress, pot split, queue state, and past draws with per-winner tranche charts. `GIVEAWAY_LIVE` stays FALSE — draws/payouts refuse until Nov 20 + owner go-live.
- HOUSE RULES (all 9, stated in a new House Rules section in the Wallet/$STONER & Rewards area and enforced in logic): (1) `reverseSubscriptionRewards` (owner action `reverseWelcomeCreditAction`) claws back welcome credit + vesting, the yearly credit, subscription/renewal points (source "clawback"), and giveaway-cycle counts on refund/chargeback; (2) settled-only points (above); (3) one rewards account per verified driver — all awards once-per-account idempotent, device + payment fingerprint attaches at go-live billing, disputes settle by the sealed hash chain (stated in copy); (4) `isCompedFleetDriver` (company role "driver") — fleet-included Diamond seats earn NO points, NO yearly credit, NO giveaway entries/counts (features only), enforced in setPlan + recordSubscriptionRewards; (5) promo rule (above); (6) all pools queue publicly (conversion, yearly credit, giveaway) — queue states user-visible; (7) ANNUAL FIRST — Plans sheet leads with yearly pricing everywhere (cards, comparison grid, Owner sizing, FAQs, pitch copy) with a Yearly/Monthly toggle whose choice feeds `setPlan.billingPeriod`; (8) team no-sell window (first 30 days post-genesis, giveaway payouts + pool funding only) stated in Coin Flow; (9) yearly price lock stated on the Plans sheet/cards ("rate frozen for your full year").
- PRIVACY RULE: split/funding percentages removed from EVERY user-facing surface — the Coin Flow "how each payment flows" panel, bucket notes, locker deposit history, the Beta-panel funding rule, the referral FAQ, and the CHIP burn line now describe the flow in plain language with NO percentages (amounts, prices, prize/vesting numbers unchanged). Internal ledger notes written by owner fund actions no longer carry percentages either.
- SWAP FEE 1% (x, 2026-10-25): raised to 3% mid-build, then REVERTED to 1% by x the same day ("Change swap fee back keep insta pay on"). Every in-app swap fee statement now says 1% (quote panel label, fee math `amt * 0.01`, "Into the swap (99%)" — the `intoSwap` calc already used `amt * 0.99` — swap explainer copy, one-tap subscription path copy, Coin Flow note, Swap section header comment). Swap stays staged OFF behind the same gates. Instant Pay / instant cash-out is untouched by this revert: its ≤1% advance target stays exactly as specced.
- GATES UNCHANGED: `GIVEAWAY_LIVE` false, `pointsConversionLive` still owner-gated off, billing still staged (no charges), no $STONER movement activated, production data still empty (migration 0044 is schema + a kind rename that touches zero rows in production).

## Addendum — Referral SM Points (edit, 2026-10-25, x locked)

- REFERRAL POINTS: on the same settled-first-subscription event that already pays the referrer 100 $STONER from the Reward Wallet (`maybeAwardReferralReward`), the referrer now ALSO earns +250 SM Points (`REFERRAL_POINTS = 250`, constant in `server/src/actions.ts`). Recorded via `awardPoints` in the referrer's `points_log` with source `"referral"`, refKey `referral-points:<referredUsername>`, and a reason naming the referred driver and the settled first subscription. Idempotent via the refKey and via the existing one-reward-per-referred-user guard. No new table or migration — points reuse the existing ledger.
- SETTLED-ONLY (House Rule 2): `maybeAwardReferralReward` is invoked only for settled first subscriptions (`recordSubscriptionEvent` gates on `settled=true` + `kind=new`; `setPlan` stands in for a settled payment in staging). An unsettled, attempted, or failed payment awards neither the $STONER nor the points.
- UNCAPPED: referral rewards are explicitly uncapped — no per-referrer, per-month, or lifetime cap exists in code or copy; every settled referred first subscription pays both halves. The Reward Wallet cage rule (queue publicly when short) remains the only brake, and it applies to the $STONER half only; points land on settlement.
- CLAWBACK (House Rule 1): `reverseSubscriptionRewards` (owner action `reverseWelcomeCreditAction`) now also reverses the referral points when the referred driver's subscription is reversed — a −250 row on the REFERRER's points ledger via `awardPoints` with source `"clawback"`, refKey `clawback-referral-points:<referredUsername>`, and a reason referencing the referral award for that driver's settled first subscription, plus a sealed `referral_points_clawed_back` chain event. Idempotent; a no-op when no referral award landed.
- READS/UI: `getReferral` now returns `referralPointsSetting` (250), `referralPointsEarnedTotal` (net referral points for the referrer), and per-referral `pointsAmount` / `pointsClawedBack`. Referral copy updated everywhere it appears (Wallet Referrals section + stats + per-driver lines, Settings referral block, Coin Flow referral note, Beta-panel admin note, referral FAQ, House Rules 1–2) to read 100 $STONER + 250 SM Points, uncapped, points on settled payment, reversing on refund.
- GATES UNCHANGED: no activation — production data stays empty, `pointsConversionLive` stays false, `GIVEAWAY_LIVE` stays false, billing/swap remain staged off.

## Addendum — referral levels + level badges (edit, 2026-10-25, locked by x)

- LEVELS on ACTIVE referrals (settled first sub, not refunded; cancel after real paid months still counts; only refund/chargeback removes one): Spotter 1–4 → 250 pts/referral · Dispatcher 5+ → 350 pts + one-time 1,000 milestone at 5 · Fleet Builder 15+ → 500 pts + one-time 2,500 at 15 · Road Captain 40+ → 750 pts + one-time 5,000 at 40 + badge + featured profile slot on the STONEMILES Board. The 100 $STONER from the Reward Wallet stays FLAT at every level. Referral rewards remain explicitly UNCAPPED — the Reward Wallet public queue is the only brake.
- RATE TIMING: the rate is the referrer's level at the moment the recruit's sub settles, with the settling referral itself counting toward the active total — the 5th active referral pays 350, the 15th pays 500, the 40th pays 750, and the milestone lands on the same event. Per-referral awards keep source `"referral"` (refKey `referral-points:<referred>`); milestones use the distinct source `"referral_milestone"` (refKey `referral-milestone:<referrer>:<levelKey>`).
- MILESTONE STATE (migration 0045, `referral_milestones`): one row per (referrer, level) with threshold, bonus, `paid` flag + `paidAt`, `reversed` flag + `reversedAt`. `paid` is permanent — it NEVER resets — so a milestone pays once per level, ever.
- CLAWBACK (House Rule 1, extended): a refund reverses that referral's points at the rate ACTUALLY PAID (read from the original award row, −actual via source `"clawback"`, refKey `clawback-referral-points:<referred>`). If the refund drops the active count below a milestone threshold, that milestone bonus is reversed too (−bonus, refKey `clawback-referral-milestone:<referrer>:<levelKey>`, state `reversed=true`) BUT its `paid` flag STAYS set — a later re-cross never re-pays (x's locked choice). Sealed events: `referral_milestone_paid`, `referral_milestone_clawed_back` (payload `milestonePaidFlagKept: true`).
- BADGES (x, 2026-10-25): every level above Spotter has its OWN distinct badge — 🎧 Dispatcher (5), 🏗️ Fleet Builder (15), 👑 Road Captain (40, gold) — shown on the driver's public profile and next to their name on the STONEMILES Board. SPOTTER GETS NO BADGE (entry level). Shared client helper `client/src/referralLevels.ts` (`referralBadgeFor`, null for Spotter). Board featured slot: `listBoardPosts` returns `featuredCaptains` (all referrers with 40+ active, top 10, display name resolved from profile/social/board) and the Board renders a "Featured — Road Captains" strip only when at least one exists.
- READS/UI: `getReferral` returns activeReferralCount, levelKey/levelName/levelRate, nextReferralPoints, next level + remaining count, isRoadCaptain, milestonePointsEarnedTotal, the full `referralLevels` table (with per-level milestonePaid/milestoneReversed), and per-referral `pointsAmount` now carries the exact rate paid. `getUserProfile` returns referralLevelName/Key/ActiveCount/isRoadCaptain for any driver. Wallet Referrals section shows the level panel + full levels table (Spotter row marked "no badge"); Settings referral block shows level, badge, progress, and the levels line; FAQ + House Rule 1 + Coin Flow copy updated to the leveled rates and the actual-rate clawback. Production data stays empty; points conversion and giveaway gates untouched (live:false).

## Addendum — Parking & fuel assistance is a Diamond standard (edit, 2026-10-25, x locked)

- DECISION: "Parking and fuel assistance are a DIAMOND STANDARD; Gold keeps basics." DIAMOND (Diamond Driver + Owner — Owner-plan included Diamond seats get these features; the rewards exclusions for comped fleet seats are unchanged) carries the FULL assistance as standard. GOLD keeps only basics. BASIC unchanged (core GPS/alerts/trip sheet + generic pre-trip checklist).
- BEFORE → AFTER, per feature:
  - Net-Price Fuel Planner: was Gold+ → now DIAMOND ONLY. Server: `getFuelPriceData` returns the IFTA rate table (the NET engine) only to Diamond; Gold still receives its own fuel-log purchases + driver-reported prices (age + source) for the basic stops list, with `rates: []` so no NET price can be figured or ranked. Client: the planner section, the Drive-tab best-NET card, per-stop NET figures, and Ask Meca's net-stop context now render only on Diamond; Gold sees the Diamond lock + the basic stops list.
  - Per-load lane fuel plan (BEFORE/DURING): was Gold+ → DIAMOND full. `getLoadFuelTips`: Diamond = full lane plan as before; Gold = basic per-load pre-trip tips only (stops list + toggles, speed rule); Basic = generic checklist only. `after` is returned to Diamond only.
  - After-load debrief: was Gold (basic cost line) / Diamond (full) → DIAMOND ONLY. `sealLoadFuelDebrief` now refuses off-Diamond (`locked: true`); the Gold cost-line branch is removed.
  - Fuel Coach: UNCHANGED split, verified — Basic locked, Gold basic weekly speed/idle summary only (no per-trip debrief, no fix), Diamond full (per-trip debrief + weekly rollup with the fix). This already matched the decision.
  - Fuel stops along route (list + map) and brand toggles (Love's, Pilot/Flying J, all brands): Gold basics KEPT, unchanged. Prices on Gold come only from the driver's own fuel log + labelled reported prices (age + source), never presented as live — unchanged honesty rules.
  - Parking: was an ungated HOS planner for every tier → tiered in `ParkingSheet`. DIAMOND: full — HOS-clock reachability ranking, end-of-day threshold warning banner (also gated on the Drive tab), parking intel candidates, and saved parking spots (device-local `tgps-saved-parking`, save/remove + route). GOLD: basic pins/list — stops + rest areas along the route, nearest first, availability only where map data tags it; no HOS ranking, no threshold control, no intel, no saved spots. BASIC: locked sheet with the Gold/Diamond upgrade path. The scale & parking intel strip (`WeighIntelStrip`) shows parking intel kinds only on Diamond; weigh-station intel is unchanged for all tiers. General road-intel reporting/points (Drive tab) is not parking assistance and is unchanged.
- PRICING COPY (annual-first, House Rule 7; locked prices unchanged — Gold $99/yr or $9.99/mo, Diamond $199/yr or $19.99/mo, Fleet/Owner $499/yr or $49.99/mo + $9.99/mo or $99/yr per extra seat): Plans intro, recorded-plan banners, tier cards, comparison grid, and FAQs updated. Diamond copy lists "Parking & fuel assistance — full" and names the Fuel Coach, the Net-Price Fuel Planner, and HOS-aware parking planning. Gold copy reads "Fuel stops & parking — basic." Every app claim that the Net-Price Fuel Planner or full parking/fuel assistance is Gold or Gold+ was removed (planner lock card, Gold card's old "Parking planner — reachable parking before your clock runs out" line, comparison grid rows, FAQs, menu and Ask Meca lines).
- WEEKLY GIVEAWAY GREP (2026-10-25): `grep -ri "weekly giveaway" client/src server/src` returns ZERO hits — app-facing copy already uses "subscriber giveaway" throughout (the giveaway section, admin rows, Coin Flow). The only remaining "weekly giveaway" strings in the repo are inside this DATA-PLAN's own dated 2026-10-22 historical addendum, superseded in full by the Subscriber Giveaway addendum above; historical addenda are left as written.
- GATES UNCHANGED: no activation — production data stays empty, billing/swap/giveaway gates untouched, nothing charged or turned live.

## Addendum — perf timing capture + GPS always-on (edit, 2026-10-25, x ordered)

- PERF TIMING CAPTURE (first-party only, NO third-party trackers, no separate data collector): the app records its own measured timings in its own records so routing/GPS thresholds tune from x's real Nov 10/11 road-test drives.
  - `perf_events` (migration 0046): one row per event — `kind` = `reroute` (elapsed ms from reroute fire to corrected-route publish, ok flag, small JSON detail: off-route distance) or `route_request` (Valhalla request duration for the published primary, ok flag, detail: fast/full + option count). Written via `recordPerfEvent`, read back only by the same account via `listPerfEvents`. No location is ever stored in these rows.
  - Per-trip GPS fix quality: the client fix filter's tallies during a trip (accepted fixes, rejections by kind — accuracy/jump/time — and the accuracy distribution in buckets ≤10/≤25/≤50/≤65/>65 m + no-reading) ride `saveTrip` and seal onto the trips row as `fix_quality` JSON (migration 0046), exactly like the existing pace/Fuel Coach aggregates. Null on older sheets — never back-filled or estimated.
  - SURFACE: Settings → Driving → "App performance" panel (counts + averages, latest 10 timings, JSON export via the standard step-up-gated ExportBar); each trip sheet shows its own "GPS fix quality" line under Pace. No new analytics dashboard.
- GPS ON/OFF BUTTON REMOVED (x's order): the manual GPS toggle is gone from the interface — the top-bar chip is now a read-only status (GPS LIVE / GPS WEAK / GPS STARTING), the Drive-tab Start/Stop GPS buttons and the planner Start GPS buttons are removed, and `stopGps` no longer exists. GPS starts automatically when the app opens and stays on while the app is in use; the foreground re-acquire on visibility return is unchanged.
- PRIVACY UNCHANGED: the browser location-permission flow is untouched; on-duty-only location sharing, raw-GPS retention (7/30/90 days), and opt-in learning (off by default) are exactly as before. This change removes the manual switch only — it does not expand what is collected or when. Prices, tiers, rewards, and routing thresholds untouched.

## Addendum — Fleet Load Board + Owner Console (edit, 2026-10-25, x approved)

- FLEET LOAD BOARD (migration 0047, `board_loads`): company-private load board. Owner/Dispatch POST a load by uploading a rate confirmation or BOL — the existing `extractLoad` document reading pre-fills lane (pickup/delivery city+state), pay, and pickup/delivery windows; the poster reviews/edits every field before it goes live (`postBoardLoad` / `updateBoardLoad`, open loads only). Manual entry without a document works the same. The uploaded doc is stored as a blob with a SHA-256 fingerprint on the board row (`docSha256`), shared with the driver load a claim/assign creates.
- VISIBILITY: every board read filters on the caller's own companyId (`getLoadBoard`). Drivers see all OPEN loads + their own taken loads; Owner/Dispatch see the whole company board. A board id from another company answers exactly like a load that doesn't exist ("That load isn't on your company's board.") — including its document (`getBoardLoadDoc` returns nulls). Solo (non-company) drivers get `inCompany:false`, no rows, and the app never mounts a board tab for them.
- CLAIM (atomic, first-claim-wins): `claimBoardLoad` (driver or owner seat; dispatch refused — dispatch assigns instead) flips the row with a single conditional UPDATE … WHERE status='open'; a concurrent loser re-reads and is told who got it first. ASSIGN: `assignBoardLoad` (Owner/Dispatch) sends an open load to a specific same-company member (never dispatch). Both paths run `createLoadFromBoard`: a real `loads` row at status "booked" (Load Tracker starts at Booked) + a `trip_queue` trip with the driver's pay profile inherited, sealed as `board_load_claimed` / `board_load_assigned` (+ `load_saved`, `doc_sealed`, `trip_queue_created`). Downstream flow (routing, pickup proof, geofence POD seal) is the unchanged solo load flow.
- UNASSIGN/REPOST: `unassignBoardLoad` (Owner/Dispatch) only while the linked load is still Booked with no pickup proof and no POD seal — the board row itself carries the plain-language blocked reason otherwise (`canUnassign` / `unassignBlockedReason` in the read shape). Unassign deletes the created load + trip (sealed `load_deleted` + `board_load_unassigned`) and reopens the board row exactly as posted. `withdrawBoardLoad` removes an OPEN post (doc blob deleted only when no load shares it). Full data wipe now also clears board rows + their doc blobs.
- GATING: post/edit/assign/unassign/withdraw = Owner or Dispatch seat in that company; claim = driver (or owner) seat in that company. Board access rides the Owner plan (company drivers' included Diamond). NO price, tier, rewards, or points changes; the solo load flow is untouched.
- OWNER CONSOLE (same app, same login, same data — NOT a separate portal): when an Owner/Dispatch-seat account opens the app on a wide viewport (≥1024px), a full-viewport console layer replaces the layout: persistent left sidebar (Dashboard · Fleet Map · Load Board · Drivers · Hours · Settlements · Documents · Company Chat · Settings) + wide content. Phone/narrow layouts and driver-role accounts never see it; "Standard view" leaves the console for the session and the top bar offers the way back.
  - Data: one gated aggregate read `getOwnerConsole` (Owner/Dispatch only; everyone else gets canView:false and nothing) joining the company roster with the sealed effective duty timeline split per driver (legacy unstamped rows belong to the local account, per the timeline's own rule), HOS planning clocks per driver (same 11/14/70/break rules as the in-app clocks and DOT packet), privacy-gated positions (IDENTICAL rule to `getCompanyLocations`: coordinates only while on duty / on an active trip — off-duty locations are never sent), each driver's current taken board load (lane/pay/live tracker status), and today's daily-log miles.
  - Sections wire to EXISTING features, not copies: Fleet Map = Leaflet map of the visible drivers + click-to-select detail (duty, HOS clocks, current load); Load Board = the same `FleetLoadBoardPanel` in a desktop grid with the post form beside it (desktop file upload included); Drivers = roster + per-driver detail + owner-only seat changes (`setCompanyRole`), remove (`removeCompanyMember`), add-by-username (`addCompanyDriver`); Hours = fleet-wide clocks + the existing `HosLogPanel` with its role-gated owner log-fix tools; Settlements = fleet IFTA + trip-spreadsheet ExportBars, per-trip sheet exports, carrier settlements list; Documents = chain-integrity status + paperwork index export + the company paperwork vault list; Company Chat = the existing `ChatPanel`; Settings = the existing `CompanySetupSection` + a shortcut to the full Settings sheet.
  - HONEST STAGING LIMIT: this build is single-device — another member's sealed duty log and live position live on their own phone, so their console rows read "no records on this device yet" / "No live location reported" rather than anything estimated. Fleet-wide live data fills in when real driver accounts report from their own devices at release. Standalone portal address/login remains a launch-packaging item, as x was told.

## Addendum — Broker upload links + approval queue (edit, 2026-10-25)
- Migration 0048: `broker_optins` (one row per scope — `company:<id>` fleet / `user:<username>` solo; enabled flag, OFF by default), `broker_links` (one unguessable-token link per broker: broker name/company/contact, expiry, revoked-at, creator), `broker_submissions` (the Broker Inbox: full load-field copy, doc blob + SHA-256 fingerprint, status pending|approved|rejected, reviewer + review time + note, boardLoadId for fleet approvals / loadId+tripQueueId for solo approvals).
- Gating (x amendment): OPTIONAL and OFF by default. Eligible = Diamond plan or an Owner/Dispatch fleet seat (the fleet runs on the Owner plan). Fleet: the OWNER flips the opt-in; Owner/Dispatch create/revoke links and review. Solo Diamond owner-operator: personal scope, approving creates their own load at Booked + trip (no board). Gold/Basic get no links/inbox; Ask Meca states plainly it's a Diamond/Fleet feature. Company DRIVER seats are never eligible and see nothing until an approval posts a load to the board.
- Broker side is exactly two token-keyed actions (`getBrokerUploadInfo`, `submitBrokerUpload`) that read no company/board/driver data — upload-only by construction. Expired/revoked/disabled links answer dead with a plain reason. On submit, the existing load-document vision extraction runs server-side (no plan gate — the broker has no plan) and pre-fills only fields the broker left blank; PDFs attach unread, fields typed by hand, exactly like the board post flow.
- Approval (fleet) inserts a normal `board_loads` row (postedBy = reviewer, doc attached, notes stamp the broker source) so claim/assign downstream is untouched. Approval (solo) reuses `createLoadFromBoard` against the submission fields. Rejects keep the row + doc + fingerprint on record. Audit = submission row (broker + link, submitted-at, doc fingerprint, reviewer/at) + hash-chain events: broker_feature_enabled/disabled, broker_link_created (token hint only, never the full token), broker_link_revoked, broker_submission_received (+ doc_sealed), broker_submission_edited, broker_submission_approved (+ board_load_posted in fleet mode), broker_submission_rejected. Full wipe clears all three tables + submission doc blobs.
- HONEST LIMIT (in UI wherever a link shows): a broker outside the app opening the link rides on the standalone/public portal packaging (same step as the public portal address / iOS install). In this build the complete flow runs in-app; the Broker view preview renders exactly the upload-only form (BrokerUploadForm) a broker gets, and submissions from it land in the real inbox.
- No changes to prices, tiers, rewards, points, routing, claim/assign, or the driver experience.

## Addendum — Instant Pay Phase 0 records + seal grade + payout hardening (edit, 2026-10-25)
- PHASE 0 PAYMENT PACKET (migration 0049, `payment_packets`): one packet per load (UNIQUE load_id), assembled at the delivery seal from records the app already holds — no new upload flow. Contents: the rate confirmation fingerprint (loads.doc_sha256), the pickup BOL fingerprint (loads.pickup_bol_sha256), the live POD seal's chain hash, a broker-identity snapshot, and a GENERATED invoice (ref `INV-LOAD-<id>`, amount = rate + additional charges) whose fingerprint is SHA-256 over a canonical invoice string (deterministic per load). The packet itself carries one fingerprint (SHA-256 over the canonical assembly) and a human ref `PKT-<loadId>-<fp8>`. A re-seal after a void refreshes the POD linkage on the same packet (`payment_packet_refreshed`); loads sealed before packets existed assemble theirs on first view (`listLoads` / `getPaymentPacket`). Chain events: `payment_packet_created`, `payment_packet_refreshed`, `payment_packet_error` (a packet failure never fails the seal), `invoice_fingerprint_duplicate`. RECORDS ONLY: no escrow, no advances, no releases, no money state anywhere on a packet.
- INVOICE FINGERPRINT REGISTRY (migration 0049, `invoice_registry`): APPEND-ONLY, one row per invoice fingerprint (UNIQUE), first registration wins and is never updated or deleted. Written at packet assembly; a fingerprint already registered by an earlier packet on a DIFFERENT load flags the new packet (`invoice_seen_before`) and seals a warning event — the record-level double-financing guard. `checkInvoiceFingerprint` is the pure-read lookup later phases run before any release/advance exists.
- RECEIVABLE KEY + REGISTRY (migration 0051, fix for a defect live-probed 2026-10-25): the invoice fingerprint's canonical string embeds `load:<id>`, so every distinct load mints a distinct fingerprint and the cross-load `invoice_seen_before` flag was structurally unreachable through normal load creation — the registry proved a fingerprint was registered but could NOT detect the same invoice under two load records. The fix adds a SECOND fingerprint, the RECEIVABLE KEY (pure builder `server/src/receivableKey.ts` + unit tests): SHA-256 over a canonical string versioned `stonemiles-receivable-v1` built ONLY from the receivable's own facts — rate-con document fingerprint (loads.doc_sha256) + broker identity (MC preferred when present, else normalized company, else normalized name; normalization = trim, collapse internal whitespace, uppercase) + invoice amount (the same rate + additional-charges computation the invoice fingerprint uses). NO load id or load-scoped data anywhere in it. Registered in the append-only sibling table `receivable_registry` (UNIQUE key, first registration wins; existing `invoice_registry` rows untouched) at packet assembly; a key first registered by a DIFFERENT load sets `payment_packets.receivable_seen_before` (new column; `receivable_key` also stored on the packet) and seals the duplicate in the existing `packet_document_duplicate` chain event, whose payload now carries the receivable key, the first-registered load id + packet ref, the broker-identity source, and which facts matched (compared against the first packet's snapshot). The event now fires on EITHER duplicate signal (identical rate-con document fingerprint OR receivable-key collision). Packets expose `receivableKey` + `receivableSeenBefore` via `packetShape`/`getPaymentPacket`; the load-detail packet block warns on either flag. `checkReceivableKey` is the pure-read counterpart of `checkInvoiceFingerprint` (registered / firstLoadId / firstPacketRef / registeredAt). The load-scoped invoice fingerprint is UNCHANGED (packet identity depends on it; staging registrations exist). Packets assembled before migration 0051 keep `receivable_key` NULL and the flag false — history is not rewritten. RECORDS ONLY, as with all of Phase 0: no money movement, every money gate stays OFF.
- BROKER IDENTITY CAPTURE (migration 0049): `broker_name` / `broker_company` / `broker_mc` (+ `broker_source`, `broker_link_id` on loads/board posts) added to `loads`, `board_loads`, `broker_links` (MC only), `broker_submissions` (MC only). Capture paths: broker link creation takes an optional MC (Broker panel field) → submissions inherit it from their link → approvals stamp identity + source `broker_link` + link id onto the board post (fleet) or the created load (solo, via createLoadFromBoard); board post form takes optional broker name/company/MC (source `board_post`); saveLoad takes optional broker fields (source `manual`). Packets snapshot the load's broker identity at assembly. Existing rows keep NULL — deliberately NOT backfilled, so Phase 2 risk-score history starts honest from this migration forward.
- RELEASE-GRADE POD SEAL (no migration — grades live on chain records): pure evaluator `server/src/sealGrade.ts` (+ unit tests). Release-grade ⇔ ALL of: (a) seal distance ≤ 0.25 mi from the delivery spot (measured distance recorded; the 3-mile points classification is unchanged); (b) pickup BOL attached + fingerprinted; (c) no open exception flag (today: pickup seal-number mismatch); (d) trip GPS fix quality meets the good-fix standard — ≥50% of the trip's raw fixes inside the 65 m radius (the fix filter's GPS_ACCURACY_LIMIT_M) and ≥1 accepted fix, from `trips.fix_quality`. Grade + per-criterion results are stored ON the `pod_seal` payload at seal time; because the trip sheet seals after the POD, `saveTrip` re-evaluates and appends a `pod_seal_grade` event when anything changed (the seal record itself is never rewritten). `podStatusByLoad` folds seal + grade events; surfaces: load tracker/load detail (grade + what's still short + broker + packet block), Settlement Checker report lines. NOTHING consumes the grade yet — Phase 1 escrow will require release-grade.
- VESTING-RACE FIX (migration 0050): `ref_key` (UNIQUE) added to `earning_pool_ledger` + `reward_wallet_ledger`. The four rewards payout processors — `processWelcomeQueue`, `processWelcomeVesting` (pay + lapse-return paths), `processReferralQueue`, `payGiveawayVesting` — now claim-and-pay: runs are serialized in-process (`enqueuePayout`, the enqueueChain pattern); the claim is ONE atomic conditional UPDATE (… WHERE status='queued'/'pending' … RETURNING id) so exactly one run can flip a row; the payout ledger row is written under a unique ref_key (`welcome_credit:<id>`, `welcome_vesting:<id>`, `welcome_vesting_returned:<id>`, `referral_reward:<id>`, `giveaway_vesting:<id>`) so the database refuses a second record for the same source. `repairClaimedPayouts` runs at the top of each processor and restores a ledger row a crashed run claimed but never recorded — matching on exact kind/username/amount/note so pre-hardening payouts (ref_key NULL) are recognised as recorded and never duplicated; clawback "returned" rows (note "reversed — …") are untouched. NO amounts, schedules, or gates changed — GIVEAWAY_LIVE stays false, vesting activation stays off, all money gates stay exactly as they were.
