## Addendum — Round 3: My Desk, the dispatcher's own desk (edit, 2026-11-02)
- Ordered by x 2026-11-02 ~11:03 CT ("Dispatcher should have their own desk"). Spec: ~/workspace/goals/trucker-gps-app/files/dispatcher-my-desk-spec-2026-11-02.md. Builds on Round 1 (dispatch-company accounts, 0087) and Round 2 (sign-out/Face ID, driver gate, 20% fee cap, 2% platform fee, platform approval queue, 0088–0089). The company Dispatch Desk stays the owner roof view, unchanged.
- **Eligibility** (pure `server/src/myDesk.ts` · `myDeskAccess`): (a) dispatch-seat holder in ANY company (fleet or dispatch kind); (b) dispatch company owner; (c) SOLO dispatcher — any profile that opened My Desk (`openMyDesk` sets `social_users.dispatcher_poster`, migration 0090, sealed `my_desk_opened`). A fleet owner seat and driver seats are NOT doors in on their own; they enter solo like anyone else. Ineligible accounts get the plain explanation + the three doors inside the sheet and via Ask Meca.
- **Contents** (`getMyDesk`, person-scoped on every query): MY PIPELINE — poster loads on my poster account or created by me, plus company-board loads with `posted_by = me` (poster-released rows never double-counted; an overflowed original is represented by its Open Board copy), in plain states Draft → In platform review → Live on the board → Claimed → Delivered — sealed (`myDeskPipelineState`), with the approval state spelled out (in-queue note, release credit, return note verbatim). POST A LOAD through the existing doors (Broker Portal → platform approval queue; company board). MY FEE — existing poster-override → company-default precedence (`myDeskFee` names the source), cap 20% server-side via the existing setMyPosterFee / setDispatcherFee. MY PLATFORM FEES — `platform_fee_entries` scoped by the new `poster_username` column (captured at accrual from `broker_poster_loads.created_by`; backfilled for pre-0090 rows in the migration). SHORTCUTS — my company chats (open the exact group via a ChatPanel group-focus prop) + desk context (company Dispatch Desk, My company).
- **2% accrual — who accrues after this round** (`platformFeeApplies`, extended): (1) posters of a DISPATCH-ONLY company (unchanged, Round 2 — accounting entity stays the company); (2) NEW: SOLO DISPATCHER POSTERS — a poster account whose owner opened My Desk (dispatcher_poster flag); outside a dispatch company's roof the flag alone decides, so a poster under a fleet roof (or none) who opted in accrues on their own poster-account loads, owed as a person (the entry's `company_id` is NULL, `poster_username` is the owing entity). NEVER: converted (Fleet-Owner-active) dispatch companies (their posters are judged by the company rule alone, flag or not), fleet overflow, and any poster — fleet, shipper, or broker — who never opened a dispatcher desk (no flag, no accrual; the round-2 rule). Same module, same label ("Platform fee 2% — invoiced at sealed delivery"), one entry per load at sealed delivery, records only.
- **Schema (migration 0090)**: `social_users.dispatcher_poster` boolean (the opt-in flag); `platform_fee_entries` rebuilt with `company_id` NULLABLE + `poster_username` (unique load_id index preserved; poster backfilled from the board/poster chain).
- **Solo poster link** (`linkMyPosterAccount`): a profile links its OWN poster account (name + contact email, validated in myDesk.ts) routed to the PLATFORM review queue by the same `platformQueueCompanyId` rule as a dispatch company's poster — never self-review; linking also sets the dispatcher-poster flag (posting under your own account is the opt-in). Sealed `solo_poster_account_linked`.
- **Findability**: "My Desk" button at the top of More (visible only when eligible — `getMyDesk` standing read by the App shell) AND in Settings → My social profile (same gate); Ask Meca destination `my_desk` (resolver phrases: my desk / my pipeline / loads I posted / my platform fees / solo dispatch…) with the doors explanation as the note for ineligible accounts — the jump is never hard-locked because the sheet itself carries the explainer + the working solo door (same soft-lock pattern as team_portal / shipper_portal).
- **Unchanged**: all locked pricing, no money movement (records/sealing only), conversion, private board, approval-queue mechanics, company Dispatch Desk.
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite + typecheck (counts in the build report); NOT phone-proven.

## Addendum — Round 4: Dispatcher exclusivity, one desk or the other (edit, 2026-11-02)
- Ordered by x 2026-11-02 ~12:09 CT ("a dispatcher can only do one or the other, never have both — to prevent double booking taking from company owners"). No migration (0091 stays last) and no schema change: the rule is resolved from records that already exist — company_members rows, companies.ownerUsername, and the social_users.dispatcher_poster flag (0090).
- **Modes** (pure `server/src/myDesk.ts` · `resolveDispatcherStanding`, resolved server-side by `dispatcherStandingFor` over ALL of a profile's roster rows + owned dispatch companies + the flag): COMPANY = an active dispatch seat in ANY company (fleet or dispatch kind) or owning a dispatch company (owner seat or ownerUsername). SOLO = flag open AND no company standing. A driver seat and fleet-company ownership are NOT company standing (round 3 unchanged). COMPANY dispatcher's My Desk (person-view under the company roof) is unaffected — the rule governs the SOLO channel against company standing, never the person-view of company work.
- **Solo door refuses under company standing**: `openMyDesk` and `linkMyPosterAccount` (the same opt-in — linking a solo poster sets the flag) return "You're dispatching under {company} — a dispatcher runs one desk or the other, never both. Leave the company's dispatch seat to run solo." (`soloOpenGate`). An already-open flag under company standing is the stored-BOTH case: nothing new opens, reads suppress the solo side, and closeMyDesk stays available to clean the flag up.
- **Company doors refuse while the solo desk is effectively open**: `createCompany` kind=dispatch → "Your solo desk is open — one desk or the other. Close the solo desk first (live loads must finish or be withdrawn first)."; `setCompanyRole` → dispatch checks the TARGET member's standing and refuses with the named variant ("@name's solo desk is open — …They have to close the solo desk first…"). The white-glove roster import is a roster-add path too: dispatch-role rows for solo-open profiles are rejected with the same reason in BOTH preview and commit (never half-written); driver rows are untouched. Note: code joins (`joinCompany`) always land as DRIVER (driver_codes carries no role) — no join-with-dispatch-role path exists, so there is no dispatch seat to guard at join; seats arise only via the guarded doors above.
- **Switching guard** (`closeMyDesk`, new, sealed `my_desk_closed`): clears the flag; REFUSED while the caller has LIVE loads under their solo poster — poster loads on the poster account they OWN whose state is live by `soloPosterLoadIsLive`: poster status released, board row still standing (a withdrawn row is deleted — missing row = withdrawn, not live), board status open or taken, delivery unsealed (podStatusByLoad). Drafts / in-review / returned were never on the board and never block. History and platform-fee accruals are NEVER deleted or rewritten by closing — the action touches only the flag row. Leaving a company seat (leaveCompany / removeCompanyMember) carries NO load guard: company loads belong to the company and stay on its desk; the seat must actually be gone (row deleted) before the solo door opens — pinned by test.
- **Reconciliation**: every reader now consumes the EFFECTIVE solo value (`standing.soloEffective`), never the raw flag — `getMyDesk` (plus new response fields dispatcherMode / soloDeskOpen / soloSuppressed / standingCompanyName), the `getLoadBoard` shell standing, and `ensurePlatformFeeEntry` (a stored-BOTH poster's loads accrue NOTHING on the solo door while company standing stands; dispatch-company posters remain judged by the company rule alone). Stored accrual history is never rewritten.
- **Client**: MyDesk.tsx shows the caller's mode line, the doors copy states the one-or-the-other rule on all three doors, and a "One desk or the other — close the solo desk" section renders while the stored flag is open (solo mode: the switch explanation + live-load warning; suppressed: company-wins explanation + clear-the-flag button) calling `closeMyDesk` with refusals surfaced verbatim. Ask Meca `my_desk` blurb + nav notes state the rule, both refusal sentences, the close guard, and the reconciliation.
- **Unchanged**: all locked pricing/economics (20% cap, 2% platform fee, Fleet conversion), no money movement, company Dispatch Desk, approval queue, private board.
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite 826 pass / 0 fail (804 baseline, +22: standing modes, both refusal directions with exact copy, seat-removal-then-solo-opens, live-load matrix incl. seal/withdraw unblocking the close, round-4 accrual reconciliation), typecheck clean. NOT phone-proven.

## Addendum — Broker Desk + Owner Desk (round 5, x 2026-11-02: "Build Broker Desk + Owner Desk to match My Desk")
- **Pattern**: desks are home screens ON TOP of the existing surfaces, not replacements — the Broker Portal keeps its posting flows (the Broker Desk is its front door) and the Owner Console keeps its deep work (the Owner Desk is the command screen with shortcuts in). Same visual language and sheet pattern as MyDesk.tsx; pure rules in `server/src/desks.ts` (+ `desks.test.ts`); actions `getBrokerDesk` / `getOwnerDesk` in actions.ts. NO migration — no new stored state; both desks are read models over existing rows.
- **Broker Desk eligibility** (`brokerDeskAccess`): a linked poster account (broker_accounts.linked_username — the Broker Portal population) AND no dispatcher standing. Exclusivity interaction (brief §4): a solo dispatcher (solo flag effectively open) or a dispatch-company poster resolves dispatcherMode company|solo and does NOT get a Broker Desk — `getBrokerDesk` answers reason "dispatcher" and the sheet's explainer points at My Desk. No poster account + no standing → reason "no_poster" + the become-a-poster explainer (desk-invited accounts; access via the Stonervision team email). Contents: my posted loads as a pipeline in My Desk's plain states (shared `myDeskPipelineState` / labels) over MY poster account's loads only, with `brokerQueueNote` spelling out whose review queue a sent load sits in (the account's queue company by name; the platform queue when none) and why, returned-load desk notes, and releaser on released loads; my poster fee = the existing Broker Portal surface (`broker_accounts.dispatcherFeePct`, set/edit via the existing `setMyPosterFee`, poster → dispatch-company default → none precedence exactly as the release path snapshots); totals via `deskTotals` (posted / sealed / gross moved = sealed loads' rates only).
- **Owner Desk eligibility** (`ownerDeskAccess` + `fleetCompanyForOwner`): owner of a kind=fleet company — an owner seat in a fleet company or owning one outright (companies.ownerUsername). Dispatch company owners are NOT eligible through a dispatch company (their roof stays the Dispatch Desk). Contents: company pipeline = every board_loads row with companyId = the company OR originCompanyId = the company (private board + Open Board presence), overflowed originals represented by their Open Board copy (My Desk's rule), PLUS poster loads with status "sent" in the company's review queue as "review"-state rows; review queue front and center (`reviewQueueCount` = those sent rows; the sheet jumps into the Broker Portal queue); roster snapshot (`ownerRosterSnapshot` — role counts + seat math on the locked allowances from fleetCreation.ts: 3 driver seats incl. owner, first dispatch seat; available never negative); plan/billing = recorded plan + period + `fleetSwitchBillingBasis` over the CURRENT roster, owner-plan line stating base + extra-seat charges as the per-cycle figure at app-store release (the switch basis's difference total is NOT presented as the ongoing price); company totals via `deskTotals` (gross = sealed board rows' rate + extra charges). Shortcuts into the Owner Console functions (Fleet Board, Broker Portal queue, My company, Plans, My Desk when eligible) — nothing duplicated.
- **Walls**: broker queries filter `broker_poster_loads.brokerId = my account`; owner queries filter board rows by the caller's own company and queue rows by `broker_poster_loads.companyId = my company`. Another poster's/company's rows never match — nonexistent, same discipline as My Desk. Round 4 untouched: fleet owners who also dispatch under their own fleet hold Owner Desk + My Desk (company mode) side by side (verified live in sandbox: x's account resolves Owner Desk on RUGGS LOGISTICS with My Desk eligibility unchanged).
- **Entry points**: More menu + Settings → My social profile buttons for eligible accounts only, gated on `brokerDeskEligible` / `ownerDeskEligible` carried by the always-on `getLoadBoard` gate query (same pattern as `myDeskEligible`); Ask Meca destinations `broker_desk` / `owner_desk` with plain lock reasons (never hard-locked — the sheets carry the explainers, like `my_desk`), intent patterns, nav notes per standing, and the Ask Meca destination summary updated.
- **Unchanged**: locked pricing/economics (20% fee cap, Fleet Owner seat math, plan prices), poster review rules, no money movement, Broker Portal / Owner Console / Dispatch Desk / My Desk behavior.
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite 848 pass / 0 fail (826 baseline, +22 desks tests: eligibility matrix incl. both exclusivity routings, queue notes, totals, roster snapshot), typecheck clean (client + server). Sandbox action proof: getBrokerDesk no-poster explainer state; eligible path with a sandbox-created poster account (pipeline draft row, setMyPosterFee 7.5% reading back source "poster", totals posted=1); getOwnerDesk on RUGGS LOGISTICS (fleet, Fleet Owner plan, yearly basis, roster 1/3 driver seats). NOT phone-proven. NOT shared/published.

## Addendum — Weigh-station approach prompt + crowd-sourced status (edit, 2026-11-02)
- Ordered by x 2026-11-02 ~19:47 CT ("on the gps can we add when approaching weight stations a prompt of open n close pop up to update status") — his second explicit exception to the Nov 13 GPS code freeze (the first was Fuel + Stop Planner). Routing engine (Valhalla + TomTom), duty-prompt behavior, and all gates untouched.
- **Data (migration 0092)**: stations stay client-side OpenStreetMap POIs from the existing hazard layer (Overpass `amenity=weighbridge` / `highway=weigh_station` nodes, stable id `weigh-<osm node id>`, re-queried within ~5 mi as the truck moves). `weigh_station_status` (PK station_id: name, lat/lon, status open|closed, last reporter, report_count, updated_at) holds the current status per station; `weigh_station_reports` is the append-only report log (reporter, status, driver GPS, station coords, verified flag, points awarded). `app_settings.weigh_prompts` boolean default ON.
- **Trigger (pure `client/src/weighStation.ts`)**: trip live + prompts on + station not yet prompted this trip + status unknown or ≥60 min old → prompt at ~2 mi ahead: along-route remaining distance (route projection, station within 0.3 mi of the route line) when a route is active, else bearing-ahead fallback (±60° cone of GPS course, ≤2 mi straight-line; no heading fix = no fallback prompt). Nearest qualifying station wins; WeighPromptTracker owns the per-trip cooldown + one-at-a-time; a new trip (session change) resets it.
- **UI**: `WeighStationPromptCard` on the Drive screen (both drive-mode and panel faces, next to the duty prompt): "⚖️ Weigh station ahead — {distance}", cab-size OPEN / CLOSED buttons (min 72px), dismiss X. 20 s no-answer auto-dismiss records NOTHING (structural: only `answer()` produces a report; expiry/dismiss return none). Answered → ~3 s "Thanks — status updated" card, then gone.
- **Submit/points (`reportWeighStationStatus`)**: records the report and upserts the station's current status for every driver. Server-side GPS verification (haversine vs station, ≤2.5 mi = approach zone + answer-lag tolerance; pure rules in `server/src/weighStation.ts`): verified reports earn through the EXISTING road-intel path — `awardDailyTask` task `intel` (+35 paid table value, first verified intel of the day, daily cap unchanged; points conversion stays gated as-is). Unverified reports update status, earn 0, and are marked unverified on the log.
- **Display/freshness**: `listWeighStationStatuses` applies 4-hour staleness at read time (older reads `unknown`, age still returned); the Alerts near you list carries a status line under every weigh station ("Open — updated 12 min ago" / "Status unknown — …") via `weighStatusLine`. 60-minute freshness suppresses prompts (fresh status just displays).
- **Findability**: Ask Meca destination `weigh_station_status` (resolver: weigh station / scale house / scales open|closed|status…; jump → Drive tab, panel open, anchored to #alerts-near-you); Settings → Driving toggle "Weigh station prompts" (default ON).
- **Data gap, honestly**: station coverage is exactly OSM's — where mappers haven't tagged weigh stations (some rural corridors), no station exists to prompt on; the hazard query window is ~5 mi around the truck, so a station is only known once the truck is inside that window (the 2 mi trigger sits well inside it). Status starts unknown everywhere until drivers report — there is no external status feed, by design (crowd-sourced only, never invented).
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite 882 pass / 0 fail (848 baseline, +34: trigger distance/ahead incl. route-vs-bearing cases, 60-min gate boundaries, per-trip cooldown incl. reset, 20 s expiry producing no report, 4 h staleness, verified-only points radius, prompts-off), typecheck clean (client + server). Sandbox action proof: verified report at 1.5 mi → verified=true, +35 points; far report → status recorded, verified=false, 0 points; list returns fresh status + unknown for unreported; getAppSettings round-trips weighPrompts=true. NOT phone-proven. NOT shared/published.

## Addendum 2026-11-02 (later) — weigh prompt at 0.7 mi + "Station here" (migration 0093)

X's follow-up order, verbatim: "Can you do .7 miles and if station isn't on the map can we make a easy way to update it's a station here n open".

- **Prompt distance 0.7 mi**: client `WEIGH_TRIGGER_MI` 2 → 0.7 (route-remaining distance; bearing-cone fallback unchanged). All other trigger rules unchanged (unknown or >60 min status, once per station per trip, 20 s no-answer records nothing, late answers rejected). Server mirror `WEIGH_APPROACH_MI` = 0.7.
- **Verification radius deliberately NOT shrunk**: server `WEIGH_VERIFY_RADIUS_MI` stays 2.5 mi, now explicitly decoupled from the prompt distance in code comments — the sandbox proof paid a verified report at 1.5 mi and that still verifies (pinned by test).
- **Station here (two taps)**: "⚖️ Station here — not on the map?" button on the Drive screen (both layouts) and mirrored in Alerts near you → tap OPEN or CLOSED → saved. No form, no typing. GPS position captured at the first tap. Label is best-effort only: reverse-geocoded town via the existing Nominatim reverse lookup (`fetchCityState`, 3 s race), fallback "Driver-reported weigh station"; saving never blocks on it.
- **Storage (migration 0093)**: new table `driver_weigh_stations` (station_id `driver-<uuid>` PK, name, lat, lon, reporter, created_at) — the station's existence record; persists after status staleness. Status rides the existing `weigh_station_status` / `weigh_station_reports` under the same id, same 4-hour staleness and 60-minute freshness rules.
- **Actions**: `listDriverWeighStations` (all driver-added stations, newest first, cap 500) — the client merges them into the prompt station list, the status fetch, and the Alerts display, so a created station prompts OTHER drivers under the same 0.7 mi / freshness / per-trip cooldown rules. `listWeighStationStatuses` now also returns `driverReported` / `addedBy` / `addedAt`; displays mark driver-added stations ("Driver-reported — added by @name · 2 h ago", `weighAddedByLine`). `reportStationHere` — dedupe decided server-side by pure `planStationHereReport` over the full known universe (client's nearby map stations + all stored stations): within ~0.3 mi of an existing station → status report on THAT station, response `created:false`, client banner "Updated the station already on the map."; otherwise creates at the tap position.
- **Points**: creation/update through this flow is GPS-verified by definition (driver standing there; distance 0 / ≤0.3 mi, inside the verify radius) and pays through the same existing road-intel path (`awardDailyTask` task `intel`, same rate as a verified prompt report — no new rate invented). One creation reward per location, enforced by the dedupe rule (a second tap at the same spot plans an update, never a second create — pinned by test).
- **Proof**: full suite 903 pass / 0 fail (882 baseline, +21: 0.7 mi boundary on both route-remaining and bearing paths incl. 1.5 mi no longer prompting, verify-radius decoupling, dedupe within/beyond 0.3 mi client + server, created station prompting another driver with freshness/cooldown/staleness, creation-exactly-once planning, added-by line).

## Addendum 2026-11-03 (night) — Drive banner removal + "Not here" place corrections (migration 0094)

X's road-test verdicts 2026-11-03 ~18:46 CT, from screenshots of the live Drive screen.

- **FIX 1 — TURN AHEAD banner removed**: the giant maneuver card (huge street name, "Turn left onto…", "At the split") duplicated the slim top instruction bar and covered half the map. Ordinary-turn cards no longer render anywhere (Go map overlay, Drive mode, Drive panel): `maneuverCard` in App.tsx now keeps only EXIT cards from `evaluateManeuverCard` (its pure logic + tests unchanged); the split-sign prompt (SignCheckPrompt) is untouched. Turns ride the slim top bar alone — turn arrow + distance + street name, with the trip readout (minutes left · miles left · ETA) in the same bar. Nothing renders in the banner's place; the map reclaims the space. Speedometer, LIMIT chip, weather chip, traffic legend, End route / Full screen, and map controls untouched. Help copy (Ask Meca sign_check destination, SignCheckPanel) updated to match.
- **FIX 2 — "✕ Not here" corrections (migration 0094)**: unified road-intel place universe keyed by one string place id — `weigh-<osm>` (OSM weigh stations), `driver-<uuid>` (driver-reported stations, 0093), `intel-<id>` (intel reports, every kind), `explore-…` (Explore places). New tables: `road_place_corrections` (append-only audit: place id/kind, reporting driver, GPS at report time, outcome, timestamp — plus a sealed hash-chain event `road_place_not_here` per mark), `road_place_hides` (per-driver hide, PK place+username), `road_place_retired` (for-everyone tombstone: reason `creator_deleted` | `threshold`). `intel_reports.reporter` added (0094) so intel spots have a known creator going forward; legacy rows read creator-null. Pure outcome rules in `server/src/weighStation.ts` + client mirror (`planNotHereCorrection`, `NOT_HERE_RETIRE_THRESHOLD = 2`, `isPlaceSuppressedForDriver`, `roadPlaceKindForId`, `intelReportIdForPlaceId`, `excludeRetiredCandidates`): a mark always hides for the marker; the creator's mark deletes outright (driver-owned row deleted + tombstone); a second independent mark retires for everyone; the same driver twice never counts twice.
- **Actions**: `markPlaceNotHere {placeId, lat, lon}` → `{outcome: hidden|deleted|retired, correctionCount}` (idempotent on retired/re-marked places); `listRoadPlaceState` → this driver's hidden ids + all retired ids + their correction history. Server lists enforce suppression: `listIntelReports` and `listDriverWeighStations` never serve hidden-for-me or retired places; `listWeighStationStatuses` carries `notHereHidden`/`notHereRetired` per station; `confirmIntelReport` refuses retired spots; `reportWeighStationStatus` records nothing for a retired station (no resurrection); `reportStationHere` dedupe excludes retired candidates (a tap near a retired spot creates new). NO points move in any correction path.
- **Client**: persisted state via `listRoadPlaceState` (60 s refetch) drives every filter — map markers (weigh hazards, intel, Explore), Alerts near you, Road intel list, driver-station list, and the 0.7 mi prompt universe; `evaluateWeighPrompt` also takes `hiddenPlaceIds`/`retiredPlaceIds` and suppresses inside the evaluator, so the OPEN/CLOSED prompt can never fire for a hidden or retired spot. "✕ Not here" buttons: weigh hazards + driver stations in Alerts near you, every Road intel row, the approach prompt card (closes with NO status recorded, correction instead), and the Explore selected-place card — each next to the existing OPEN/CLOSED reporting for that place.
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite 923 pass / 0 fail (903 baseline, +20 new: outcome planning incl. creator-outranks-threshold and same-driver-twice, place-id universe, suppression, prompt suppression for hidden/retired incl. tracker-level, retired-excluded Station-here dedupe + live-station dedupe unregressed); client + server typecheck clean. Sandbox action proof: intel report created at the Government Blvd coords (30.6347, −88.1589) → creator mark → outcome `deleted`, gone from `listIntelReports`, correction + hidden + retired all recorded in `listRoadPlaceState`; OSM `weigh-999001` mark → `hidden`, re-mark idempotent (count stays 1), `listWeighStationStatuses` flags `notHereHidden`; Station here created a driver station (+35 verified points, unregressed) → creator mark → `deleted`, `listDriverWeighStations` empty, later OPEN report on the retired id recorded nothing (verified false, 0 points). The two-independent-drivers retirement path is pinned by the pure-logic unit tests only — staging is single-user (`myUsername` is the one profile), so a second distinct reporter cannot be exercised through the actions here. NOT phone-proven — x's next drive is the real proof. NOT shared/published.

## Addendum 2026-11-03 (late night) — big prompts 5-second auto-dismiss + small popup see-through (NO migration)

X's third road-test verdict, from his 10:59 PM Drive screenshot: the small weigh-station status popup still sat as a solid black box mid-map, and big prompts (interstate sign / exit / switch / sign prompts) should show 5 seconds then disappear. Client-only repair — no schema, no migration (continues from 0094 unused).

- **REPAIR A — 5 s auto-dismiss for BIG on-map prompts**: new pure module `client/src/bigPrompt.ts` — `BIG_PROMPT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5_000` + `BigPromptTracker` (seen-set per pass, one current prompt, replacement-never-stacking, repeat evaluations never reset the clock, reset on a new route = new pass). App.tsx gates every `SignCheckPrompt` / `ManeuverCardPrompt` render (Go-map overlay, Drive Mode, Drive panel) on the tracker's visible key, keyed by the prompts' stable dedupe keys, with a 5 s timer that expires the prompt recording NOTHING (the tracker is display-only — no report exists on its timeout path). A genuinely new event replaces a showing prompt and gets its own full 5 s. The parked `SignCheckPanel` preview is not a prompt and is not gated. The weigh OPEN/CLOSED prompt joins the family: `WEIGH_PROMPT_TIMEOUT_MS` 20_000 → 5_000 (a test pins it equal to `BIG_PROMPT_TIMEOUT_MS`); its existing semantics stand untouched — a tap inside the window records exactly as before, expiry/dismissal produce no report (structural in `WeighPromptTracker`), and the station stays in the per-trip cooldown so it never re-fires on the same pass. All user-facing "20 seconds" copy updated to 5 (WeighStationPromptCard, Ask Meca weigh_station_status + sign_check blurbs, SignCheckPanel).
- **REPAIR B — small place popup, SEE-THROUGH branch taken** (not removal): the MapLibre tooltip popup (`.sm-map-popup`, used by weigh/intel/hazard/POI/fuel/pin markers — the screenshot's "Weigh station OPEN — reported 9d ago — confirmed by your GPS — old report" box is an intel marker's tooltip) rendered with a solid `rgba(8,13,28,0.95)` panel. Now: `background: transparent`, `box-shadow: none`, popup tip transparent, at most a hairline 1px gold-tinted outline; the text is white with a heavy layered dark halo (text-shadow). Why see-through stays legible in both modes: on the pale Liberty day basemap the dark halo supplies contrast; on the CARTO dark-matter night basemap and satellite the white letterforms do, with the halo separating them from bright photo detail — the standard cartographic label treatment, mode-independent, so no day/night fork needed. Single source in `client/src/mapPopup.ts` (`MAP_POPUP_*` constants + `mapPopupTextStyle()`, consumed by `mapEngine.ts` bindTooltip); theme.css mirrors the container values and unit tests pin both sides. Markers stay tappable exactly as before — taps still select/open the place detail with its "✕ Not here" action; only the popup's box changed.
- **Untouched, per the brief**: the slim top instruction bar, route chooser, OPEN/CLOSED recording semantics, the Not here system (0094), pricing/points, voice lines for sign prompts.
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite 942 pass / 0 fail (923 baseline, +19 new: bigPrompt tracker — 5 s constant + weigh-constant parity, show/visible, auto-dismiss at 5 s, no-record-on-timeout shape, no re-fire same pass, clock never reset by re-evaluation, new event gets its own prompt, replacement resets timer + never stacks + replaced never returns, event-passed close, dismiss-seen, reset-per-pass; weigh tracker — explicit 5 s constant, no re-prompt after fade on the same trip, tap at 4.999 s still answers; mapPopup — transparent background, 1px hairline, no shadow, halo legibility, theme.css mirror/no-solid-fill regression pin); client + server typecheck clean. NOT phone-proven — x's next drive is the real proof. NOT shared/published.

## Addendum 2026-11-03 (later that night) — TomTom traffic legend chip removal + Repair 5: see-through Drive layout & GPS-screen customization (migration 0095)

X's fourth road-test verdict (screenshots 11:23–11:43 PM): "Take off the tomtom traffic icon off gps screen" — followed ~23:28 CT by: "That layout need to be see through because it blocking the map or organize it another way. User should be able to customize the gps screen."

- **Legend chip removal (the standing standard)**: the floating legend pill on the driving map — traffic-light icon, flowing/slow/heavy swatches, "TomTom <time>" line — rendered from ONE site in App.tsx whenever a route was selected, so it floated over Go/driving, Explore, and route preview alike (no separate parked-Drive-panel or route-preview render exists — the route cards' "Traffic: … (TomTom)" text lines are a different element and stay). The chip is gone from the standard layout entirely: no replacement, no collapsed form. The traffic flow coloring painted ON the route line (buildTrafficSegments — orange slow / red heavy over the blue) is untouched, as are all TomTom fetching, delays, and ETAs. TomTom attribution after this change lives OFF the driving view, where it already lived: the route-details "Why this route" panel and the Routes-tab route cards ("Traffic: … (TomTom, data as of …)"), plus the Privacy section's data-sources line. The map's own credit line (OSM/OpenFreeMap/CARTO/Esri) never carried TomTom and is unchanged.
- **Repair 5 — see-through by default**: the map wrapper carries `.drive-scope` + `data-drive-glass` (the driver's level). Inside the scope, theme.css drops the glass panel/pill backgrounds to the level's alpha and overlay text gains a dark halo (the mapPopup cartographic-label technique), so EVERY floating element on the driving map reads translucent with the map beneath: top instruction bar (glass panel), weather chip + ETA pill + radar pill (glass pills), weigh prompt card (glass panel), speed/LIMIT bubble and End route / Full screen / right-rail controls (inline backgrounds from `driveSolidBg` — still translucent at every level, with more substance than panels), and the big sign/exit prompt cards in SignCheckPrompt.tsx (solid `#0b3d24` / `rgba(8,13,28,0.96)` → `rgba(11,61,36,0.8)` / `rgba(8,13,28,0.8)` + 8px backdrop blur + text halo). Single source for alphas: `client/src/driveScreen.ts` (`DRIVE_GLASS_PANEL_ALPHA` low .7 / med .55 / high .4; `DRIVE_GLASS_SOLID_ALPHA` low .88 / med .78 / high .66) — theme.css mirrors the panel values and tests pin both sides.
- **Repair 5 — per-driver customization, server-persisted (migration 0095)**: five new `app_settings` columns — `drive_show_weather`, `drive_show_big_prompts`, `drive_show_traffic_legend` (DEFAULT FALSE), `drive_show_eta_pill`, `drive_glass_level` ('med' default) — riding the existing mapPrefs plumbing end to end (schema → server mapPrefs row mapping → appSettingsShape/get/saveAppSettings → client mapPrefs/normalize → `visibleDriveOverlays` gate in App.tsx). Settings UI: a "Drive screen" section in the Layers & display panel (where the map prefs already live): see-through Low/Med/High segmented control, switches for Weather chip, Speed & LIMIT (the existing single mapShowSpeed switch), Big prompt cards, ETA pill, and Traffic legend, plus a one-tap "Reset to standard layout" (`STANDARD_DRIVE_LAYOUT` — legend OFF, everything else on, glass Med; the button says "✓ Standard layout" when already on it). The legend renders ONLY behind `driveVis.has("trafficLegend")` — customization is the only way it ever appears, per-driver only. The radar pill stacks at 76px only when the legend is opted in, else owns the 46px spot.
- **Limits held**: customization governs VISIBILITY + TRANSPARENCY only. The 5-second big-prompt timers and no-record-on-timeout are untouched (the big-prompt toggle gates renders; the tracker, voice lines, and weigh recording run exactly as before), Not here (0094) untouched, OPEN/CLOSED semantics untouched, top-bar contents untouched. Never toggleable: the top instruction bar, right-rail controls, End route, reroute state panels, the truck marker, and guidance itself. The weigh OPEN/CLOSED prompt keeps its own existing Settings → Driving switch (weighPrompts, 0092) rather than a second gate.
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite 962 pass / 0 fail (942 baseline, +20 net new: driveScreen 10 — alphas translucent/ordered + CSS mirror + wrapper scope + prompt-card translucency + prompt cards follow the level (`DRIVE_PROMPT_ALPHA` low .9 / med .8 / high .66 via `promptCardBg`, passed at all 4 driving renders) + solid-control alphas + per-overlay toggle absence + render gates + reset standard; trafficLegend rewritten 7 — standard-layout default-off both stacks, absence from visible overlays by default, opt-in gate, per-driver round-trip, radar stacking, route-line paint + route-details attribution preserved; server mapPrefs +3 — standard layout, round-trip persistence, bogus glass level → Med; client mapPrefs defaults/round-trip updated). Client + server typecheck clean. Sandbox action proof: getAppSettings returned the standard layout on the existing row; a saved custom layout (legend on, glass high, weather + big prompts off) read back exactly, then the sandbox was restored to standard. Formal audit: content review confirmed both repairs; its one P0 (prompt cards ignoring the level) is fixed above; its envelope flagged a transient /actions 503 on cold launch under concurrent audit load — the same hiccup the prior two builds hit, after which the same build served nav 200 with zero console errors in the live probe session (Drive tab + Layers panel driven and screenshotted). ## Addendum 2026-11-04 — MARKET PULSE weekly spot-rate context card (migration 0096)

X approved 2026-11-04 ~00:29 CT as an exception to the Nov 13 code freeze (same class as the Fuel + Stop Planner exception), after reviewing Truckstop.com's weekly Spot Market Insights post for Week 43. Follows the EIA-diesel precedent (0079): public context data, clearly labelled, never presented as a price for any specific load.

- **Source**: Truckstop.com / FTR public weekly Spot Market Insights summary (the Facebook-shared post x reviewed). The figures are THEIR product summary — NO scraping of Truckstop/FTR is built or permitted; a licensed live feed is a later money decision by x. Refresh path is MANUAL ENTRY from public summaries via the `saveMarketPulseWeek` action — a new week lands with no code change.
- **Seed (verified figures x handed over, Week 43 ending Oct 30, 2026 — the ONLY figures used)**: total spot $3.23/mi incl. fuel, −1.3¢ WoW, +41.5% YoY, total loads +8.5%, Market Demand Index at a four-week high; flatbed −1.6¢ / +42% YoY / loads +11.2%; dry van −2.5¢ / +48% / +6.3%; reefer −3.5¢ / +41% / +4.2%. Per-equipment ABSOLUTE $/mi rates are deliberately NOT stored or shown — the source's public text did not publish them; only the total spot average is a published absolute.
- **Data model (migration 0096)**: `market_pulse_weeks` — one row per week (week_ending UNIQUE): week number, week-ending date, total rate/change/YoY/loads %, MDI note, per-equipment change cents / YoY % / loads change %, source line. Bootstraps from `MARKET_PULSE_SEED` in `server/src/marketPulse.ts` on first read (the EIA seed pattern); readers serve the newest row.
- **Labelling (required, verbatim)**: "Broker-posted averages — market direction, not a quote for any load." + "Source: Truckstop.com / FTR · Week 43, ending Oct 30" + the plain week label ("Week 43, ending Oct 30, 2026"). Past ~2 weeks (age > 14 days from week-ending) the card flags stale with the week and its age — stale data never masquerades as live.
- **Placement**: Today tab (below the Fuel plan) AND the top of the Loads tab (above the views tablist) — where load-judging happens. Flatbed FIRST (x's lane, step-deck). ABSOLUTELY NOT on the Drive/GPS screen.
- **Actions**: `getMarketPulse` (ungated public context, EIA precedent) and `saveMarketPulseWeek` (manual entry; validated by `validateMarketPulseRecord` sanity rails; same week-ending corrects in place).
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite 982 pass / 0 fail (962 baseline, +20 new: server marketPulse 14 — seed figures exact, no per-equipment absolute, seed passes validation round-trip gate, flatbed-first ordering, required labels verbatim, source line verbatim, staleness boundary + note, formatting, validation rejects; client marketPulse 6 — card render model for Week 43, flatbed-first even when rows arrive shuffled, labelling presence, no per-equipment $/mi in the model, stale note surfacing).

## Addendum 2026-11-04 — OS/OW PERMIT MODE (migration 0097)

X approved 2026-11-04 ~20:19 CT as a named exception to the Nov 13 code freeze. Feature ideas modeled on Route Pilot Pro (routepilotpro.com — a competitor x reviewed) but built entirely in this app's own code and patterns; no code, text, or UI assets taken from it.

- **Permit scan + confirm-before-save**: `extractPermit` joins the vision extraction family (extractLoad / extractPayStub pattern) — reads a permit photo or a PDF's rendered pages (pdfDoc.ts pipeline) and extracts ONLY what's printed: permit number, issuing state/agency, the ordered approved route EXACTLY as printed (routeSteps) plus the ordered named places on it (routePlaces, used only for corridor matching), dimensions as printed (text) + plainly-convertible numbers, valid window (YYYY-MM-DD), escorts, curfew/no-movement windows, bridge bypasses and other restrictions, and a printed QR code's decoded content where legible. NOTHING files off the read: `acceptPermit` is the confirm/save half — the driver edits every field on the confirm form in PermitsPanel (Driver tab → OS/OW Permits, beside the Document Vault) and only that tap files. The document bytes seal behind the permit row (SHA-256); attached to a load, a copy also files into `filed_doc_copies` (docKind "permit", source "permit_scan") — written directly, NOT through fileConfirmedDocCopy, because permits NEVER send email copies (x's hard rule for this build; mail stays staged/queued app-wide).
- **Data model (migration 0097)**: `permits` — username, nullable load_id, the full confirmed docket (route steps/places, curfews, restrictions as JSON line arrays, verbatim), dimensions text + numeric, valid_from/valid_to, escorts, qr_text, doc blob key/mime/sha256, filed_copy_id, completed_at, timestamps. Status is DERIVED (server/src/permit.ts + client/src/permit.ts own the same rule): completed (stamp set) > expired (window end passed) > upcoming (window not open) > active. Library groups Active / Completed / Expired; upcoming rides Active labelled with its start date. Actions: extractPermit, acceptPermit, listPermits, getPermit (docket + doc URL), setPermitCompleted. Chain events: permit_filed / permit_completed / permit_reopened. NO points/rewards wiring (road-intel points values are not locked — none invented). Tier gate: NONE at first build (permits lived wherever document scanning lived) — SUPERSEDED 2026-11-04 by x's call below.
- **Approved-route navigation**: the route chooser offers "Permit route" alongside the computed options when an active filed permit covers the run (permitOfferFor: the load's own permit wins; a lone unattached active permit is offered; several unattached are never guessed between). `startPermitRoute` geocodes the docket's ordered routePlaces through the shared geocodeSearch chain and routes sectioned Valhalla truck routing through them as via points; the resulting line is the corridor and is labelled MATCHED/DERIVED everywhere (chooser copy, banner, Go turn banner chip, alert fine print) — never presented as the state's own geometry. Voice guidance reads the corridor's own maneuvers through the existing guidance machinery. While permit mode is active, automatic rerouting and the reroute heartbeat are suppressed (locked corridor), and taking a scan-ahead traffic escape ends permit mode with an out-loud banner. If matching fails (no places named, none resolve, routing fails), the app says so plainly and lands the driver on the permit's open docket — it NEVER silently substitutes a computed fastest route.
- **Off-route hard alert**: corridorReturn (client/src/permit.ts) projects the truck onto the matched line (routeGeometry projectOntoRoute) — off-corridor beyond PERMIT_OFF_ROUTE_MI (0.1 mi, wider than the 0.08 mi reroute threshold because the corridor is a matched line) raises a big red HARD alert on the Go screen with distance off + compass heading back to the corridor (both labelled derived), plus one spoken alert per episode (re-arms on return). Display-only: no tap asked, no driver response recorded or stored.
- **Restriction placards**: PermitPlacardChips on the Go screen above the weather chip, built ONLY from the confirmed docket via buildPlacards (escorts — suppressed when the docket says none required — curfews, printed width/height/weight, restriction lines; capped at 6 with the overflow counted, never hidden). Display-only, no taps, nothing recorded. Drive see-through layout untouched — no banners, no stacks.
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite 1010 pass / 0 fail (982 baseline + 28 new permit tests: server permit 13 — ISO date validity, derived-status rules incl. inclusive edges + completed precedence + malformed-date safety, line-list parse/clean/split, driver-local-day window judging; client permit 15 — status/groups, offer selection rules, placard build incl. no-escort suppression + cap/overflow, compass + corridor off-route math with heading back, PDF page-read merging without invention). Client + server typecheck clean. NOT phone-proven — x's drive is the real proof. NOT shared/published; the public milestone-preview demo is untouched.
- **Open question flagged (NOT decided in code)**: tier gating for permit mode. It currently lives wherever document scanning lives, per the build order. Whether permits should sit behind a plan tier is x's call, to be flagged in the build report.
- **TIER DECISION — PERMIT MODE IS DIAMOND (x, 2026-11-04: "Put ours in diamond")**: decided after the competitor read on Route Pilot Pro (one paid tier, $19.99/mo or $199/yr, everything behind it — the same money as Diamond for permit mode alone). Placement follows the Settlement Checker / Safety Academy Diamond pattern exactly: (a) SERVER gates via `hasDiamondAccess` — `extractPermit` runs NO read off Diamond (returns the empty shape with `premium:false` + the recorded plan so the panel shows the lock, never a fake "not a permit" result), `acceptPermit` refuses to file, `setPermitCompleted` refuses the toggle, each with copy naming the recorded plan and the Diamond/Owner unlock (Owner drivers get Diamond included). (b) READS NEVER GATED: `listPermits` / `getPermit` stay open on every plan — a driver who drops off Diamond keeps sight of dockets already on file; the library renders read-only under the lock. (c) CLIENT: PermitsPanel takes `diamond` + `planName` props from App's plan query — off Diamond it renders the lock banner (Safety Academy pattern), hides Scan / File-by-hand and the docket's completed toggle + drive hint; the route chooser shows a locked note naming the filed permit instead of the "Use the permit route" offer, and `startPermitRoute` carries the same backstop guard. (d) PLAN SURFACES updated to match: the Plans comparison grid gains the row "OS/OW Permit Mode — permit scan, approved-route driving, restriction placards" (— / — / ✓ / ✓), and Permit Mode joins the Diamond feature lists in the Plans staging blurb, the recorded-plan line, the Diamond FAQ answer, and the server plan-catalog comment. Proof: client + server typecheck clean, full suite 1010 pass / 0 fail (unchanged count — the gate is action/UI wiring over the already-tested pure logic). NOT phone-proven. The public STONEMILES site copy was aligned to Diamond in the same decision (separate site artifact).

## Platform v27 — guest access inspection (2026-11-04)

Inspected every action's identity basis for the public-shared-apps guest upgrade. Findings: (1) NO action reads `ctx.viewer` — the app is a single-driver staging build whose identity is the device's own stored profile row (`myUsername` reads profile id=1) and company membership rows; there are no collaborative guest operations to open, and nothing keys a row off a fabricated or missing viewer identity. (2) All data actions are owner operations on that stored identity (trips, loads, permits, wallet, company records); the existing private-row checks (username / company-membership scoping, role gates, team-seat checks) are unchanged and never treat an absent viewer as ownership. (3) Inference / tool / agent / privileged paths (document reads incl. extractPermit, Ask Meca, TomTom server fetches) run only through server actions on the owner's runtime; under a shared runtime they surface as capability-unavailable and the client already degrades honestly (scan failures offer the by-hand form; no fake reads, no unsaved state shown as saved). (4) Blob URLs are issued only inside owner-scoped actions (e.g. getPermit checks the permit's username before returning its doc URL) — no guest path to private blobs was added. Conclusion: no source adaptation was required beyond this inspection; the app's boundary stays its stored-identity scoping, and restricted shares still authenticate. The Diamond gate added the same day follows the same rule — it reads the recorded plan server-side, never a client claim.

## Addendum 2026-11-05 — MARKET PULSE all-freight coverage + per-lane prices (migration 0098)

X's order 2026-11-05 ~08:00 CT: "add oversized load and all freight market prices to freight market pricing" — a named exception to the Nov 13 code freeze, same class as the original Market Pulse build.

- **What the research established (verified 2026-11-05, before any number was written)**: (1) The full Truckstop/FTR report for the SAME Week 43 already on file DOES publish per-lane absolute rates — flatbed $3.31/mi, dry van $2.85/mi, reefer $3.45/mi, total $3.23/mi — reconciling exactly with the direction figures x handed over (each lane's rate moved by exactly its published change). The original build's "source never published them" was true only of the summary post x reviewed. Full report text: https://truckdrivernews.com/latest-spot-market-results-show-rates-down-loads-up/ (Truckstop/FTR report of Nov 2, 2026, week ending Oct 30, 2026). (2) NO weekly index publishes a rate for oversize/heavy haul, step-deck, tanker, power only, or hotshot: Truckstop/FTR covers dry van/reefer/flatbed only, and DAT's free national averages likewise cover van/reefer/flatbed only — corroborated by https://smallfleetmoney.beehiiv.com/p/fleet-rate-benchmark-sheet ("DAT publishes free national averages only for van, reefer and flatbed. We don't have sourced public averages for step deck, hotshot, power-only or box trucks, so we are not listing numbers for them."). (3) Oversize specifically has NO published average anywhere — pricing is per load (a permit in every state crossed, pilot cars when dimensions require them, daylight-only movement, specialized RGN/lowboy/step-deck equipment): https://oversizeops.com/oversize-transport-cost-estimator ("An oversize load on a lowboy or RGN trailer pays more; nobody publishes an average for that, so we don't invent one."). Typical-quote ranges circulating on dispatch/calculator sites are explicitly "typical market quotes, not published averages" and were NOT used.
- **Data model (migration 0098)**: `market_pulse_weeks` gains nullable `flatbed_rate_per_mile` / `dryvan_rate_per_mile` / `reefer_rate_per_mile` — stored ONLY when a week's full report publishes the lane's absolute (never derived from the change figures; a week without them stores NULL and renders direction only). The migration also fills the Week 43 row's three rates (3.31 / 2.85 / 3.45) ONLY where all three are still NULL, so a hand-corrected week is never overwritten. `saveMarketPulseWeek` accepts an optional per-lane `ratePerMile` under the same plausibility rails (finite, > 0, ≤ 20 when present).
- **All-freight coverage list (`NO_INDEX_LANES`, server/src/marketPulse.ts)**: oversize / heavy haul FIRST (the lane x named), then step-deck, tanker, power only, hotshot — each rendered on the card under "Every other lane — no weekly index" with its plain-words reason and a "No published rate" marker, and NO number of its own. The two open-deck lanes (oversize, step-deck) carry a labelled pointer to the closest published context — "Closest published context: Flatbed $3.31/mi" — rendered from the week's actual flatbed rate and dropped automatically when that rate is unpublished. Required label, source line, week label, and ~2-week staleness rules are unchanged; placement unchanged (Today + top of Loads, never Drive).
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite 1016 pass / 0 fail (1010 baseline, +6 net new: server marketPulse +5 — Week 43 per-lane rates exact + key set, coverage list order/numberlessness/context pointers, null-rate week storable, implausible rates rejected; client marketPulse +3 net — rows carry exact rate labels, null-rate direction-only render, coverage model with flatbed context + context-drop rule; the two superseded "no per-equipment $/mi" assertions were replaced by the exact-rate assertions). Client + server typecheck clean (build). NOT phone-proven. NOT shared/published.

The first audit of the all-freight build flagged two coverage gaps; both were checked against the source before any change. (1) SPECIALIZED: Truckstop/FTR's system tracks a FOURTH segment beyond the three principal types — its fuller weekly releases publish a Specialized rate (Week 39, ending Oct 2, 2026: +0.7% to $3.42/mi, +36.4% YoY — https://www.ajot.com/news/truckstop-ftr-spot-rates-rose-last-week-breaking-seasonal-patterns ; corroborated for Week 35 at $3.34 via the FTR release summary).
