## Addendum 2026-11-05 — DOCKED GANTRY SIGN BOARD (placards) on the Drive screen

X's order 2026-11-05 ~08:33 CT: "Build it — docked board + night dim" — the Rand McNally-style sign placards from the approved previews (~/workspace/tourbuild/placard-preview/), a named Nov 13 freeze exception. Pure client work: no schema change, no new server action, no new external source.

- **DATA (verified live before building)**: a real truck-costing request to the app's own routing server (valhalla1.openstreetmap.de, Birmingham AL run) established that the sign data rides ONLY in each maneuver's structured `sign` object — `exit_number_elements` / `exit_branch_elements` (with `consecutive_count`) / `exit_toward_elements` / `exit_name_elements` — while the flat `exit_*` fields the app parsed come back EMPTY. `buildRouteOption` now parses the sign object into `Maneuver.sign` (placard.ts `ManeuverSignData`) and backfills the flat exit fields from it, so the whole prompt family gets the data the server actually sends. The same live response proved the routing data carries NO lane information anywhere — so `Maneuver.lanes` exists structurally but is null on every real route, and the board renders WITHOUT its lane strip rather than inventing lanes (x's rule). Lane arrows + the "Keep right — N lanes exit" caption render only from a real lane record, whenever a source provides one.
- **BOARD (new pure module client/src/placard.ts + component client/src/SignPlacard.tsx)**: `buildPlacard` boards exit-family maneuvers (ramps 17–19, exits 20–21, stay/keep 22–24, or any maneuver carrying exit data / a complete sign) that carry sign data; merges (38–39) and plain turns NEVER board (x's mini-strip rule), and a maneuver with no sign data returns null → the existing one-line strip + 5-second card rules stand untouched. Staging (x's locked numbers): slim approach strip (~15dvh) from 1.0 mi to 0.3 mi, full board inside 0.3 mi, collapsed the instant guidance advances past the point; one tap shrinks to the mini strip until the next maneuver. The board REPLACES the Go map's turn strip and the 5-second sign/exit cards while it is up (gated also by the driver's Big prompt cards overlay choice — off there, everything behaves as before). Voice is untouched: every spoken prompt reads signPrompt/guidance as before; the board adds no audio. The exit arrow is a clean stroked SVG curve (the preview's chunky amber arrow was mock-grade, per x's build note).
- **DOCKED, NOT FLOATING**: the board docks to the top edge of the map wrapper; its measured height (ResizeObserver on the dock) is reserved through the new map-adapter `setCameraPaddingTop` (MapLibre viewport padding), so follow-camera framing puts the puck and the road ahead in the unobstructed area below the board. Reserve collapses to 0 when the board leaves.
- **NIGHT DIM**: the board wears placard.ts's night palette after dark — judged by the real sun at the truck via mapPrefs' `resolveMapDayNight("auto", …)` (device clock before a fix), re-judged every minute; the driver's basemap Day/Night pin does not govern it.
- **SMART CHIPS (approach strip only, real data only)**: weigh chip = the next weigh station ahead ON the route (hazard layer + driver-added stations, "Not here" suppressions honored) whose road-intel status is currently known under weighStation.ts's staleness rule (reports older than 4 h read unknown → chip omitted); bridge chip = the next bridge ahead whose clearance the map data actually tags — queryHazards now also collects EVERY tagged clearance (the hazard list still keeps only too-low ones) — read against the truck profile height, clear ✓ / TOO LOW. Either feed silent → its chip is omitted.
- **PERMIT VARIANT (Diamond-gated, same gate as Permit Mode)**: while `permitOfferFor` returns an active filed permit with an approved route AND the truck is not driving that permit's matched corridor (permitNavId ≠ permit) AND the board's exit/branch is verified absent from the docket's printed route (`permitExitVerdict` — the maneuver's refs/destinations matched against the docket's route steps/places; an exit the permit names, or one unmatchable either way, keeps the green board with no permit claim), a boarded maneuver renders the red variant — "EXIT NOT ON YOUR APPROVED ROUTE" + "Permit route stays on [route]" with the road parsed from the docket's own printed route lines (general line when the docket names no route token). On the corridor, its maneuvers ARE the approved route and the board stays green; off Diamond, no variant ever renders. Known limit, stated plainly: the variant keys on the board's decision points (signed exits/branches) and judges them against the docket's printed route TEXT — without the corridor loaded there is no geometry to judge a plain turn against, and none is claimed.
- **PRESERVED**: all existing Drive behavior — voice prompts, road intel, weigh-station prompts, bridge proximity alerts, reroute + scan-ahead, Fuel Coach, the see-through layout and per-driver GPS screen settings, the parked SignCheckPanel, the Drive-mode panel cards. Help copy updated (Ask Meca sign_check destination + SignCheckPanel) to describe the board.
- **Proof**: builder-verified — full suite 1050 pass / 0 fail (1018 baseline + 32 new placard tests: sign parse incl. state-route refs, honesty gates — no-data/turn/merge nulls, lane null-until-real + caption arithmetic, stage boundaries + tap-shrink, chip omission rules + exact preview figures 14'2"/13'6", permit stay-line parse + no-invention fallback, night palette is a true dimming). Client typecheck clean. NOT phone-proven — x's drive is the real proof. NOT shared/published.

## Gap-report updates (2026-11-05, x: "All that's needed")

The trucker-needs vs STONEMILES gap report's update list, executed. Test suite run for real first: 479 pass / 0 fail baseline; 486 pass / 0 fail after (7 new pins in `client/src/gapUpdates.test.ts`).

- **OFFLINE MAPS — COMPLETED (was a silent half-feature)**: the offline route download warmed OpenStreetMap raster tiles (`tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png`, corridor zooms 9–13) into the browser cache, but the MapLibre vector engine never requested them — in a dead zone the route line survived (geometry is in localStorage) while the basemap went blank. Fix: `mapEngine.ts` gained an `"offline"` base style — a plain raster style over the SAME tile template the download warms, so what was downloaded is what the map draws. App wiring: when the connection drops (`offline` event) with ≥1 saved offline route on the device, the basemap auto-switches to the offline layer (banner says so; a home-screen pill shows while it's on) and restores the live map on reconnect; a driver who switches back by hand while offline is not overridden until the next reconnect. Manual switch in Settings → Offline maps ("Show the offline map now" / "Back to the live map", disabled until a route is saved). Tiles outside a saved corridor stay blank — stated in the switch's banner as the honest edge of what was saved. The download banner now says the map switches to the saved tiles by itself when signal drops.
- **VOICE CONTROL**: Ask Meca gained a mic (🎙️ beside the input). Device speech recognition (same pattern as Explore voice search) transcribes into the exact `sendMeca` path — spoken questions get the same answers, spoken commands execute ("use route 2", brand toggles), and spoken "where…" questions get the same gated "Take me there" jumps. A listening status line names example commands; browsers without speech recognition get a plain banner, never a dead button.
- **DETENTION PAY — THE FEATURE HAS ITS NAME**: the sealed detention tracker is now named Detention Pay everywhere it surfaces — the per-load panel header ("⏱️ Detention Pay — sealed detention tracker"), the exported report ("DETENTION PAY REPORT"), the chat tracking snapshot line, and the post-trip debrief. Ask Meca gained a real `detention` destination (resolver + `MECA_DEST_INFO` blurb + `goMecaDest` jump to the Load Tracker, scrolling to the active load's tracker panel `detention-pay-<loadId>`), and the local answer computes live totals from the sealed arrival/departure events: open clocks, hours beyond the driver's free-time allowance, and the estimate at the driver's own set rate — labelled estimate-for-claims, as everywhere else.
- **TOMTOM SEARCH RUNG — SETTLED IN THE OPEN**: the rung's state was invisible (key not entitled to the Search product → server breaker trips → free chain quietly serves). Settings gained a "🔎 Place search — TomTom" section reading `getTomTomStatus`: searchDisabled states plainly that TomTom place search is OFF, why (Search not enabled on the account key), that the free sources (OpenStreetMap + ArcGIS) serve real labelled results meanwhile, that live traffic is a separate TomTom product and stays on, and that the app picks TomTom Search up on its own if the entitlement lands — plus the session's TomTom call counts. ON and Basic wordings cover the other states. Per x's 2026-11-05 ruling, paid data feeds (live fuel prices, live parking, any TomTom purchase) wait for app revenue — this change buys nothing and promises nothing; it only makes the current state visible.
- **NOT DONE HERE (not code)**: the phone-drive proof (reroute speed, limit chip, traffic painting, weather chip) still needs a real drive; the ELD link still needs one real hookup verified. Load board supply is unchanged (zero loads — supply, not software).

## Maneuver side geometry check (2026-11-05, x's field report: "Mis information on place card")

- **THE REPORT**: the docked sign board (place card) read "Turn left to take the I 10 ramp" with a left arrow — the ramp was on the driver's RIGHT, and the follow-on guidance then called for the left lane at an exit that also ran the other way. Everything else checked out.
- **DIAGNOSIS**: every guidance surface took a maneuver's side from the routing response's type code / narrative alone (`laneSideOf` type-first rule; the board's arrow, the sign prompt's lane label, the spoken lines, and the verbatim instruction text all read the same fields), and nothing ever checked that word against the road. Live replay at build time (Valhalla truck routes, driver's own corridor): the narrative usually agrees with the shape — the genuine left-turn ramp onto I-10 at Theodore Dawes Rd measures ~66° LEFT in the returned shape, so "Turn left to take the I 10 ramp" is correct THERE — but the router derives a ramp's side from the turn angle at its chosen node, which at ramp forks / collectors can sit past where the ramp presented to the driver, naming the opposite side. Proven live in the other direction too: a Birmingham I-65/US-280 fork where Valhalla narrates "Keep left at the fork" (type 24) while its own shape turns ~55° RIGHT — the check catches it and corrects it.
- **THE FIX (`client/src/maneuverSide.ts`, wired once in `buildRouteOption`)**: at route build, each maneuver's stated side (type code first, then its own wording) is checked against the signed turn of the route's OWN shape at the maneuver's `begin_shape_index` (incoming bearing over the last ~50 m of shape, outgoing over the next ~80 m, segment-interpolated; per-leg shape bases account for the section-stitch point dedupe). On a CLEAR contradiction (shape ≥15° the other way; ≥30° to override a "straight" ramp/keep; U-turn swings >150° never judged) the shape wins: the type code is mirrored to its same-family other-side twin (so the arrow, lane label, spoken lane, split/board family gates all agree) and the direction word in the instruction + verbal text is swapped in the router's own direction phrases only (verb phrases, "on the left/right", "<side> lane", "the <side> exit/ramp" — road names are never rewritten; a "straight" maneuver's words claimed no side, so only its type is corrected). Near-straight, agreeing, or unmeasurable maneuvers pass through byte-identical. Because the correction lands on the Maneuver objects at build, the saved-route snapshot carries it through resume with no extra plumbing. The truck's heading is never consulted (on a bend it points the wrong way — the sign-check rule).
- **PROOF**: 18 new unit tests (`maneuverSide.test.ts`) incl. the driver's case (left-stated ramp over a rightward shape → type + text + verbal corrected), the follow-on keep-left lane call, the real Theodore Dawes shape points as an agreement fixture (untouched), gentle-fork / curving-mainline / U-turn no-verdict pins, and road-name safety. Live sweep through the production function: 115 maneuvers across 10 real truck routes (Mobile corridor, Birmingham, Atlanta, Houston, Nashville) — 1 correction, the verified Birmingham contradiction; 0 false corrections. Full suite 504 pass / 0 fail. NOT phone-proven — x's next drive past a forked ramp is the real proof.

## Addendum 2026-11-05 — SELF-REPORTING + LIVE UPDATES (app health)

X's ask: "make the app completely AI controlled to where it will auto update and like it will notify itself through the AI to fix certain things… that way you can give me live updates." Approved shape (freeze exception, x's word): app reports → AI diagnoses + drafts fix → tests verify → X approves → ships through Play. NO self-rewriting code (Play bans downloaded executable code) and NO auto-shipping — x stays the human gate, stated in the panel copy itself.

- **STORAGE (migration 0099)**: `app_health_settings` (single-row self-reporting switch, default ON by x's approval; while OFF automatic reports are dropped server-side, manual reports still land; `last_check_at` stamps runs), `app_health_reports` (the failure inbox — fingerprint-unique, occurrences counted, status rides the fix pipeline, `diagnosis` + `fix_plan` JSON once drafted; sanitized error text + screen name ONLY — no location/document/personal column exists), `app_live_updates` (the owner's live feed: health checks, reports, diagnoses, pipeline moves, setting changes, each with its time).
- **PURE RULES (`server/src/appHealth.ts`, tested)**: `sanitizeReportText` strips emails / phone / SSN / card shapes / coordinate pairs / secret mentions and caps length (client twin in `client/src/appHealth.ts` strips before anything leaves the device; the server re-sanitizes regardless); `classifySeverity` (crash = critical; failure wording = warning; manual note = info); `fingerprintFor` (kind + screen + number-collapsed title — the same failure is one report, occurrences counted); `REPORT_TRANSITIONS` (open → diagnosed → fix_drafted → tested → APPROVED → shipped; approval is its own move nothing automatic can make; dismissed can reopen); `buildHealthChecks` reads REAL state only — sealed-chain verification result, stored EIA snapshot age/staleness, stored Market Pulse age/staleness, the TomTom search circuit-breaker state, perf-event failure rate from the app's own timings (no timings yet = honest "info", never a fake pass), and the open-report counts.
- **ACTIONS**: `reportAppIssue` (auto kinds dropped when the switch is off; dedupe-merge by fingerprint; a resolved/shipped failure that fires again REOPENS with a feed line saying so; sealed as `app_issue_reported`), `getAppHealth` (switch, last check, overall, checks, pipeline, inbox, feed — read-only, writes nothing), `runAppHealthCheck` (computes the board; failing/warning checks open or update reports — the open-reports check never reports on itself; a health-check report whose check now passes RESOLVES itself with a feed line; stamps `last_check_at`; sealed `app_health_check`; also the scheduled silent run — daily cron `trucker-gps-app-health-daily`), `setAppHealthSettings`, `diagnoseAppHealthReport` (ctx.inference drafts diagnosis + ≤6-step fix plan + risk from the sanitized report alone; failure leaves the report untouched; sealed `app_issue_diagnosed`), `updateAppHealthReportStatus` (transition-table enforced; every move narrated in the feed; sealed `app_issue_status`).
- **CLIENT WIRING**: `client/src/healthReporter.ts` installed once from `main.tsx` before first render — window `error` (script errors only; resource-load errors ignored) + `unhandledrejection` → `reportAppIssue`, gated by `SessionReportGate` (same failure once per session, ≤10 automatic reports per session, manual always allowed; the reporter never throws). The app root now carries `data-sm-screen={tab}` so a report names the screen it died on (tab name only, never a URL). Panel: `client/src/LiveUpdatesPanel.tsx` on the Driver tab under SI (`#live-updates`, Ask Meca `live_updates` destination wired) — switch, Run-health-check, the 6-step pipeline with the gate named, the live health board, the failure inbox (Diagnose with AI, per-status pipeline buttons, "Approve fix to ship" only at the tested step), a manual problem-report form, resolved/dismissed history with Reopen, and the live feed (60-second refetch while mounted). Copy holds the honesty lines verbatim: the app never rewrites its own code, never auto-ships, reports carry no location/personal data, no third-party tracker.
- **NOT IN SCOPE / UNCHANGED**: data feeds keep their own live channels (EIA weekly cron, market entries); no code-update mechanism was built into the app (impossible + banned); duty status, money, sealed records untouched by every action here.

## Addendum 2026-11-06 — ATLAS (state road reference; x's freeze-exception green light)

X's ask: "Green light" on a digital truckers' road atlas inside the app — one page per state, reachable from navigation and Ask Meca, working offline, built from public federal/state facts and NEVER copied from the Rand McNally Motor Carriers' Road Atlas (its tables, layout, and wording are its copyright; every page here is original copy and design).

- **DATA (`client/src/atlas.ts`, bundled + static — no server action, no feed, no per-request cost)**: all 50 states (`ATLAS_STATES`), each with DOT name, patrol/state-police name, and OPTIONAL note fields that default to null. The federal Interstate/National Network baseline prints on every page (`FEDERAL_WEIGHTS`: 80,000 lb GVW / 20,000 lb single axle / 34,000 lb tandem; `FEDERAL_WIDTH_IN` 102) — 23 CFR 658 / 23 U.S.C. 127 class facts. A state note exists ONLY for well-established public facts: MI 164,000 lb axle-table law + spring-thaw postings; ME & VT 100,000 lb federal Interstate exception; ID 105,500 lb designated-route program; NY divisible-load permits + NYC 55-ft overall-length cap; CA 40-ft / FL 41-ft kingpin rules; MN/ND/SD/WI spring-thaw restrictions; clearance traps NC Durham "Can Opener" (11'8" → raised 2019, posted 12'4"), NY car-only parkways, MA Boston Storrow Drive, IL Chicago viaducts (qualitative, no invented heights). Every entry without a confident state note prints the federal baseline under `FEDERAL_STANDARD_LABEL` ("Federal standard — verify state specifics before you roll."). Phone numbers print ONLY for verified published lines — Caltrans 1-800-427-7623, CHP non-emergency 1-800-835-5247, TxDOT 1-800-558-9368, FHP *347, GSP *477, MSHP *55, OSHP #677 — everywhere else omitted with the reason stated on the page (never guessed); 511 noted as the near-universal travel-info line. `atlas.test.ts` PINS this: a phone or gross-weight number may only ever appear for the vetted short lists, so a future edit can't silently add a plausible number.
- **NO SECOND COPIES**: weigh/inspection stations are NOT re-listed in the Atlas — the state page hands off to the app's one existing station list (OSM stations + driver-added, driver-reported OPEN/CLOSED on Drive → Alerts near you; same landing as Ask Meca's `weigh_station_status`). Low clearances likewise: past the famous-trap notes, the page hands off to the Drive map's real OSM maxheight tags (Explore). Atlas data is presented as a static planning reference, never as live; the live layer stays the GPS's.
- **UI (`client/src/AtlasPanel.tsx`)**: a sheet in the app's design language — Atlas home (standing label banner, federal-baseline card, state search, all-50 list with "State notes inside / Federal standard" markers) and per-state pages (weights, length & width, clearances, stations hand-off, contacts with tap-to-call only where a number is verified). The standing label `ATLAS_REFERENCE_NOTE` ("Reference only — posted signs, your permit, and your ELD govern.") prints on the home AND every state page. Reachable from the More tab list (always-visible row; the More always-visible count went 5 → 6 so no existing row was pushed behind the expander) and the ☰ app menu. ALL TIERS — a core driver safety reference, never paywalled (same standing as manual load entry).
- **ASK MECA**: new `atlas` destination end-to-end — resolver rules (`\batlas\b` anywhere; named state + rules topic like "weight limit in Michigan" / "Texas DOT number"), `MECA_DEST_INFO` blurb, `MecaNav.atlasState` carries the named state's code through `buildMecaNav` into `goMecaDest(dest, nav)` so "open the Texas atlas page" lands on Texas (the chat's Take-me-there call now passes the nav through), the appDestinations snapshot + the server askMeca navigate description both name the destination and its facts (model fallback validated against the same id list), and `localMecaAnswer` answers state-rule content questions straight from the bundled data offline (`atlasStateSummary` — baseline first, reference label last). Voice goes through the same sendMeca path untouched.
- **PROOF**: builder-verified — full suite 523 pass / 0 fail (511 baseline + 12 new atlas tests); client + server typecheck clean. NOT phone-proven — x's phone gives the real verdict, as always.

## Addendum 2026-11-06 (later) — ENHANCED MAP VIEW: satellite + tilt as one removable feature (migration 0100)

X's ask: GPS graphics like the reference photo (Google Maps satellite + tilted view) — "Build it now", then refined: "With optional removal." The satellite/hybrid Esri base styles and the Go face's 55° lean ALREADY existed (map-customization + MapLibre builds); this build turns them into the brief's complete feature: an on-map Map/Satellite toggle, a driver tilt control, the removal switch, and honest offline fallback. NO Google Maps tiles or SDK anywhere — imagery stays Esri World Imagery (tiles at `https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}`, verified live 200 image/jpeg in the earlier map build), with the source attribution `Imagery © Esri, Maxar, Earthstar Geographics` carried in the style (MapLibre attribution control).

- **PREFS (migration 0100, `app_settings.map_enhanced_view` + `map_tilt_mode`)**: `mapEnhancedView` boolean default TRUE (the feature ships on), `mapTiltMode` `auto|flat|tilted` default `auto`. Server round-trip in `server/src/mapPrefs.ts` (legacy row reads ON/auto; only an explicit stored false removes; bogus tilt reads auto), zod shape in `appSettingsShape`, client mirror in `client/src/mapPrefs.ts` (`enhancedMap`, `tiltMode`, normalize keeps feature unless explicit false). All display-only — routing, alerts, and data never consult either.
- **PURE LOGIC (`client/src/mapPrefs.ts`, unit-tested)**: `effectiveBaseStyle` (enhanced OFF → drawn style is Streets; the STORED `baseStyle` is never rewritten, so ON restores the driver's satellite choice exactly), `effectivePitchDeg` (OFF → 0 everywhere; flat → 0; tilted → `DRIVE_TILT_PITCH_DEG` = 55 everywhere; auto → Go 55 / Explore 0, the shipped faces — pitch is style-independent, so tilt leans streets, satellite, hybrid, and the offline base alike), `tiltActive`/`nextTiltMode` (the rail button flips from the CURRENT camera: flat → pin "tilted", leaned → pin "flat"), `isSatelliteStyle`, `satelliteFallbackActive` (chosen satellite/hybrid AND (offline OR offline base on) AND enhanced on), `offlineAutoSwitchApplies` (the shipped saved-route rule OR the satellite rule).
- **UI**: (1) Drive map bottom-left quick controls, rendered ONLY while enhanced is ON — a Map/Satellite segmented pill (writes the same persisted `mapBaseStyle` as Layers; Hybrid stays a Layers choice, and picking Satellite on the map sets plain satellite) + a Tilt button ("Tilt view" ⇄ "Tilted — tap for flat", aria-pressed = the real camera state). Bottom-left sits above the traffic-legend/radar stack (bottom 46px + the embedded-view reserve); the speed bubble keeps its own higher spot. (2) Layers & display: while ON, the style group gains the satellite streaming note (or the fallback status), plus a Tilted driving view Auto/Flat/Tilted segmented control; while OFF, the whole style/tilt block collapses to a plain note — no satellite buttons anywhere. (3) Settings → Appearance carries the "Enhanced map view" switch with plain copy: ON explains satellite streams + falls back honestly; OFF states the feature is REMOVED, not paused (Satellite choice gone, Tilt button gone, camera flat, plain Streets map) and the stored choices are kept and return with it. (4) Ask Meca gains the `satellite_view` destination (resolver: satellite/aerial/tilt/enhanced-map wording → Drive map; blurb carries the full honest story). Guidance overlays never tilt — only the MapLibre camera pitch changes; turn cards, placards, LIMIT/weather chips, and banners are flat DOM overlays by construction.
- **OFFLINE HONESTY**: satellite tiles stream — on signal loss with a satellite style chosen, the offline-base auto-switch now engages EVEN WITH NO SAVED ROUTE (`offlineAutoSwitchApplies`), the banner names the satellite fallback ("Signal lost — satellite photos need a connection, so the map switched to the offline map. Satellite comes back on its own when signal returns."), and while `satelliteFallbackActive` no surface claims satellite: the quick toggle's Map side reads selected and its Satellite side shows 📴 with a fallback aria-label, the Layers style group shows no style as pressed and prints the fallback status ("Satellite is never available offline"), and the Drive panel's offline pill switches to the satellite wording. Reconnect restores the live style automatically (the existing auto-off path). Regular map + tilt keep working fully offline — pitch is a camera move over the stored-tile base.
- **TIERS**: available on ALL tiers — core driving display like the rest of the Drive screen (no gate added; the map prefs were never tier-gated).
- **PROOF**: builder-verified — full suite 1131 pass / 0 fail (1112 baseline + 19 new: client mapPrefs enhanced-view/tilt/fallback describes + server round-trip describe; two pre-existing exact-shape fixtures updated for the new fields, one source-pin in gapUpdates re-pointed at the intact `effectiveMapStyle` line after `drawnMapStyle` split the engine hand-off from it); client + server typecheck clean. NOT phone-proven — x's phone gives the real verdict, as always.

## Addendum — Trailer Package (edit, 2026-11-06; x's green light, Nov 13 freeze exception)
- **Owner ask**: build the per-trailer package proposed in chat — trailer profiles as the backbone, then reefer cold-chain log, securement calculator, loaded-height tracker, bridge-formula axle calculator, dock helper, high-wind alerts, and per-trailer pre-trip checklists — without regressing the satellite/tilt enhanced map view, the Atlas, offline maps, placards, weigh-station prompts, Fuel + Stop Planner, Market Pulse, Permit Mode, or Live Updates.
- **PROFILE (the backbone)**: `profile.trailer_type` (migration 0101, default `dry_van`) extends the EXISTING truck-dimension profile row — same `getProfile`/`saveProfile` path, no parallel system. One active type per truck: `dry_van | reefer | flatbed | step_deck | rgn`, switchable any time (Settings → Truck picker, or the picker atop Loads → Trailer tools, which saves immediately through the same saveProfile mutation). Pure logic in `client/src/trailer.ts`: per-type typical empty height (13'6" all — tractor-dominated; open decks also carry deck heights 60"/42"/24"), high-profile flag (dry van + reefer), open-deck flag, and the tool order each type surfaces first. Nothing is hidden — tools outside a profile's order sit below under "More trailer tools".
- **REEFER COLD-CHAIN LOG**: per load — `loads.reefer_setpoint_f` / `reefer_band_f` (driver-set ± band, default 5°F) via `setLoadTrailerData`; check-ins in `reefer_checks` via `addReeferCheck`, each sealed at write time into the load's hash chain (`reefer_check` event, payload stamped `source: "driver-entered"`) with timestamp + GPS when a fix exists. Every surface labels temps DRIVER-ENTERED — the app has no reefer sensor feed and never presents them as sensor readings. Out-of-band checks warn live (before sealing) and in the sealed list. `listLoads` returns the checks with their load, and the load paperwork export prints setpoint/band + every check with the driver-entered label.
- **SECUREMENT CALCULATOR**: `securementCalc` / `minTiedowns393110` in trailer.ts implement the published general-freight rules verbatim — 49 CFR 393.110(b) unblocked (≤5 ft & ≤1,100 lb → 1; ≤5 ft & >1,100 lb → 2; >5–10 ft → 2; >10 ft → 2 + 1 per additional 10 ft or fraction) and 393.110(c) blocked (1 per 10 ft or fraction), plus 49 CFR 393.106(d) aggregate WLL ≥ 50% of article weight, counting indirect tiedowns at full WLL and direct at half. Verified 2026-11-06 against the CFR text at https://www.law.cornell.edu/cfr/text/49/393.110 (the 48-ft → 6 tiedowns cross-check matches). Labelled "planning guide — not legal advice" in the UI and in every result's notes; special-commodity sections (§§393.116–393.136: logs, coils, paper rolls, pipe, autos, boulders, lumber, containers) are declared OUT OF SCOPE rather than guessed, and missing length/weight returns an explicit out-of-scope answer with no number.
- **LOADED-HEIGHT TRACKER**: `loads.loaded_height_in` per load via `setLoadTrailerData` (recording/clearing seals a `loaded_height_recorded` chain event on the load). While set on the active load, THAT height drives both low-bridge alert paths (the Overpass hazard scan and the Sign Placard bridge chip) via `heightInUse` in trailer.ts; the truck profile height is the fallback and every surface labels which height is in use ("loaded height on this load" vs "truck profile height"). The per-load prompt lives in the load's Trailer tools for open-deck profiles/families; the height-in-use line also prints for enclosed loads. Routing (Valhalla costing) still uses the profile dimensions — the brief scoped the loaded height to alerts.
- **AXLE WEIGHT & SPACING (BRIDGE FORMULA)**: `bridgeFormulaCalc` in trailer.ts — W = 500 × [(L·N)/(N−1) + 12N + 36] rounded to the nearest 500 lb (23 CFR 658.17 / 23 U.S.C. 127), single-axle cap 20,000 lb, tandem cap 34,000 lb for two axles ≤8 ft apart (a wider spread is formula-governed — the 10-ft spread-axle 40,000 lb case checks out), gross cap 80,000 lb. Formula + caps cross-checked 2026-11-06 (e.g. https://gearloads.com/calculators/federal-bridge-formula-calculator and the FHWA table values: 3 axles @ 16 ft → 48,000; 5 @ 51 ft → 80,000). Labelled planning guidance; sits beside Permit Mode and does not alter it. Out-of-range inputs answer out-of-scope instead of guessing.
- **DOCK HELPER**: per load — `loads.dock_appt_at` / `dock_door` / `lumper_fee` via `setLoadTrailerData`; the lumper receipt files through the existing blob plumbing via `attachLumperReceipt` (SHA-256 fingerprinted, `lumper_receipt_attached` chain event; `getLumperReceipt` serves the view link) — same pattern as load documents, no new storage path. Fields ride the load paperwork export.
- **HIGH-WIND ALERTS**: `windAdvisory` in trailer.ts — fires only when ALL hold: trailer profile is high-profile (dry van / reefer), the trailer is running empty (no active load, active load not yet picked up, or delivered-sealed), and gusts reach the labelled 40 mph advisory threshold. It reads the Go weather chip's OWN Open-Meteo reading (the chip reports its "here" reading up via a new optional `onReading` callback — no second fetch) and renders as an amber banner stacked over the chip on the Go face. Text labels itself "STONEMILES wind advisory — not an official warning" and names the threshold.
- **PRE-TRIP CHECKLISTS**: per-type item sets in trailer.ts (common coupling/lights/tires/brakes set + reefer unit/setpoint/fuel/battery/chute for reefer, deck/winches/straps/rub rail/rack/tarp for flatbed, ramps for step deck, neck/hydraulics/booster for RGN, doors/seal/walls for van). The driver marks OK/defect per item with defect notes, attaches ONE optional photo for the checklist (blob + SHA-256), and seals via `savePretripInspection` into `pretrip_inspections` + a `pretrip_inspection` chain event (refKind load when filed against the active load, else driver). Recent sealed inspections list under the checklist. This is the trailer package's own checklist — the existing DVIR machinery is untouched.
- **ASK MECA**: new `trailer_tools` destination with full blurb, resolver patterns (reefer/securement/tiedowns/bridge formula/loaded height/dock/lumper/pre-trip/blow-over/trailer profile), a nav case that opens Loads with the Trailer tools panel expanded (`trailerToolsNonce`), destinations-string coverage, snapshot lines (profile type, height in use, running-empty, active-load reefer setpoint/band/checks with the driver-entered label, dock fields), and local answers from live data for "what's my setpoint / last reefer check", "what height am I running", and "what trailer am I pulling".
- **TIERS**: all trailer tools are core working-driver tools on EVERY plan, following the app's existing pattern (like the satellite view) — nothing gated, nothing here moves money. No tier exception to report.
- **FILES**: new `client/src/trailer.ts` + `client/src/trailer.test.ts` (18 tests), new `client/src/TrailerPanel.tsx` (tools panel, per-load section, wind banner), migration 0101, schema + actions additions, surgical App.tsx wiring (profile field, settings picker, effective height, Loads-tab mounts, Go-face banner, Meca, export lines), one optional-callback prop added to GoWeatherChip.tsx. Satellite/tilt and Atlas code paths untouched.
- **PROOF**: builder-verified — full suite 1149 pass / 0 fail (1131 post-satellite baseline + 18 new trailer tests); client + server typecheck clean; sandbox action round-trips verified (profile trailerType save/read incl. switching, setLoadTrailerData, addReeferCheck sealed + returned with the load, savePretripInspection sealed with chain hash + defect count). NOT phone-proven — x's phone gives the real verdict, as always.

## Addendum — Money & Flow batch (edit, 2026-11-06, owner green light "All is good"; Nov 13 freeze exception)
- Scope: seven tools built in their own modules (`client/src/moneyMath.ts` pure logic + tests, `client/src/MoneyFlow.tsx` panels, migration 0102 + actions in actions.ts); satellite/tilt, Atlas, and the Trailer Package untouched (full suite 1149 → 1169 pass, 0 fail).
- (1) Document expiry wallet: extends the EXISTING personal_docs vault — new kind `dot_inspection` added to savePersonalDoc's enum + DOC_KINDS so the five required documents are CDL / medical / registration / insurance / annual DOT inspection. Wallet panel (Driver tab) shows per-kind status with alert tiers at 30 / 14 / 7 days + expired (`docAlertTier` in moneyMath.ts, matching the server's ceil daysLeft), a warning strip at the TOP of the Driver tab whenever any doc is ≤30 days or expired, and a quick-add expiry form over the same records. In-app alerts only — copy says so; no push is claimed. Saving a document still passes through the vault's existing Gold gate (its plain error surfaces unchanged).
- (2) Who owes me: new `load_payments` table (driver-recorded payments per load, sealed as `load_payment` chain events; removal seals too). `getOwedBoard` returns POD-sealed (delivered) loads only, freight = rate + extraCharges, plus sealed claim totals, minus recorded payments; the client board (moneyMath `buildOwedBoard`/`owedTotals`) drops a load when payments cover the total, sorts oldest-first, and buckets aging 0–14 / 15–30 / 31–60 / 60+. A delivered load with NO rate on file stays on the board with "amount not on file" (excluded from dollar totals) until any payment is recorded against it. Records only — no invoicing, no money movement.
- (3) True rate: `loads.deadhead_miles` (migration 0102, driver-recorded via saveLoad's new optional field, edited per load in the True Rate panel). loadShape now also carries `claimsTotal` (sealed claim sum). True rate = (rate + extraCharges + claims) ÷ (loaded + deadhead). Net-after-fuel: fuel EXPENSES filed against the load (expenses.loadId, category fuel) = "logged"; otherwise an estimate = total miles ÷ profile MPG × gallon-weighted average price of the driver's logged fuel purchases, labelled "ESTIMATE — from your logged fuel prices + MPG, not logged for this load" everywhere it renders; no logged price basis → no net figure at all. Before-booking decisions keep using the existing Worth-It Advisor (same inputs).
- (4) TONU + layover logger: new `claim_events` table (tonu | layover | driver_assist, amount DRIVER-entered — never suggested), sealed as `claim_event` chain events with refKind=load (or driver when standalone — a TONU often has no load), listed in the load's sealed timeline, counted in load totals + the owed board, and exported via `exportMoneyFlow` (claims + payments text) alongside the per-record exports. Detention Pay's separate arrival/departure system is untouched; the panel copy says they sit beside each other.
- (5) IFTA estimator: the existing IFTA Complete panel/formula (miles by state from sealed trips, fuel by state, fleet MPG, net = (miles ÷ MPG) × rate − bought × rate) now runs on the REAL published table — IFTA, Inc. fuel tax matrix, **4th quarter 2026 (Oct–Dec 2026), matrix dated October 15, 2026, updated November 5, 2026, not final until December 4, 2026** (source: https://www.iftach.org/taxmatrix4/Taxmatrix.php — verified 2026-11-06 against the official matrix page and an Oklahoma Q3 return; CA 0.9790, PA 0.7410, IL 0.7380, IN 0.6300, TX 0.2000). This REPLACED the prior "October 2026"-labelled snapshot, several of whose values no longer matched the published matrix (e.g. CA was 0.89). KY (0.2200 + 0.1050) and VA (0.3360 + 0.1430) carry their surcharge folded in, labelled in the UI/export. Oregon 0 (weight-mile tax, matrix prints no fuel rate). Alaska, Hawaii, and DC are NOT IFTA members and were REMOVED from the table — a quarter touching them now reads "rate not loaded" (panel + exports) and stays out of totals. Canadian provinces added at the matrix's US$/gal equivalents. Panel carries the standing label "Estimate for planning only — not a filing", rates source + as-of printed from the server (ratesSource/ratesDate).
- (6) Maintenance countdown: new `maintenance_services` table (item UNIQUE: oil | tires | brakes | dot_inspection; driver-set interval + last-done odometer/date). `getMaintenance` derives the current odometer as the HIGHEST reading the app already records — fuel purchase odometers, DVIR odometers, daily-log start/finish miles — and names the source; the panel also offers a display-only dash check that rewrites nothing. Defaults offered (oil 25,000 mi; tires 100,000; brakes 100,000; DOT = 12-month date rule at interval 0) are labelled defaults the driver can change; states ok / due-soon (≤1,000 mi or ≤30 days) / due / overdue / unknown (no baseline → the panel says what's missing instead of guessing).
- (7) Breakdown mode: the existing Drive-screen BreakdownSheet (nearest repair/towing from Overpass map data, coordinates, share card) stays the help-now half and the new panel links straight to it + the parking planner. New `breakdown_logs` table: start (sealed `breakdown_log_open`), cost entries (sealed `breakdown_cost`), finish (sealed `breakdown_log_close` with downtime hours); one open log at a time enforced server-side. `exportBreakdownLog` renders the warranty/claim text (down/up, downtime, cost lines, total, seal hash, the standing "logs and locates — does not dispatch or pay anyone" line). Records only.
- (8) Ask Meca: seven new destinations (doc_expiry, owed_board, true_rate, claims_log, ifta, maintenance, breakdown_log) in the type union + MECA_DEST_INFO + resolver rules (placed before the generic documents/trips/pay rules) + goMecaDest jumps to the Driver-tab anchors. Live-data local answers for "who owes me money" (owed-board query), "when does my med card expire" (+ CDL/registration/insurance/DOT variants, personal-docs query), "what do I owe IFTA this quarter" (current-quarter getIftaReport, same cache key as the panel), and maintenance state — all from recorded data, honest when empty. buildMecaContext gains a `moneyFlow` section (owed rows, wallet docs with plain-words states, maintenance + odometer source, current-quarter IFTA with rates source/as-of) so the server model answers from the same records.
- (9) Tiers: all seven tools are working driver tools on EVERY plan (Trailer Package pattern). The one pre-existing gate that touches the batch is the document vault's Gold gate on savePersonalDoc — unchanged, and its error message surfaces in the wallet form; expiry READS (wallet, badge, Ask Meca) are ungated. No money movement anywhere in the batch.
- Deliberately left out, for honesty: per-load fuel allocation from the raw fuel-purchase log (purchases carry no load link, so window-matching would fabricate precision — the estimate path labels itself instead); push notifications for expiry (the app has none); IFTA rates for non-member jurisdictions (none exist in the matrix); any claim-amount suggestion (the driver sets claim amounts).

## Multi-language support (2026-11-06, x's order) — builder-verified

- **Picker + persistence:** Settings → Appearance → Language (SegRow, native names: English / Español / Português / Русский / Українська). Stored on the existing per-account `app_settings.language` TEXT column (migration 0014 — no new migration; the column carries any code). Server changes: `appSettingsShape.language` widened from `["en","es"]` to `["en","es","pt","ru","uk"]`, and the `getAppSettings` read coercion accepts all five (previously anything but "es" collapsed to "en"). Applies immediately — the whole client re-renders off `appSettings.language`; `<html lang>` follows via `localeFor()`.
- **Framework:** `client/src/i18n.ts` (new, own module) + `client/src/i18n.test.ts` (23 tests). English is the source language. Two catalogues: PAIRS — keyed by the exact English source string, carrying pt/ru/uk (es lives at the legacy `tr(en, es)` call sites); the app's `tr()` helper now routes through `pairText(lang, en, esLiteral)`, so all 210 unique harvested tr() strings (verified mechanically against App.tsx: 210/210 covered, 0 stray keys) gained pt/ru/uk with no call-site changes. STRINGS — keyed catalogue (settings.*, meca.*, govern.*, console.*, trailer.*, money.*, records.*, common.*) for surfaces that never had a pair. Fallback chain everywhere: chosen language → English source → (keyed only) the key itself. A missing string renders English, never blank.
- **Coverage wired:** bottom dock, Today home, Drive screen cards/buttons (the tr() surfaces), More-tab launcher + subs, Settings (Appearance heading, font size, language picker + note), Owner Console sidebar sections + header, Ask Meca chrome (button, placeholder, Take-me-there + instead + locked lines), MECA destination path labels (DEST_LABELS, all 57 destinations × es/pt/ru/uk), Trailer tools (panel title, type chips, per-load section, reefer/securement/height/axle/dock/pre-trip headings), Money & Flow panel titles (expiry wallet, owed board, true rate, TONU, maintenance, breakdown), Pay Book / Money Truth / IFTA Complete / Worth-It / Settlement Checker titles, Records titles (scale tickets, document vault, DVIR). Voice: speech-recognition `rec.lang` and TTS utterance lang follow the choice.
- **Honesty boundaries:** legal/regulatory/safety-governing text keeps the English original on screen; the translation rides beneath with the govern.note line in the chosen language ("English text governs" × 5). Implemented at the Atlas standing label (ATLAS_REFERENCE_NOTE stays English; translation + note below), the Permits panel (govern.permitsNote under the title; permit terms stay as issued), and the securement calculator's CFR paragraph (English + govern note). Sealed records/exports are never retro-translated (unchanged behaviour; the Settings note says so in all five languages). Units/values never convert: miles, °F, lbs stay; only date/time FORMATTING localizes (`localeFor`/`fmtDate`/`fmtDateTime` in i18n.ts).
- **Ask Meca per language:** (a) Navigation — `destForLocalizedQuery()` in i18n.ts: ordered phrase catalogues per language (~90 phrases each for es/pt/ru/uk covering the everyday destinations incl. owed board, expiry wallet, trailer tools, detention, per diem/Pay Book, IFTA, maintenance, breakdown), hooked at the top of `resolveMecaDest` and into the early nav-answer path, so "Take me there" parity holds in-language; destination labels on the button render via `destLabel()`. (b) Everyday answers — `askMeca` action gained a `language` request field; the server prompt instructs the model to answer in that language (plain driver register), keeping road-sign text, FMCSA/CFR citations, place/brand names in English, adding the English-governs sentence when resting on legal/safety wording, and never translating navigate/command ids. Verified live in-sandbox: a Russian question returned a Russian answer + navigate "loads". (c) Honest limits — Meca's long English destination blurbs (the where-it-lives explainers) stay English in the local-answer path (framing sentence + button + labels are translated); the Safety Academy's course content exists only in EN/ES, so pt/ru/uk fall back to the English original there rather than an unreviewed translation of safety-training material (call site maps language to "es"|"en").
- **Deliberately untranslated (fallback to English, by design):** long explanatory paragraphs outside the tr() catalogue (Settings descriptions, Plans sheet terms/pricing copy — pricing commitments stay English-governed), portal surfaces (Shipper/Broker/Team portals), Onboarding, LoadBoard/Board internals beyond their tr() strings, MECA blurbs (above), Safety Academy content for pt/ru/uk (above). All render the English original — the documented fallback, never a blank or a guess.
- **Translations provenance:** authored in-repo for this build (driver register; working vocabulary — detention/prostой/простій, lumper kept as a loanword with the fee glossed, deadhead as empty-miles with the term kept, setpoint glossed). No external translation service or fetched dataset was used.
- **Verification:** full suite 1192 pass / 0 fail (baseline 1169 + 23 new i18n tests); client + server typecheck clean; sandbox round-trip: saveAppSettings language "uk" → getAppSettings returns "uk". Preserved feature sets re-verified present after the edit: satellite/tilt + Settings removal switch (mapEnhancedView), Atlas, Trailer Package, Money & Flow batch, route weather, scale tickets, parking layer. NOTE: the Final Extras batch (receiver memory etc.) and the dashboard-cleanup pass were dispatched as QUEUED follow-on builds ("after your current turn finishes") and were not part of this build; receiver memory is not present in this artifact yet.

  (`settings.theme*`) in all five languages.

## Final Extras batch (2026-11-06, x's green light) — builder-verified

Pre-state found on inspection (correcting the earlier "never landed" read): route-ahead weather (item 1) and the parking planner already existed from the 2026-11-01 orders, and partial plumbing from the earlier Final Extras attempt WAS present — actions `getFacilityMemory` (detention aggregates), `logScaleTicket`/`listScaleTickets` (standalone, loadId always null from the UI), `reportSiWeatherAlert` (SI weather decisions). This build completed the batch on top of that plumbing without overwriting it.

- **(1) Route-ahead severe weather — already built, verified, one label added.** Corridor point checks from the app's existing weather source (Open-Meteo forecasts + NWS alerts, both public/keyless): here, ~25/60/120 mi ahead, destination, and tonight's stop at the HOS clock-out point (`routeWeather.ts` + `RouteWeatherPanel` on the Drive screen). Decision-grade findings only (ice/freezing rain, snow, thunderstorms, gusts over the driver's own threshold, near-zero visibility, severe/freeze at tonight's stop) surface as a Drive banner + voice (per alert settings) + SI decisions inbox via `reportSiWeatherAlert`, deduped per corridor segment per 6-hour window, with distance-to-impact in the text ("about N mi ahead") and a last-clear-stretch stop hint. ADVISORY labelling throughout. COVERAGE HONESTY: the source supports point checks, not continuous corridor sampling — this build added the explicit coverage line to the panel: point checks at the listed spots + NWS corridor alerts; stretches between points are not sampled and a clear list is not a promise for every mile.
- **(2) Receiver memory — completed.** Server: new `getFacilityVisits({facility})` action returning per-visit history from the driver's OWN sealed records only: detention dwell + past-free-time from sealed arrival/departure chain pairs, appointment behaviour (sealed arrival vs the load's recorded `dockApptAt`; null when no appointment is on file — never guessed), lumper fee + dock door from the load's dock helper, and the driver's own check-in note (new `loads.dock_note`, migration 0103, written in the dock helper via `setLoadTrailerData.dockNote`). No crowdsourced/scraped data, no other driver anywhere in the path. UI: the Worth-It Advisor's Facility Memory section now renders grouped visit history (`groupVisitsByFacility` in `client/src/extras.ts`) with last-visit detail; each load's dock helper (TrailerPanel `DockSection`) shows a "What to expect here" card for that load's pickup + delivery facilities, excluding the current load. Empty history renders the honest no-history line in all five languages.
- **(3) Scale ticket log + finder — completed.** Log: the ScalePanel now attaches a ticket to a load (load picker); `logScaleTicket` seals into the LOAD's chain record when a load is attached (refKind "load"; standalone tickets keep the driver seal), and `exportAuditPacket` prints a per-load SCALE TICKETS section (weights, location, date, photo SHA-256, "driver-entered"). Every surface labels the weights driver-entered. Finder: new Explore "Scales" chip querying the app's existing OpenStreetMap/Overpass place data — CAT Scale by brand, `amenity=weighbridge`, `highway=weigh_station`, and fuel/services tagged with scales; pins classify via `classifyScalePoi` (extras.ts) and the note labels it map data, locations only, no live status.
- **(4) Tax-ready expenses + per-diem counter — built.** Expense categories: `meals` + `supplies` added to the `logExpense` enum and the Money Truth form, completing the brief's set (fuel, maintenance, tolls, parking, meals, supplies, other) alongside the pre-existing lumper/insurance/scale/permits; the category that seals is the one the DRIVER confirms in the form (a scan may suggest, the driver confirms). New `server/src/taxYear.ts` (pure, tested): per-diem days = union of UTC calendar days touched by the driver's sealed trips for the year (no trips → zero; nothing estimated); expense totals grouped by the driver-confirmed category. Actions `getTaxYearSummary({year})` + `exportTaxYearSummary({year})` (locked-account guard + chain head, same pattern as the IFTA export; fuel purchases totalled separately so nothing double-counts). UI: new `TaxPanel` (Driver tab, after Scale Tickets) — year picker, per-diem day count, category totals, fuel line, download button. Labels, printed on the panel AND on the export packet: per-diem is an owner-operator/1099 deduction, W-2 company drivers cannot claim it (2017 tax law change); a record for tax prep, not tax advice; NO deduction amount is computed (no per-diem rate assumed anywhere).
- **(5) Night parking layer — built on the existing planner.** New Explore "Night Parking" chip: one map layer over the app's existing OpenStreetMap data (parking + rest areas + truck services), note-labelled map data with space counts as lot sizes, never live availability. Stop-planning flow: `StopPlannerSheet` gained a Night parking section — route candidates the map data tags with truck parking (`nightParkingPicks`, extras.ts: tagged-yes or a tagged count only; unknown/no are never recommended), nearest to the HOS clock-out point, each with a Route button, plus a door into the full Parking planner (the pre-existing ParkingSheet, which scans map-data rest areas/services around the truck and shows driver reports as reports). No spaces/free counts are shown anywhere — the app has no live parking feed (paid feeds stay parked until revenue, per x's 2026-11-05 rule).
- **(6) Ask Meca parity — built.** Five new destinations (`facility_memory`, `scale_tickets`, `scale_finder`, `tax_records`, `night_parking`): type union, MECA_DEST_INFO blurbs, resolver rules placed ahead of the generic detention/pay-book/parking rules they'd otherwise fall into, `goMecaDest` jumps (Loads→facility-memory anchor, Driver→scale-tickets / tax-records anchors, Drive→Explore with the Scales / Night Parking chip armed), DEST_LABELS + ordered NAV_PHRASES in es/pt/ru/uk. Live-data local answers: "how was detention at X last time" answers from the cached `getFacilityVisits` query (named-facility match via `facilityNameMatches`; averages + last visit incl. lumper/note; honest empty when no history), "how many per-diem days this year" from the cached `getTaxYearSummary` (count + W-2 label + expense totals), and "what's the weather ahead" via the pre-existing route-weather snapshot (`routeWeatherSummary` from the live panel fetch: corridor conditions with miles ahead + NWS alerts) in the weather branch.
- **(7) Tiers / boundaries:** all five tools are working driver tools on every plan (the app's standing pattern for driver tools); the Parking planner's pre-existing Gold gate is untouched (the Night Parking layer routes to Explore, which is ungated core map). No money movement, no external posting, no crowdsourcing anywhere in the batch.
- **Schema:** migration 0103 adds `loads.dock_note` only. Everything else rides existing tables (chain_log, loads dock fields, expenses, fuel_purchases, trips, scale_tickets).
- **i18n:** new STRINGS keys `extras.*` (tax title, per-diem days + the W-2 note, facility title/own-only/none, check-in note, driver-entered, scale-finder note, night parking title + note) in all five languages; DEST_LABELS for the five new destinations; NAV_PHRASES rows per language. New panel body copy follows the app's existing pattern (English in-component with keyed headings/labels, same as the Money & Flow panels).
- **Deliberately scoped down, for honesty:** route weather stays point-check coverage (no continuous corridor claim); facility appointment behaviour only where an appointment is actually on file; per-diem counts sealed trips only and computes no dollar figure; the scale finder and night-parking layer show map-data locations/counts only — no live status or availability is claimed anywhere.
- **(6b) Ask Meca context parity (audit fix pass, 2026-11-07):** driving the built app in the audit probe showed the per-diem question reaching the server model, whose snapshot (`buildMecaContext`) had no Final Extras section — it answered that no count existed while the deterministic nav correctly attached "Driver → Tax records". Fixed the same way the Money & Flow batch did it: `buildMecaContext` now carries a `finalExtras` section built from the same cached queries (facility visit groups with averages + last-visit detail incl. appointment behaviour and check-in note; the tax-year summary with the per-diem basis + W-2 label; scale-finder/tickets, night-parking, and route-weather coverage notes), so the primary server-model path answers from the same live records as the local fallback branches.

The More tab renders the groups (replacing the flat first-6 + "Everything else" fold; nothing is folded away now), and Today's new **Find a tool** panel renders the same five groups as jump rows (tap → More at that group → tool row = 2 taps from Today for every tool; dock More → tool row is also 2).

## Field-fix batch — drive-screen crowding, fuel pins, search typing, night route line (2026-11-06, x's field report with screenshots)

Four fixes from x's road screenshots (night driving, Semmes AL area). No schema change; one new server action; full suite 1232 pass / 0 fail, client + server typecheck clean.

- **GPS overcrowded / MPH hidden — fixed.** Root cause found in the render: the Nov 6 enhanced-map controls stacked two pills at `bottom:46px` in the left column, directly across the 92px speed bubble (bottom:8px, spans 8–100px) — the Map/Satellite pill and the "Tilted — tap for flat" pill covered the MPH digits (visible in x's screenshots). Fix: Map / Satellite / Tilt are now ONE slim (~41px) segmented row at `bottom:108px`, entirely above the bubble; the Go weather stack and the permit placard chips lift by the same 52px while the enhanced view is on (104→156px, 152→204px), so the left column reads bottom-up with no overlaps: speed bubble → view row → weather → permit chips. Nothing else about the controls changed (same settings, same aria labels, same banner copy).
- **Fuel stations not loading — fixed, two causes.** (1) Coverage: the Fuel chip's Overpass query asked for `node[amenity=fuel]` only; stations mapped as building/area ways or relations could never return. Fuel now queries `nwr[amenity=fuel]` (nodes + ways + relations, `out center` already handled the centroids). (2) Transport: the phone-only sequential Overpass failover is the same failure class the speed-limit ladder hit (2026-10-29): overpass-api.de refuses the phone webview's user-agent (HTTP 406 — verified during this fix: the identical query with a descriptive UA returns 200 with real fuel results near the truck; a bare UA gets 406) and mirror instances hang, so the chain could sit on "Loading pins…" ~80s and land nothing. New server rung mirrors `getSpeedLimit`: `server/src/explorePois.ts` (query builder + instance RACE, first healthy answer wins, losers aborted, 15s per-attempt cap, descriptive UA) behind the `getExplorePois` action; the client tries the server first and keeps its direct failover as rung 2, with both rungs' raw OSM elements shaped by the one existing pin path. Category query clauses are mirrored client/server and must stay in step. Tests: `server/src/explorePois.test.ts` (4 tests) pin the fuel nwr clause and the shared query shape.
- **Typed line below the search bar — fixed.** The Explore search input rendered at 14px (text-sm); iOS auto-zooms into any focused sub-16px input, and inside the map's fixed/absolute layout the zoom displaced the typed text below the bar. The input now wears text-base (16px at Standard; the Large/XLarge font settings only go bigger) plus `enterKeyHint="search"` — no iOS zoom, the typed line stays in the bar.
- **Night route line green — done, x's order.** New shared `routeLineColor` beside `mapNight`: the selected route line draws `#1a73e8` blue in the map's day look and `#3ddc84` grounded green in its night look (Day/Night setting resolved — Auto follows the real sun at the truck, so the line flips when the basemap does). The route-lines effect takes `mapNight` as a dependency so the line recolors the moment night falls; traffic stretches keep orange/red on top, unpicked options stay grey, and the traffic legend's "flowing" swatch uses the same variable so it never describes the wrong color. The split-badge tooltip no longer promises "the blue line" ("the main line").
